<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统课堂行为观察方法主观性强、缺乏可扩展性，亟需一种自动化、客观的课堂监测方法以评估学生参与度并提升教学质量。

Method: 构建了越南银行学院的BAV-Classroom真实课堂视频数据集，标注9种行为类别，评估并对比多种SOTA计算机视觉模型（YOLOv11表现最优）。

Result: YOLOv11在课堂行为识别任务中取得最佳性能；学生在课堂后半段（尤其是结尾部分）注意力集中度显著下降。

Conclusion: 计算机视觉技术可用于自动化课堂行为监测，为学术质量管理提供有价值的洞察。

Abstract: Classroom behavior monitoring plays a vital role in evaluating student engagement and improving teaching effectiveness. Traditional observation methods remain subjective and lack scalability. This study introduces a real-world dataset of classroom videos collected at the Banking Academy of Vietnam (BAV-Classroom dataset), annotated with nine distinctive behavioral categories. State-of-the-art Computer Vision models were evaluated and compared, with YOLOv11 achieving the best performance. Experimental results indicate that students' concentration often decreases notably during the final part of lectures, highlighting challenges in sustaining engagement. Our findings demonstrate the feasibility of applying computer vision for automated classroom monitoring, providing valuable insights for academic quality management.

</details>


### [26] [Evaluating Intellectual Property Guardrails of Generative Image Models: A Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02582)
*Austin T. Hoag,Apostolos Modas,Yunhao Ba,Julienne M. LaChance,Jinru Xue,Wiebke Hutiri,Jan Simson,Tiffany Georgievski,Alex Towli,Joseph Smith,Yuki Mitsufuji,Alice Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基准测试和自动化评估流程，用于检测生成式图像模型的知识产权(IP)防护机制及其生成可识别IP图像（包括虚构角色、名人肖像和商业标志）的倾向。评估了14个文本到图像模型（3个开源+11个私有模型），发现所有私有模型都有一定程度的IP拒答机制，但频率差异很大。商业标志被拒绝最少且生成成功率最高。截至2026年3月，所有模型仍会生成含有可识别IP的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像模型能够生成与受知识产权保护内容高度相似甚至复制的内容，这引发了严重的IP侵权风险。目前缺乏系统化的基准和自动化评估方法来检测这些模型的IP防护能力及其生成侵权内容的倾向性。

Method: 构建了一个包含虚构角色、名人肖像和商业标志等多类IP的基准测试集，开发了自动化评估管线，对14个主流文本到图像模型（包含3个自托管开源权重模型和11个私有模型）进行了系统评估，分析其IP防护机制的拒绝率和成功生成率。

Result: 所有私有模型都具备一定程度的IP防护拒答机制，但拒绝频率在不同模型间差异显著，且在不同IP类别间变化很大。商业标志被拒绝的频率最低，平均生成成功率最高。截至2026年3月，所有测试模型仍然能够生成含有可识别IP的图像。

Conclusion: 当前生成式图像模型在IP防护方面存在不足，特别是对商业标志的保护最弱。尽管部分模型部署了防护措施，但效果参差不齐，无法完全阻止可识别IP内容的生成，需要更有效的防护机制。

Abstract: Generative image models are capable of producing images that bear a strong resemblance to, or replicate, recognizable intellectual property (IP). In this technical report, we present a benchmark and automated evaluation pipeline to test for evidence of IP guardrails in generative image models along with the propensity for these models to generate images with recognizable IP. The IP categories we tested include fictional characters, celebrity likeness, and commercial logos and do not encompass the full range of IP which may be implicated by image generation models. We evaluated fourteen widely used text-to-image models, including three self-hosted open weights models and eleven private models. While all of the private models were observed to refuse generations at some level due to IP guardrails, the frequency of generation refusals varied substantially among models. The refusal rates also varied considerably across the different IP categories tested. Commercial logos were refused least frequently and were successfully generated at the highest rate, on average. Though the rate varies, all models tested readily generated images containing recognizable IP as of March 2026.

</details>


### [27] [RotateAttention: RoPE-Aware Rotation and Range Rectification for INT4 Quantized Attention in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02584)
*Yaofu Liu,Wanli Lan,Jinxi Li,Binhang Yuan,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对基于DiT并配备3D旋转位置编码（3D RoPE）的视频生成模型，提出RotateAttention——一种混合精度INT4 FlashAttention框架，通过RoPE感知旋转和范围优化P量化两项技术，在保持生成质量的同时实现最高1.68倍端到端和2.2倍内核级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT视频生成模型中注意力机制因序列长度二次复杂度成为计算瓶颈，量化FlashAttention虽有加速潜力，但现有低比特量化方法未能解决两个关键挑战：1）在线旋转矩阵与RoPE难以兼容；2）非负注意力矩阵P的对称量化浪费了INT4一半的动态范围。

Method: 提出RotateAttention框架，包含两项核心技术：1）RoPE感知旋转（RoPE-aware Rotation）：使用可合并到RoPE中的旋转矩阵或低开销矩阵来缓解RoPE导致的Q/K离群值；2）范围优化P量化（Range-optimized P Quantization）：采用固定尺度和零点充分利用INT4数值范围。采用选择性FP16回退策略处理精度敏感的注意力块和去噪步骤。

Result: 保持与全精度基线几乎一致的视频生成质量，实现最高1.68倍端到端加速和2.2倍内核级加速。

Conclusion: RotateAttention有效解决了3D RoPE场景下低比特量化注意力的关键挑战，为DiT视频生成模型提供了高效的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: In \textbf{DiT-based video generation models equipped with 3D Rotary Position Embeddings (3D RoPE)}, the attention mechanism remains a primary computational bottleneck due to its quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length. While quantized \textbf{FlashAttention} offers a promising path toward hardware acceleration, existing low-bit quantization methods overlook two critical challenges in this setting: \textbf{1)} applying online rotation matrices -- a widely used technique for mitigating outliers in Queries ($Q$) and Keys ($K$) -- is difficult to reconcile with \textbf{RoPE}; and \textbf{2)} the non-negative attention matrix $P = \exp(QK - \max(QK))$ makes symmetric quantization waste half of the 4-bit dynamic range. In this work, we observe that the outlier distributions of $Q$ and $K$ are strongly affected by the dimensional partitioning of \textbf{3D RoPE}. Based on this finding, we propose \textbf{RotateAttention}, an efficient \textbf{mixed-precision INT4 FlashAttention} framework tailored for \textbf{DiT-based video generation models with 3D RoPE}, using selective \textbf{FP16 fallback} for accuracy-sensitive attention blocks and denoising steps. RotateAttention introduces two core techniques: \textbf{1) RoPE-aware Rotation}, which employs either mergeable rotation matrices that can be fused into RoPE or negligible-overhead matrices to mitigate RoPE-induced outliers in $Q$ and $K$; and \textbf{2) Range-optimized $P$ Quantization}, which uses fixed scales and zero-points to fully exploit the \textbf{INT4 numerical range} with minimal computational overhead. Experiments show that \textbf{RotateAttention} preserves video generation quality nearly identical to full-precision baselines while achieving up to 1.68$\times$ end-to-end speedup and 2.2$\times$ kernel-level acceleration.

</details>


### [28] [Reliability-Aware CT-MRI Registration: A Quality Engineering Framework with Stability Analysis and Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02585)
*Nisreen Albzour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可靠性感知的多模态CT-MRI配准框架，将配准质量分类为绿/黄/红风险等级，通过数据学习阈值在90对切片上验证，仿射配准优于刚性配准，可靠性过滤提升了配准质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配准流程仅报告聚合质量指标，缺乏逐病例的可靠性信号，无法为临床决策提供置信度信息。

Method: 使用Delta NMI、Delta SSIM、Dice重叠、配准稳定性和逆一致性误差组合为单一评分R，基于训练患者学习风险阈值，对测试患者进行绿/黄/红三色分类。

Result: 仿射配准获得44%绿色分类（刚性仅33%）；可靠性过滤改善了平均对齐效果；腹部配准可靠性优于脑部；Dice重叠是主导可靠性成分。

Conclusion: 该方法为多模态配准提供了可解释的质量控制层，但风险阈值反映统计而非临床验证。

Abstract: Multimodal CT-MRI registration is central to image-guided radiotherapy, surgical navigation, and diagnostic workflows, but most pipelines report only aggregate quality metrics without per-case reliability signals. We propose a reliability-aware framework that converts registration quality into Green/Yellow/Red risk categories using data-learned thresholds. CT images were registered to T1-weighted MRI using rigid and affine transformations on 90 paired slices from 18 patients across brain, abdominal, and neck anatomies. Reliability was assessed using Delta NMI, Delta SSIM, Dice overlap, registration stability, and inverse consistency error, combined into a single score R. Thresholds learned from training patients were applied unchanged to held-out test patients. Affine registration outperformed rigid registration on NMI and SSIM, yielding 44% Green classifications versus 33% for rigid. Reliability-filtered registrations improved the average alignment profile compared with unfiltered methods. Per-anatomy analysis showed substantial variation, with stronger reliability for abdominal registrations than brain registrations. Weight sensitivity analysis identified Dice overlap as the dominant reliability component. The proposed framework provides an interpretable quality-control layer for multimodal registration, while risk thresholds reflect statistical rather than clinical validation.

</details>


### [29] [Homer: Understanding Long-form Videos with Hierarchical Memory and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02588)
*Yixin Ji,Fanghua Ye,Juntao Li,Bo Zhao,Zexuan Qiu,Zhaopeng Tu,Liefeng Bo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Homer框架，通过构建分层记忆结构（原始感知层→实体层→事件层，含显式时序与因果连接）并结合智能体推理器进行多轮记忆检索，显著提升了多模态大模型在小时级长视频在线场景下的多跳叙事推理能力，在多个基准上超越先前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线长视频方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是压缩视觉表示缺乏语义结构；二是高层记忆组织仅基于时间邻近性而非显式因果链接，导致LLM在每次查询时需重新构建多跳叙事推理，无法高效处理一小时级视频。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Online Memory Exploration and Reasoning (Homer)框架。其记忆结构模拟长视频的多尺度特性，包含原始感知层、重复实体层、以及通过显式时序与因果关系连接的事件层；配备智能体推理器，模拟人类认知方式，通过定位相关场景、查找细节、多轮记忆检索来逐步组合答案，并配有验证与校正机制。

Result: 在M3-Bench-robot、M3-Bench-web和Video-MME-Long上分别超越先前最佳智能体方法+5.5、+10.8和+4.4个点，且一致提升了三种不同LLM骨干的性能，表明具备模型无关的结构化检索能力。

Conclusion: Homer通过将结构化因果记忆与智能体多轮推理相结合，有效解决了长视频在线场景下的多跳推理难题，且该方法具有模型无关的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models excel on short clips but struggle on hour-long videos in an online setting, where frames are processed incrementally under limited memory. Existing online methods either retain compact visual representations that lack semantic structure, or build higher-level memory stores organized around temporal proximity rather than explicit causal links, leaving multi-hop narrative reasoning to be reconstructed by the LLM at every query. We bridge this gap with \textsc{Homer}, a Hierarchical Online Memory Exploration and Reasoning framework. \textsc{Homer}'s memory mirrors the multi-scale structure of long videos, ranging from raw perception, to recurring entities, to events connected by explicit temporal and causal relations. Its agentic reasoner then explores this memory the way humans do, locating the relevant scene, looking up details, and composing the answer through multi-round memory retrieval, with a harness that verifies and corrects each step. \textsc{Homer} outperforms the previous best agent method by $+5.5$, $+10.8$, and $+4.4$ points on M3-Bench-robot, M3-Bench-web, and Video-MME-Long, and consistently lifts three various LLM backbones, indicating a model-agnostic structural capability for grounded retrieval over long videos.

</details>


### [30] [CPR: Chained Perceptual Refinement for Coarse-to-Fine Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02591)
*Si-Yuan Lu,Hanruo Zhu,Ziquan Zhu,Gaojie Jin,Zeyu Fu,Lu Yin,Ke Li,Lu Liu,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Chained Perceptual Refinement (CPR)框架，将医学图像分析建模为从粗到细的序列化决策过程，通过动态预测细化区域的位置和范围，在固定输入尺寸下逐步融合高分辨率局部证据与全局上下文，实现精度与效率的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率医学图像包含对诊断至关重要的细粒度、空间稀疏线索，但保持全分辨率会带来巨大的计算和内存开销；现有深度模型通常均匀处理图像，导致下采样时计算冗余或丢失诊断细节。

Method: 提出CPR框架，采用粗到细（coarse-to-fine）策略：从低分辨率全局视图出发，动态预测需要细化的区域位置和空间范围，从原始图像中提取高分辨率证据，并增量式地与全局上下文融合；保持骨干网络输入尺寸固定，通过收缩感知场来维持恒定的峰值GPU内存。

Result: 在五个医学影像数据集和多种骨干架构上，CPR一致优于固定分辨率及多尺度的最先进基线方法，最高超出第二名2.27个百分点；在匹配精度下，GFLOPs最多降低19.6倍。

Conclusion: CPR框架在高分辨率医学图像分析中实现了优越的精度-效率权衡，为处理细粒度诊断任务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: High resolution medical images contain fine grained, spatially sparse cues that are critical for diagnosis, yet preserving full resolution incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Most deep models process images uniformly, leading to redundant computation or loss of diagnostic detail under downsampling. We propose Chained Perceptual Refinement, CPR, a coarse to fine framework that formulates medical image analysis as a sequential global to local decision process. Starting from a low resolution global view, CPR dynamically predicts the location and spatial extent of refinement regions, extracts high resolution evidence from the original image, and incrementally integrates it with global context. By keeping the backbone input size fixed while contracting the perceptual field, CPR preserves diagnostic fidelity with constant peak GPU memory. Extensive experiments on five medical imaging datasets and multiple backbone architectures demonstrate that CPR consistently outperforms both fixed resolution and multi scale state of the art baselines, achieving improvements of up to 2.27 percentage points over the second best method. It also achieves up to a 19.6 fold reduction in GFLOPs at matched accuracy, establishing a superior accuracy and efficiency trade off for high resolution medical image analysis. The code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [31] [H-OPD: Confidence Aware Heterogeneous Multi-Teacher Multimodal On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02592)
*Qixiang Yin,Huanjin Yao,Yuchen Cai,Jianghao Chen,Ziyi Wang,Min Yang,Fei Su,Zhicheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H-OPD是一种置信度感知的异构多教师在线策略蒸馏框架，通过令牌级教师仲裁替代传统任务/样本级路由，结合视觉-语言描述传递和动态置信度机制，在多模态推理任务上取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法在多模态推理中采用静态教师路由，按模态或任务类型为每个样本分配单一教师，忽略了视觉定位和抽象推理可能主导不同解码步骤，导致单一教师不足以覆盖完整轨迹的监督需求。

Method: 1) 提出置信度感知的异构多教师OPD框架H-OPD；2) 采用视觉到语言描述传递，使纯文本教师能获取关键视觉语义；3) 设计置信度感知仲裁机制，在共享学生轨迹的每个令牌层面动态组合视觉-语言教师和纯文本教师。

Result: 在11个广泛使用的推理基准上进行全面评估，展示了H-OPD的优越性能。

Conclusion: H-OPD通过令牌级异构多教师动态路由有效解决了多模态推理中单一教师监督不足的问题，验证了异构教师在同一推理过程中的互补性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm by providing supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, existing OPD methods for multimodal reasoning usually rely on a static teacher routing, assigning each sample to a single teacher based on modality or task type. This ignores that visual grounding and abstract reasoning may dominate different decoding steps, making a single teacher insufficient for the full trajectory. To this end, H-OPD is proposed as a confidence-aware heterogeneous multi-teacher OPD framework for multimodal reasoning. By verifying the complementarity of heterogeneous teachers in the same reasoning process, H-OPD replaces task or sample level teacher routing with token-level teacher arbitration along the shared student trajectory. H-OPD employs vision-to-language description transfer to enable text-only teachers to access key visual semantics, and uses a confidence-aware arbitration mechanism to dynamically combine vision-language teacher and text-only teachers at each token. Extensive evaluations over 11 widely-used reasoning benchmarks showcase the superior performance of our method.

</details>


### [32] [Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance for Multimodal LLMs Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02593)
*Jaehyun Jang,Eunseop Yoon,Hee Suk Yoon,SooHwan Eom,Mark A. Hasegawa-Johnson,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAG方法，通过关注响应-视觉注意力信号并采用基于注意力熵的自适应加权KL散度，显著提升了多模态大语言模型的知识蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏方法仅依赖输出token分布不足以压缩多模态大模型，而先前探索的注意力蒸馏方法未能精确探究哪种注意力信号最有效，且忽略了不同token间注意力分布的方差差异。

Method: 提出Token级响应-视觉注意力引导(TRAG)：1) 将蒸馏焦点从提示-视觉注意力转向响应-视觉注意力；2) 根据注意力熵自适应加权KL散度，为每个token制定个性化的蒸馏目标。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验结果表明，TRAG显著优于先前的知识蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 响应-视觉注意力相似度与下游性能强相关，而提示条件注意力则无关；传统均匀蒸馏目标因忽略token间方差而次优，TRAG通过token级自适应蒸馏有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: While knowledge distillation (KD) is widely adopted for training lightweight models by leveraging supervision from larger teacher models, relying solely on output token distributions has proven insufficient for compressing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Since output tokens are a byproduct of the model attending to visual inputs, prior works have explored explicitly distilling attention to provide a direct supervisory signal. While promising, the precise utility of which attention signals to distill remains under-explored. In this work, we challenge the conventional reliance on prompt-to-vision attention by revealing that downstream performance correlates strongly with response-to-vision attention similarity to the teacher, but negligibly with that of prompt-conditioned attention. Furthermore, we observe that attention distributions exhibit significant variance across individual tokens, indicating that a uniform distillation objective is suboptimal. To this end, we introduce Token-level Response-visual Attention Guidance (TRAG), a distillation objective that 1) shifts the focus to response-to-vision signals and 2) employs token-specific objectives by adaptively weighting the Kullback-Leibler divergence based on attention entropy, effectively guiding the student to mirror the teacher's precise visual focus. Extensive experimental results on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that TRAG significantly outperforms prior distillation baselines.

</details>


### [33] [An automated method of identifying incorrectly labelled images based on the sequences of loss functions of deep learning networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02594)
*Zhipeng Zhang,Wenhui Shou,Wengting Ma,Dongjia Xing,Qingqing Xu,Li-Qun Xu,Qingxia Fan,Ling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过分析深度学习分类网络在多个训练周期中的损失函数序列来自动识别医学图像中错误标注的方法。在糖尿病视网膜病变筛查的眼底图像数据集上，该方法能识别出75.31%的故意错误标注，且假阳性率仅4.85%。修正后模型准确率从95.93%（含6%噪声）提升至96.50%（含1.5%噪声），接近理想状态的96.57%（无噪声）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注中高达10%的标签可能存在错误，这会降低深度学习模型的性能，因此需要一种自动化的方法来识别并纠正错误标注，以提高数据集质量和模型表现。

Method: 通过分析深度学习分类网络在多个训练周期中的损失函数序列，识别异常损失模式的样本（即可能被错误标注的图像），然后由专家对这些被识别出的样本进行审查和重新标注。

Result: 在眼底图像数据集上，方法识别出75.31%的故意翻转标签，假阳性率为4.85%。纠正识别出的样本后，模型准确率从95.93%（6%噪声）提升至96.50%（1.5%噪声），接近无噪声时的96.57%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效通过自动化标签质量控制来提升模型性能，显著降低了标注噪声对深度学习模型的影响。

Abstract: Deep learning is widely applied in medical image analysis, but up to 10% of manually labelled images may be incorrect, degrading model performance. This paper proposes an automated method to identify incorrectly labelled medical images by analyzing sequences of loss functions from deep learning classification networks over multiple training epochs. Identified images can be reviewed and relabelled by experts, improving dataset quality and model performance. Two experiments validate the method on a fundus image dataset for referable diabetic retinopathy screening. In the first, 6% (648) of 10,788 gold-standard labels were intentionally flipped. The method identified 75.31% (488) of the flipped samples, with only 4.85% (492) false positives among correctly labelled samples. In the second, reviewing and correcting the 980 identified samples (9.1% of the dataset) and retraining the model improved best accuracy on an independent test set from 95.93% (with 6% label noise) to 96.50% (with 1.5% noise), approaching the ideal 96.57% (with 0% noise). The results demonstrate the method's effectiveness in improving model performance through automated label quality control.

</details>


### [34] [CIPHER: Causal Intervention Pathways for Healthcare Equity and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02596)
*Xinyu Jia,Weidong Guo,Wangyuan Zhao,Yi Guo,Zeju Li,Yuanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化医学图像生成过程中的四种敏感属性影响路径，并提出CIPHER框架，利用扩散模型进行多路径因果干预，在胸部X光和皮肤镜基准上平均减少35.8%的最差组差异并提升诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式数据增强方法仅处理敏感属性与图像特征之间的一两种依赖通道，忽视了医学图像形成过程中敏感属性通过四种不同因果路径影响图像内容的复杂结构。

Method: 提出CIPHER框架，基于结构因果模型形式化四种因果干预路径，利用配备无分类器引导和空文本反转的扩散模型骨干，实现患者特定解剖结构的忠实重建和反事实图像的精确可编辑合成。

Result: 在胸部X光和皮肤镜基准测试中，相比疾病条件合成基线，最差组差异平均减少35.8%，同时总体诊断准确率提升。

Conclusion: 多路径因果干预策略在公平性和鲁棒性上优于传统单路径或双路径方法，为医学影像中的公平性研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical diagnosis frequently exhibit substantial performance disparities across sensitive subgroups (e.g., race, sex), even when average accuracy is high. While generative data augmentation offers a route to mitigate this, existing strategies are suboptimal; they typically address only one or two dependency channels between sensitive attributes and image features. We formalize the medical image formation process via a structural causal model, revealing that sensitive attributes actually influence image content through four distinct pathways-a structural complexity neglected by prior works. Based on this insight, we introduce CIPHER (Causal Intervention Pathways for Healthcare Equity and Robustness), a framework designed to systematically intervene on all four causal paths. To achieve this, CIPHER utilizes a diffusion backbone equipped with classifier-free guidance and null-text inversion. This technical design enables the faithful reconstruction of patient-specific anatomy while allowing for the precise, editable synthesis of counterfactuals required to break sensitive dependency chains. We tested CIPHER using chest X-ray and dermoscopy benchmarks across both standard and shifted data distributions. By employing a multi-pathway intervention strategy, our model reduced worst-group disparities by an average of 35.8% compared to disease-conditioned synthesis baselines, while also improving total diagnostic accuracy

</details>


### [35] [Evaluating Agentic Harness Systems for Autonomous Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02598)
*Jie Lin,Zongyi Chen,Qiaoling Zheng,Liuyi Wang,Hengyi Jiang,Jiabao Chen,Xiang Liu,Yinghong Yang,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACP-Bench是一个评估自主计算病理学（ACP）工作流能力的框架，涵盖41项病理学任务、9种模型和3个工具组，共369条完整轨迹。评估发现工作流启动、任务解释和诊断报告较为成熟，但工具执行、结果绑定和反思性工作流修订仍不成熟，端到端完成罕见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用智能体系统适配到病理学特定任务、工具、证据标准和临床声明边界，需要系统性评估框架来验证ACP工作流能力。

Method: 构建ACP-Bench框架，包含41项病理学工作流任务（24项生物标志物、7项形态学、10项预后），覆盖6个体系统组和9个终点家族。评估9种模型和3个工具组（Claude Code、Codex、Open Code），共369条轨迹。通过专家裁定的过程审计、诊断评估和病理学家验证的安全审查，从工作流执行、诊断性能和临床边界对齐三个维度评估。

Result: 工作流启动、任务解释和诊断报告成熟度较高；工具绑定执行、结果绑定和反思性工作流修订成熟度较低；正式的端到端完成罕见。

Conclusion: ACP-Bench提供了可复用的标准，用于审计智能体系统在声称可靠临床自主性之前是否具备操作病理学工作流的能力。

Abstract: Autonomous computational pathology (ACP) converts high-level pathology analysis goals into executable, traceable and clinically bounded workflows. Realizing this capability requires adapting general agentic harness systems to pathology-specific tasks, tools, evidence standards and clinical claim boundaries. We contribute ACP-Bench, a framework that adapts existing harness systems from computational pathology support toward ACP workflow capability. ACP-Bench evaluates 41 pathology workflow tasks, including 24 biomarker, 7 morphology and 10 prognosis tasks spanning 6 body-system groups and 9 endpoint families. The benchmark evaluates 9 models and 3 harness groups (Claude Code, Codex and Open Code), yielding 369 complete trajectories. ACP-Bench evaluates each trajectory across workflow execution, diagnostic performance and clinical-boundary alignment, combining expert-adjudicated process audits, diagnostic assessment and pathologist-validated safety review. Across evaluated systems, workflow initiation, task interpretation and diagnostic reporting were more mature than tool-bound execution, result binding and reflective workflow revision, and formal end-to-end completion remained rare. ACP-Bench provides a reusable standard for auditing whether agentic systems can operationalize pathology workflows before claims of reliable clinical autonomy.

</details>


### [36] [CV-DCLR: Causal-Visual Dynamic Label Refinement for Robust Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02601)
*Can Wang,Jiangnan Li,Mingyu Li,Yining Song,Kangrui Ren,Min Gan,Jinfu Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Causal-Visual Dynamic Label Refinement (CV-DCLR)框架，通过双流互纠机制解决零样本学习中的语义纠缠问题，在CUB、SUN、AWA2基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本学习中视觉表示与语义概念存在语义纠缠，传统全局对齐方法会过拟合虚假相似性，无法捕捉类别独特身份特征。

Method: 提出CV-DCLR框架，包含双流互纠机制：视觉似然流（建模观测模式）和因果重要性流（通过反事实干预验证候选原型的结构必要性），并采用自适应门控机制动态调制特征响应，放大因果特征、抑制虚假干扰。

Result: 在CUB、SUN、AWA2基准上采用严格的语义纠缠注入协议进行实验，CV-DCLR在高模糊场景下显著优于SOTA方法，现有模型在纠缠下性能严重退化时，该框架保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: CV-DCLR通过因果干预与动态标签精炼有效解耦真实类别身份与语义混淆因素，为高歧义场景下的零样本学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Learning (ZSL) facilitates knowledge transfer via shared semantic spaces. However, a critical bottleneck in this paradigm is Semantic Entanglement, where visual representations are inevitably conflated with visually similar semantic concepts, such as distinguishing the intrinsic traits of a Wolf from the shared features of a Husky. Existing global alignment methods often indiscriminately maximize correlations between visual and semantic modalities, leading models to overfit spurious similarities rather than capturing distinctive class identities. To address this fundamental limitation, we propose the Causal-Visual Dynamic Label Refinement (CV-DCLR) framework. Unlike traditional approaches that rely on superficial visual statistics, CV-DCLR recalibrates visual-semantic associations via a Dual-Stream Mutual Correction Mechanism. This includes a Visual Likelihood Stream to model observational patterns and a Causal Importance Stream that verifies the structural necessity of candidate prototypes through Counterfactual Intervention. Acting as a logical filter, our adaptive gating mechanism dynamically modulates feature responses to amplify genuine causal traits while suppressing visually plausible but structurally irrelevant distractors. Extensive experiments on the CUB, SUN, and AWA2 benchmarks under a rigorous Semantic Entanglement Injection protocol demonstrate that CV-DCLR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in high-ambiguity scenarios. Specifically, while existing models suffer catastrophic degradation under entanglement, our framework maintains robust performance, effectively disentangling true class identities from semantic confounders.

</details>


### [37] [DynaWM: A Base-VLA-Guided World Foundation Model for Moving-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02604)
*Chongkei Chang,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaWM是一个基于基础VLA引导的世界基础模型，通过Mamba-3动作编码器、V-JEPA 2.1视觉编码器和本体感觉状态编码器，结合流匹配DiT，实现对动态移动物体的操作。在构建的DynaGrasp-32基准和DynaGrasp-1600数据集上，相比多个基线模型取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在处理动态移动物体操作时面临挑战，端到端世界模型整合到预训练VLA架构中会因不当微调导致性能下降，需要一种能适应多种微调策略的基础世界模型。

Method: DynaWM包含三个编码器组件：Mamba-3动作编码器编码基础VLA的动作块、V-JEPA 2.1视觉编码器提取多视角观测历史特征、本体感觉状态编码器编码机械臂状态。这些特征联合条件化一个流匹配DiT模型，用于生成运动感知的动作轨迹。

Result: 在精细微调的基础VLA检查点上，DynaWM相比SmolVLA、X-VLA、π0和π0.5分别提升7.19、45.31、1.88和10.94个百分点；在粗略微调检查点上分别提升35.13、44.06、35.69和26.13个百分点。消融实验表明视觉编码提升27.50%成功率，去除动作条件化则降低45.44%成功率。

Conclusion: DynaWM通过基础VLA引导的世界模型架构，有效适应不同微调策略，显著提升了动态移动物体操作的成功率，验证了各组件设计的有效性。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) models have received widespread attention, many challenges remain in manipulating dynamic moving objects. In most existing approaches, end-to-end forward or inverse dynamics models, i.e., world models, are incorporated into high-performance base VLA architectures, which may degrade the performance of well-pretrained base VLA models due to inappropriate fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose DynaWM, a base-VLA-guided world foundation model that adapts to a wide variety of fine-tuned and coarse-tuned base-VLA checkpoints for moving-object manipulation. DynaWM uses a Mamba-3-based action encoder to encode the base action chunk produced by the base VLA into an action-conditioning representation, a V-JEPA 2.1 vision encoder to extract features from multi-view observation history, and a proprioceptive state encoder to encode robotic-arm proprioceptive states. These feature representations jointly condition a flow-matching DiT to regenerate motion-aware action trajectories for moving-object manipulation. For systematic evaluation, we construct the DynaGrasp-32 benchmark, covering six categories of moving-object manipulation tasks, including velocity variation, trajectory variation, and multi-object manipulation, as well as the DynaGrasp-1600 dataset, which consists of 32 scenarios, 1,600 demonstration trajectories, and approximately 1.53M images. For fine-tuned base-VLA checkpoints, DynaWM achieves percentage improvements of 7.19, 45.31, 1.88, and 10.94 over SmolVLA, X-VLA, π0, and π0.5, respectively. For coarse-tuned base-VLA checkpoints, performance increases by 35.13, 44.06, 35.69, and 26.13 percentage, respectively. Ablation experiments show that visual encoding enhances success by 27.50%, while reducing success by 45.44% if action conditioning is removed.

</details>


### [38] [Latent Visual Cache for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02607)
*Yongheng Zhang,Zhipeng Xu,Hao Wu,Yinghui Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Video Cache (Latent-VC)方法，通过在解码器中插入循环潜在视觉缓存来保留紧凑的视觉记忆，解决视频推理中的视觉锚定衰减问题。该方法在Qwen3.5-9B上超越多个强基线，在六项视频基准测试中取得一致优势，尤其在接地密集型与长视频任务上表现突出，同时生成更短响应却获得更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs采用"一次读取、多次生成"范式，导致视觉接地在生成过程中逐渐弱化（即视觉锚定衰减），缺乏在推理过程中持续保留视觉记忆的有效机制。

Method: 提出Latent-VC，在解码器中插入循环潜在视觉缓存以保留紧凑视觉记忆；采用监督对比缓存对齐和基于潜在接地奖励的视觉引导GRPO进行训练；通过原生解码器隐藏状态保持严格的训练-推理对齐。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B上，Latent-VC在六项视频基准测试中持续优于强CoT和SFT+GRPO基线；在接地密集型和长视频任务上增益尤为明显；同时以更短响应获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: 潜在视觉缓存通过保留视觉证据而非依赖更长的文本链来提升视频推理能力，为解决视觉锚定衰减提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Video reasoning requires Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) to remain grounded in dense evidence, yet existing systems largely adopt "read-once, generate-many" paradigm, in which visual grounding weakens during generation. This phenomenon has been widely observed and is known as Visual Anchoring Decay. To fill this gap, we introduce Latent Video Cache (Latent-VC), a recurrent latent visual cache inserted into the decoder to preserve compact visual memories throughout reasoning. The cache is trained with supervised contrastive cache alignment and vision-grounded GRPO with a latent grounding reward, while maintaining strict train-inference alignment through native decoder hidden states. Built on Qwen3.5-9B, Latent-VC consistently outperforms strong CoT and SFT+GRPO baselines across six video benchmarks, with especially clear gains on grounding-intensive and long-video tasks. In addition, it also achieves higher accuracy with substantially shorter responses, suggesting that latent visual caching improves video reasoning by preserving visual evidence rather than relying on longer textual chains.

</details>


### [39] [Privacy-Preserving Industrial Ergonomics: mmWave-Based Automated REBA Scoring and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02611)
*Xuhan Zhang,Zhuangzhuang Dai,Luis J. Mans,Victor Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于毫米波雷达的端到端多任务学习框架，通过重建3D人体骨架并回归REBA风险评分，在保护隐私的同时实现了77.78%的分类准确率和5.70ms的实时推理延迟，在高风险场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统REBA人工监测劳动强度大，基于视觉的自动化方法存在隐私问题，亟需一种非侵入式、隐私保护的自动化人体工学评估方法。

Method: 提出端到端多任务学习框架：1）时空骨干网络从毫米波雷达点云重建3D人体骨架；2）回归头基于骨架生成REBA风险评分；3）多目标损失函数结合生物力学约束和时间平滑性以克服点云稀疏性；4）过采样策略解决数据集中高风险姿势不平衡问题。

Result: 在MMFi数据集上达到77.78%的分类准确率，5.70ms推理延迟，高风险REBA MAE为0.93，显著优于直接回归和两阶段流水线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为工业环境中非侵入式、隐私保护的人体工学评估提供了一种鲁棒且实时的解决方案。

Abstract: Work-related Musculoskeletal Disorders (WMSDs) require continuous ergonomic assessments. While Rapid Entire Body Assessment (REBA) is a gold-standard observation tool, manual monitoring is labor-intensive, and vision-based automation leads to privacy concerns. This paper proposes a novel end-to-end multi-task learning framework for privacy-preserving ergonomic assessment using millimetre-wave (mmWave) radar. A spatio-temporal backbone reconstructs 3D human skeletons, which serves as the biomechanical foundation for a subsequent regression head to generate REBA risk scores. To overcome the sparsity of radar point clouds, we utilise a multi-objective loss function incorporating biomechanical limits and temporal smoothness constraints. Furthermore, we implement an oversampling strategy to address the imbalance of high-risk postures in existing datasets. Experimental results on MMFi dataset demonstrate that our framework achieves a Categorical Accuracy of 77.78% and real-time performance with an inference latency of 5.70 ms. Our method reaches a High-risk REBA MAE of 0.93, which significantly outperforms both direct regression and two-stage pipelines in high-risk scenarios, providing a robust solution for non-invasive industrial ergonomic assessment.

</details>


### [40] [Fusion: A Framework for Unified Sequential Token AdaptatIon in VisiOn TraNsformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02612)
*Aravind Pradeep,Samira Nazari,Mahdi Taheri,Christian Herglotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fusion 是一种统一的自适应推理框架，通过协调 token 合并、提前退出和 token 剪枝三种机制，以阶段式设计（先合并、再评估置信度、最后剪枝）实现 ViT 的高效推理，在 ImageNet-1k 上匹配或超越现有方法，并将校准误差降低 4 倍、推理能耗降低 48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers 对所有图像区域施加近乎相同的计算量，而许多区域存在冗余或信息量低。现有自适应推理方法虽能降低计算成本，但组合 token 压缩与提前退出等机制时会导致中间表示不稳定和精度下降。

Method: 提出 Fusion 框架，采用阶段式设计：首先合并 token，然后评估置信度进行提前退出，最后对需要继续推理的样本执行 token 剪枝。包含轻量级路由模块，可根据输入自适应调整压缩强度，并支持推理时动态调节精度-延迟权衡而无需重训练。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1k 上使用 DeiT-S，Fusion 在相当计算预算下匹配或超越 SOTA 自适应 ViT 方法，校准误差降低最多 4 倍，推理能耗降低 48%。在 ImageNet-100、CIFAR-100 和 ImageNette 上使用多种 ViT 骨干网络均表现出一致的可迁移性。

Conclusion: Fusion 通过有序协调多种自适应机制而非竞争性使用，有效解决了组合时的稳定性问题，实现了精度、校准误差和推理效率的综合提升，且无需针对特定数据集调参即可迁移。

Abstract: Vision Transformers achieve strong image classification accuracy but process all image regions with nearly the same computation, even when many regions are redundant or uninformative. Recent adaptive inference methods reduce this cost by selectively compressing tokens or terminating inference early, but combining these mechanisms often causes unstable intermediate representations and accuracy degradation. We introduce Fusion, a unified adaptive inference framework that coordinates token merging, early exiting, and token pruning through a simple staged design: tokens are merged first, confidence is evaluated next, and pruning is applied only to samples that continue inference. This ordering allows the three mechanisms to operate cooperatively rather than competitively. Fusion further includes lightweight routing modules that adapt compression strength to each input and support inference-time adjustment of the accuracy--latency trade-off without retraining. On ImageNet-1k with DeiT-S, Fusion matches or surpasses state-of-the-art adaptive ViT methods at comparable compute budgets while reducing calibration error by up to $4\times$ and inference energy by $48\%$. Experiments across ImageNet-100, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette with multiple ViT backbones demonstrate consistent transferability without dataset-specific tuning.

</details>


### [41] [SE-UNet: Singular Equivariant Imaging for Real-World Constrained Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02628)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SE-UNet通过结合D4群等变性和奇异值门控作为强归纳偏置，实现了无需大规模预训练的零样本图像修复，在CIFAR-10上以80%像素缺失条件下超越Deep Image Prior基线4 dB以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在逆问题中依赖大数据集且难以施加严格物理约束，现有方法缺乏数据高效的零样本解决方案。

Method: 将生成建模为受几何等变性（D4群）和奇异值门控约束的优化问题，构建SE-UNet框架标准化解空间。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上实现80%像素缺失的零样本修复，PSNR超越DIP基线超过4 dB，呈现"奇异快照"收敛特性。

Conclusion: SE-UNet提供了一条数据高效的约束生成路径，在实践中桥接了理论先验与实际部署。

Abstract: While diffusion models have revolutionized image synthesis, their application to real-world inverse problems is often hampered by the need for massive datasets and the difficulty of imposing strict physical constraints. In this work, we introduce \textbf{SE-UNet} (Singular Equivariant UNet), a framework designed to solve ill-posed imaging tasks without extensive pre-training. By treating generation as an optimization problem constrained by geometric equivariance ($D_4$ group) and singular value gating, SE-UNet effectively standardizes the solution space. We demonstrate that these strong inductive biases allow for state-of-the-art zero-shot inpainting results (80\% missing pixels) on CIFAR-10. Our method surpasses Deep Image Prior (DIP) baselines by over 4 dB in PSNR and exhibits a characteristic "singular snap" convergence -- rapidly locking into the signal manifold. SE-UNet thus offers a data-efficient pathway for constrained generation, aligning with the ReALM-GEN goal of bridging theoretical priors with practical deployment.

</details>


### [42] [CLABTOOLKIT: An Open-Source Toolkit for Routine Processing, Manipulation, and Visualization of Neuroimaging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02638)
*Yasser Alemán-Gómez,Nino Hervé,Patric Hagmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLABTOOLKIT 是一个开源Python工具包，将神经影像学研究中涉及的多模态数据结构（体积、表面、纤维束）的操作整合到统一框架中，提供五个核心数据结构、19个模块、234个方法和207个独立函数，支持BIDS、FreeSurfer、扩散MRI、形态测量、图论网络分析及GPU加速可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像学研究需要操作多种异构数据结构（如原始MRI体积、体积分割、皮层表面网格、纤维束图、连接矩阵），而现有工具接口和文件格式不兼容，迫使研究者重复实现常规但技术要求高的操作。

Method: 将体积、表面和纤维束数据表示为可互操作的Python对象；设计五个核心数据结构（Parcellation、Surface、AnnotParcellation、Tractogram、Connectome）封装常见神经解剖实体；提供19个模块组织为六层架构，暴露13个面向对象类及234个方法、207个独立函数；采用JSON配置系统支持无需修改代码的工作流定制。

Result: 实现了跨标准神经影像格式（NIfTI、GIFTI、FreeSurfer标注、TCK/TRK）的一致数据加载、处理和导出方法，支持连接矩阵生成、标量图投影到纤维束、多面板可视化等功能。兼容Python 3.9-3.12，采用Apache 2.0许可，代码和文档已开源。

Conclusion: CLABTOOLKIT区别于现有神经影像库（通常分别处理各类任务），在单一框架内整合了色彩和查找表管理、分割操作、多表面可视化及纤维束追踪工具，降低了研究者的重复劳动和技术门槛。

Abstract: Neuroimaging research requires manipulating heterogeneous data structures, including raw MRI volumes, volumetric parcellations, cortical surface meshes, tractograms, and connectivity matrices, across tools with incompatible interfaces and file formats, forcing researchers to repeatedly re-implement routine but technically demanding operations. We present CLABTOOLKIT, an open-source Python package that consolidates these operations into a single, coherent framework by representing volumetric, surface, and streamline data as interoperable Python objects. Five core data structures (Parcellation, Surface, AnnotParcellation, Tractogram, and Connectome) encapsulate common neuroanatomical entities and provide consistent methods for loading, processing, and exporting data across standard neuroimaging formats (e.g., NIfTI, GIFTI, FreeSurfer annotations, TCK/TRK), including connectome generation from a parcellation and scalar-map projection onto tractogram streamlines. Complementary modules support BIDS dataset management, FreeSurfer integration, diffusion MRI processing, morphometric analysis, graph-theoretical network analysis, and GPU-accelerated multi-panel visualization via PyVista. The toolkit comprises 19 modules organised into six layers, exposing 13 object-oriented classes with 234 methods and 207 standalone functions, and a JSON-based configuration system enables workflow customization without code changes. Unlike existing neuroimaging libraries, which typically address these tasks separately, CLABTOOLKIT combines color and lookup-table management, parcellation manipulation, multi-surface visualization, and tractography utilities within a single framework. CLABTOOLKIT is compatible with Python 3.9-3.12 and released under the Apache 2.0 license. Source code, documentation, and example workflows are available at https://github.com/connectomicslab/clabtoolkit.

</details>


### [43] [BiSLW: Bi-Spectral Latent Watermarking for Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02643)
*Aryan Pandit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiSLW是一种可训练的雙频谱潜空间水印框架，通过将身份信号联合嵌入扩散潜变量的互补频谱波段（低频全局语义+高频精细纹理），实现高感知保真度与鲁棒性，在再生攻击下PSNR提升超3dB且比特精度近乎完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法要么将潜张量视为静态空间特征图，要么依赖像素域修改，且大多未显式利用潜空间的内部频率结构进行双频带冗余嵌入，导致易受扩散随机性和再生攻击影响。

Method: 利用扩散潜变量的固有频率结构，设计双频带水印框架：低频编码全局语义，高频捕获精细纹理。通过可学习编码器将水印分别注入两个互补频带，重组后再解码；双频谱解码器从各频带恢复水印，并通过跨频带一致性约束强制语义与纹理嵌入对齐。

Result: 相比先前潜扩散水印方法，PSNR提升超过3dB；在强再生攻击和常见失真下保持近乎完美的比特精度；计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: BiSLW在感知保真度与鲁棒性之间实现了强平衡，验证了显式利用潜空间频率结构进行双频带冗余嵌入的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have transformed visual content synthesis, yet they remain vulnerable to unauthorized usage and lack reliable attribution methods. Existing watermarking techniques often treat latent tensors as static spatial feature maps or depend on pixel-domain modification, and most do not explicitly leverage the internal frequency structure of the latent space for dual-band redundant embedding, leaving them susceptible to the stochastic nature of diffusion and regeneration attacks. We introduce BiSLW, a trainable bi-spectral latent watermarking framework that jointly embeds aligned identity signals across complementary spectral bands of the decoded diffusion latent using learned encoders and decoders, going beyond fixed-pattern frequency approaches. We leverage the inherent frequency structure of diffusion latents to design a dual-band watermarking framework. Low-frequency components encode global semantics, while high-frequency components capture fine texture. We exploit this structure to embed watermarks across complementary spectral bands. The watermark is independently injected into both bands via learned encoders and recombined before decoding, ensuring it becomes intrinsic to the generative trajectory. Dual spectral decoders recover the watermark from each band, while a cross-band consistency constraint enforces alignment between semantic and textural embeddings. Experiments show that BiSLW achieves a strong balance between perceptual fidelity and robustness, improving PSNR by over 3 dB compared to prior latent diffusion watermarking methods while preserving near-perfect bit accuracy under aggressive regeneration and common distortions, all with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [44] [EmoteGPT: 3D Human Facial Expressions from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02674)
*Haoran Wang,Mohit Mendiratta,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Txt2Emote基准数据集和EmoteGPT框架，通过将文本到3D面部表情合成建模为3DMM解耦参数空间的回归问题，利用多模态大语言模型实现细粒度的3D表情控制，在情感识别和表现力指标上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D方法联合生成身份、表情和纹理，难以实现细粒度的表情控制，且缺乏配对的文本-表情参数数据。

Method: 1) 将表情合成建模为3DMM解耦参数空间的回归问题；2) 提出Txt2Emote基准数据集，包含GPT-4o和面部追踪器生成的精细文本标注（显式描述和隐式描述）；3) 提出EmoteGPT框架，基于多模态大语言模型，引入专用<Expr>标记语义化表情表征并解码为3DMM参数；4) 利用大规模图像到3DMM数据增强训练。

Result: 在情感识别指标和感知表现力上超越现有文本到3D人脸合成方法，支持逼真/风格化3D化身和3D一致的2D人脸合成。

Conclusion: 将文本驱动的表情合成与3DMM解耦参数空间结合，并借助MLLM和大规模数据增强，实现了更可控、更具表现力的文本到3D表情生成。

Abstract: Precise control of 3D facial expressions from text is crucial for virtual avatars, animation, and human-computer interaction, yet existing text-to-3D methods jointly generate identity, expression, and texture, making fine-grained expression control difficult. We instead formulate text-driven expression synthesis as a regression problem in the disentangled parameter space of a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM). This setting, however, requires paired data linking detailed language to precise expression parameters, which are missing from existing resources. To fill this gap, we introduce Txt2Emote, a benchmark of diverse 3D facial expressions with fine-grained textual annotations obtained from GPT-4o and a high-fidelity face tracker, providing both explicit descriptions detailing facial features and implicit descriptions referencing the situational context behind the expression. Leveraging this dataset, we present EmoteGPT, a text-to-3D expression framework based on a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) with a dedicated <Expr> token to semantically ground expression representations, which are then decoded into 3DMM parameters. We further improve EmoteGPT by augmenting training with large-scale image-to-3DMM data, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art text-to-3D face synthesis methods on emotion recognition metrics and in perceived expressiveness. Integrated into avatar pipelines, our method enables photorealistic and stylized 3D avatars, as well as expressive 3D-consistent 2D face synthesis from textual input.

</details>


### [45] [K9-Bench: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Canine-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02680)
*Khush Attarde,Yusuf Ali,Megha Thukral,Divye Bhutani,Thomas Ploetz,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: K9-Bench是一个专注于真实世界家犬视频的多模态基准测试，包含约5000个问答对和907个视频，覆盖5个任务类别，用于评估多模态大语言模型在犬类动作与交互理解上的零样本能力。实验发现前沿模型在此类任务上表现有限，闭源模型优于开源模型，但在长时序推理和细微姿态理解上仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物（尤其是宠物）与人类生活紧密相关，但现有MLLM基准测试忽视了动物中心场景。为构建能与人类协作的AI系统，需要专门针对宠物相关任务（如识别 distress 信号、开发响应式机器人伴侣）进行基准测试，因此提出K9-Bench填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一个可扩展的VLM/LLM驱动的数据生成流水线，自动从网络挖掘犬类中心视频，并生成需要细粒度、多跳推理的问答对。同时设计了偏差缓解策略，消除VLM在数据集构建过程中引入的偏见。数据集包含907个视频、约5000个QA对，覆盖5个任务类别，测试长形式、犬类中心的多模态推理能力。

Result: 前沿MLLM在犬类中心任务上零样本表现有限；闭源模型优于开源模型；模型在需要组合推理的细微姿态和长时序交互线索上表现挣扎；通用思维链提示对长时序推理的提升有限。

Conclusion: K9-Bench不仅提供了犬类活动分析的新数据集，还提供了一个可用于其他低数据领域的通用数据集构建流水线，推动AI系统在动物中心场景下的能力评估与发展。

Abstract: MLLMs have shown strong zero-shot capabilities across diverse inputs such as across images, video, audio, and text. A crucial, yet underexplored, application of these models lies in understanding and modeling animal-centric scenarios. As animals are integral to millions of households, benchmarking next-generation AI models on pet-focused tasks, ranging from recognizing distress signals to enabling responsive robotic companions, is essential for building AI systems that can work alongside us. We introduce K9-Bench, a novel benchmark focused on real-world domestic dog videos, specifically targeting canine action and interaction understanding via approximately 5000 question-answer pairs across 907 videos spanning 5 distinct task categories that test long-form, canine-centric multimodal reasoning in MLLMs. To create this dataset, we propose a scalable, VLM/LLM-powered data generation pipeline that automatically mines canine-centric videos from the web and curates QA pairs requiring fine-grained, multi-hop reasoning over canine actions and temporally extended interaction sequences. We implement bias mitigation strategies designed to eliminate biases introduced by VLMs during dataset curation. Through extensive experimentation, we find that frontier MLLMs exhibit limited zero-shot performance on canine-centric tasks: although state-of-the-art closed-source models outperform open-source counterparts, they still struggle with compositional reasoning over subtle posture and interaction cues spread over long horizons. We observe that generic chain-of-thought prompting provides only modest performance for such long-horizon reasoning. Beyond a novel dataset for canine activity analysis, K9-Bench provides a general-purpose dataset construction pipeline that can be adapted to other low-data domains for quantitative analysis. Our project website is available at: https://ogmenrobotics.github.io/K9Bench.

</details>


### [46] [S-EMBER: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Streaming Egocentric Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02689)
*Xiaodong Wang,Xuanyi Zhao,Pedro Rodriguez,Devendra Singh Sachan,Barlas Oguz,Seungwhan Moon,Shang-Wen Li,Gargi Ghosh,Xin Dong,Wen-Tau Yih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S-EMBER是一个大规模流式自我中心记忆基准测试，包含3,141个视频（388小时）和9,448个QA对，用于评估AI在可穿戴设备上的流式情景记忆检索能力。实验发现"定位悖论"：语义推理随参数规模提升，但时间精确定位能力不随模型规模、分辨率或帧密度增加而改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖离线评估（可访问完整视频），无法模拟可穿戴设备流式实时处理的现实场景，缺乏对因果、主动式情景记忆检索能力的评估。

Method: 构建S-EMBER基准，包含3,141个Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜录制的视频（388小时），设计9,448个需要精确时间定位和可变响应长度的QA对，将任务从全局离线搜索转变为流式触发的情景检索范式。

Result: 发现"定位悖论"：语义推理能力随模型参数规模扩大而提升，但时间定位精度作为架构性瓶颈，不随模型大小、分辨率或帧密度的增加而改善。

Conclusion: S-EMBER为可穿戴AI代理建立了硬件真实的基准，揭示了现有模型在流式情景记忆中的结构性局限，需超越暴力扩展方法来解决时间定位瓶颈。

Abstract: As wearable devices enable continuous first-person recording, AI assistants must reason across long time horizons to recall past experiences-a capability known as episodic memory. Current benchmarks often rely on offline evaluation with access to entire video files, failing to simulate the streaming reality of wearable intelligence. We introduce S-EMBER (Streaming Egocentric Memory Benchmark for Episodic Retrieval), a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,141 videos totaling 388 hours of organic activity captured via Ray-Ban Meta smart glasses. S-EMBER formalizes grounded streaming episodic retrieval, a paradigm shift from global offline search to causal, active recall triggered by visual events in a continuous stream. We provide 9,448 QA pairs requiring manual visual proof through precise temporal localization and supporting flexible response lengths to simulate natural human-AI interaction. Our extensive benchmarking of frontier models uncovers a localization paradox: while semantic reasoning improves with parameter scale, temporal grounding precision remains a stagnant architectural bottleneck that does not benefit from brute-force increases in model size, resolution, or frame density. S-EMBER establishes a hardware-authentic foundation for developing grounded, reliable episodic memory in the next generation of wearable AI agents.

</details>


### [47] [An Automated Multimodal Glaucoma Detection Framework Using ViT and a Stacking-Based Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02692)
*Ishrat Jahan,Muhammad E. H Chowdhury,Murugappan Murugappan,Kanchon Kanti Podder,Tawsifur Rahman,Shrestha Datta,Md Sakib Abrar Hossain,Md Mosarrof Hossen,Yosra Magdi Salih Mekki,Sanjiban Sekhar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合眼底图像和临床数据的多模态青光眼检测框架，在样本级和患者级两种评估设置下均取得优异性能（样本级准确率97.47%，患者级准确率98.97%），并部署为端到端Web平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统青光眼诊断流程耗时且依赖专家经验，难以大规模筛查，因此需要自动化的检测方法。

Method: 提出多模态框架，融合眼底图像（使用Vision Transformer提取深度视觉特征）和临床数据；在样本级设置下分别用图像、临床特征及多模态组合进行检测；在患者级设置下聚合多张眼底图像表示与临床信息；采用堆叠集成三个最佳分类器优化性能。

Result: 在PAPILA数据集上，样本级多模态分类准确率97.47%、F1-score 97.50%；患者级检测准确率和F1-score均达98.97%。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态框架在青光眼检测中表现优异，并已部署为Web平台，可支持自动化筛查和临床决策辅助。

Abstract: Glaucoma is a progressive eye disease that can lead to irreversible vision loss if not detected at an early stage. Conventional diagnostic procedures are often time-consuming and rely heavily on expert interpretation, limiting their scalability for large-scale screening. In this study, glaucoma detection is investigated under two evaluation settings: sample-wise, where individual samples are analyzed independently, and patient-wise, where data from each patient are aggregated for final prediction. An automated multimodal framework is proposed that integrates fundus images with clinical data. Under the sample-wise setting, detection is performed using fundus images and clinical features individually, as well as through their multimodal combination. Under the patient-wise setting, predictions are obtained by aggregating multiple fundus image representations with corresponding clinical information for each patient. Deep visual features are extracted using a Vision Transformer (ViT) architecture and classified using classical machine-learning models, with a stacking-based ensemble of the three best-performing classifiers employed to optimize performance. Experiments conducted on the publicly available PAPILA dataset demonstrate strong diagnostic performance, achieving 97.47% accuracy and a 97.50% F1-score for sample-wise multimodal classification, and 98.97% accuracy and F1-score for subject-wise detection. The proposed framework is further deployed as an end-to-end web-based platform to support automated glaucoma screening and clinical decision support.

</details>

Abstract: This study presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) framework for generating 3D porous media volumes with controlled porosity, trained exclusively on 2D thin section images. The key innovation lies in combining property-conditioned generation with 2D-to-3D reconstruction, eliminating the need for expensive 3D training data while maintaining control over petrophysical properties. The framework employs a hybrid architecture with a 3D generator and 2D discriminator, where multi-axis slice extraction enables learning 3D-consistent structures from 2D training data. Porosity labels are extracted using an Enhanced U-Net segmentation model. The methodology was demonstrated on two carbonate samples with different lithologies: dolomite-anhydrite and pure dolomite. Results show that the framework successfully generates realistic 3D volumes capturing lithological features such as anhydrite inclusions and fine crystalline textures. Porosity control achieved an $R^2$ of 0.93, with mean absolute errors of 0.019 and 0.010 for the heterogeneous and homogeneous samples, respectively.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Gabor滤波增强和YOLOv11的卫星图像目标检测框架，在飞机检测任务上实现了95%的mAP、97%的精度、85%的召回率和91%的F1分数，适用于实时监控和遥感分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: mAP 95%，精确率97%，召回率85%，F1-score 91%，在卫星图像飞机检测任务上表现优异。

Abstract: The object detection in satellite imagery has garnered considerable attention due to its extensive real-world applications and the inherent challenges it presents, including noise, fluctuating image quality, and intricate backgrounds. This paper proposed a framework for object detection that combines image enhancement and Deep Learning (DL) to make detection more accurate. First, a Gabor filter is used to process the input image to bring out important features and reduce noise. Then, normalization is applied to make sure that the data is evenly distributed so that the model works properly. After that, a model based on YOLOv11 is used to quickly learn and find object features. The proposed method achieves a mAP of 95%, precision of 97%, recall of 85%, and F1-score of 91%, which demonstrates the superior aircraft detection performance. These results show the framework accurately identify aircraft in satellite imagery and is suitable for real-time applications such as surveillance, air traffic monitoring and remote sensing analysis.

### [50] [VLRC: Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency as a scalable signal for better feed-forward 3D pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02707)
*Marwane Hariat,David Filliat,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉-语言重投影一致性（VLRC），利用冻结的视觉-语言表征作为语义多视图监督信号，通过重投影稠密特征并强制视图间一致性，无需额外3D标注即可提升前馈3D模型的重建精度和开放词汇3D语义分割能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D模型训练依赖昂贵的几何监督或自监督光度目标，两者均提供不完整的学习信号，缺乏语义层面的引导。

Method: 提出VLRC辅助目标：1) 利用冻结的视觉-语言表征作为语义多视图监督；2) 将预测的3D重建结果重投影到多个视图，提取密集视觉-语言特征；3) 强制不同视图中对应图像位置的特征一致性，无需额外3D标注。

Result: 在室内外基准测试中，VLRC在3D重建精度和零样本开放词汇3D语义分割上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: VLRC通过将几何与语言锚定特征对齐，不仅改善了深度和相机估计，还实现了更连贯的多视图语义融合，为开放词汇3D场景理解提供了可扩展的监督方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models are commonly trained using either expensive geometric supervision or self-supervised photometric objectives, both of which provide incomplete learning signals. We introduce Vision-Language Reprojection Consistency (VLRC), a scalable auxiliary objective that exploits frozen vision-language representations as semantic multi-view supervision. Given a predicted 3D reconstruction, VLRC reprojects dense vision-language features across views and enforces feature consistency between corresponding image locations, requiring no additional 3D annotations. The objective integrates seamlessly with both self-supervised monocular reconstruction and supervised-pretrained feed-forward 3D models during unlabeled adaptation. By aligning geometry with language-grounded features, VLRC not only improves depth and camera estimation but also enables more coherent multi-view semantic fusion for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding. Experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in 3D reconstruction accuracy and zero-shot open-vocabulary 3D semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [51] [When Does Resolution Help a Frozen Backbone? Global Attention at Resolution Predicts Scalable Adaptation for Camouflaged and Marine Animal Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02708)
*Tyler Rust,Chandra Kambhamettu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结视觉基础模型进行细粒度分割时，骨干网络的后验选择至关重要。各向同性ViT（全局注意力+高分辨率）优于分层骨干网络（局部窗口+池化），在低秩适配条件下效果显著。基于SALT框架，各向同性骨干在伪装目标、海洋动物等数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究冻结视觉基础模型进行细粒度分割时，骨干网络架构（尤其是注意力机制类型和分辨率处理方式）对分割性能的影响，并揭示低秩适配条件下的性能规律。

Method: 设计六种骨干网络的受控实验，通过编辑骨干网络（移除全局注意力、保留池化）进行消融分析，采用固定管道SALT（Side-stem, Attention-gated U-Net, Low-rank Tuning）进行统一评估。

Result: 各向同性ViT随分辨率提升持续改善，分层骨干在低分辨率时性能饱和；池化保留性能增益，移除全局注意力则增益消失；该效应仅在低秩适配条件下成立。SALT+各向同性骨干在MAS3K上mIoU达0.878。

Conclusion: 低秩适配背景下，骨干网络的全局注意力机制和高分辨率处理能力是提升细粒度分割性能的关键因素；各向同性ViT在多个伪装和显著目标数据集上达到新SOTA。

Abstract: Adapting frozen vision foundation models to fine-grained segmentation now largely depends on backbone selection. Whether the backbone applies global attention to a high-resolution token set predicts whether a low-rank adapter turns resolution into accuracy. Isotropic ViTs attend globally over the full grid and keep improving with resolution; hierarchical backbones confine early attention to local windows and pool the grid before their global stages, plateauing at lower resolutions. A controlled six-backbone study establishes the pattern, and editing the backbone points to the cause: pooling keeps the benefit, removing global attention does not. The effect is specific to low-rank adaptation. Under one fixed pipeline, SALT (Side-stem, Attention-gated U-Net, Low-rank Tuning), one RGB-only pass on a strong isotropic backbone wins the best S-measure on the four data-matched camouflaged sets, and leads every marine and salient set. It reaches a new state of the art on both marine-animal benchmarks (MAS3K mIoU 0.878).

</details>


### [52] [RayTun3R: Online Camera Adaptation in 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02711)
*Daniil Sinitsyn,Nikita Araslanov,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayTun3R是一种轻量级相机适配方法，通过仅调整位置编码（10,752个可训练参数）来消除3D基础模型的针孔相机偏差，显著提升其在鱼眼图像上的深度和姿态估计性能，旋转误差降低2-12倍，性能优于LoRA且参数减少约14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D基础模型（如DUSt3R、MASt3R、VGGT、π³、Depth Anything 3）在针孔图像上表现优异，但在鱼眼相机几何下性能急剧下降。作者发现这一失败部分源于预训练模型中位置编码的针孔相机偏差，因此需要一种轻量级适配方法来解决该问题。

Method: RayTun3R保持预训练网络固定，仅适配与token位置和相机几何相关的轻量级组件。包括：1) 对绝对位置编码和旋转位置编码学习参数高效的残差修正；2) 无参数的token化处理；3) 对预测网格坐标进行修正以消除残余针孔假设。总可训练参数仅10,752个。

Result: 在视场角从110°到200°的多种鱼眼数据集上：旋转误差降低2-12倍；性能优于LoRA同时使用约14倍更少的可训练参数；优于无适配基线同时避免其多视图推理开销；深度精度保持竞争力。适配后可直接迁移到序列剩余帧，无额外运行时开销。

Conclusion: RayTun3R通过极小参数量（10,752）的轻量级适配，有效消除了3D基础模型中的针孔相机偏差，使其能够泛化到鱼眼相机几何，是一种高效、实用的相机适配方案。

Abstract: Recent 3D foundation models, such as DUSt3R, MASt3R, VGGT, $π^3$, and Depth Anything 3, provide strong feed-forward depth and pose estimates on pinhole imagery, but degrade sharply under fisheye camera geometry. We show that this failure is partly caused by a pinhole camera bias in the positional encodings of pretrained 3D foundation models, and propose RayTun3R, a lightweight camera adaptation approach. It keeps the pretrained network fixed and adapts only lightweight components tied to token position and camera geometry. RayTun3R learns parameter-efficient residual corrections to absolute and rotary positional encodings, together with parameter-free tokenization and corrections to prediction-grid coordinates that remove residual pinhole assumptions. The resulting adapter contains only 10,752 trainable parameters and can be learned from a short temporal segment using geometric losses. Once adapted, RayTun3R transfers effectively to the remaining frames of the sequence without incurring additional runtime costs. Across diverse fisheye datasets with fields of view from $110^\circ$ to $200^\circ$, our adapter reduces rotation error by $2$-$12\times$ relative to the unadapted model, outperforms LoRA while using $\sim\!14\times$ fewer trainable parameters, improves pose over adaptation-free baselines while avoiding their multi-view inference cost, and remains competitive on depth accuracy.

</details>


### [53] [Global Pose Control for Generative View Synthesis in Normalized Object Coordinate Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02712)
*Zhibing Li,Amogh Gupta,Behnoosh Parsa,Dan Casas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化物体坐标空间（NOCS）的新视角合成方法，通过绝对相机位姿控制和图像编辑框架，实现了从单张或少量未标定图像生成精确、一致且可全局控制的新视角图像，达到最先进的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新视角合成方法缺乏全局且直观的视角控制能力：它们要么使用输入图像相关的相对相机位姿，要么只能生成稀疏的全局视角，严重限制了NVS在下游任务中的应用。

Method: 1) 在可定制的NOCS空间中仅使用目标视角的绝对相机位姿，无需输入图像的相机位姿或相对世界坐标系；2) 将NVS重新形式化为图像编辑问题，基于先进编辑模型构建；3) 通过上下文多模态条件策略注入专用相机token；4) 引入文本描述显式定义物体规范坐标框架以解决NOCS歧义；5) 构建了具有一致朝向对齐和对应NOCS文本定义的高质量数据集。

Result: 在多样类别上从任意未标定图像生成具有准确一致朝向的新视角，达到最先进的图像质量和保真度。

Conclusion: 该方法通过绝对位姿控制、图像编辑框架和文本辅助的NOCS定义，有效解决了NVS中缺乏全局视角控制的问题，并展现了跨类别的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Novel View Synthesis (NVS) enables the generation of unseen views of a scene from a single or multiple images, allowing users to freely explore an object from any viewpoint. Despite the recent impressive qualitative improvements of generative models for this task, existing methods struggle to provide global and intuitive control of target viewpoints because they either use input-relative camera poses or are limited to generating sparse global views. This lack of global pose control severely limits the number of downstream tasks potentially enabled by NVS. To address this limitation, we propose a novel approach for precise camera control in a customizable Normalized Object Coordinate Space (NOCS), requiring single or few unposed images. Our method operates solely on the absolute camera pose of the target view in NOCS, eliminating the need for a relative world frame or camera poses of the input images. Unlike previous methods that treat NVS as a standalone generation task, we formulate it as an image editing problem and build upon state-of-the-art editing models to leverage their superior generalization capability. Camera information is injected as dedicated camera tokens via an in-context multi-modal conditioning strategy. To alleviate the inherent ambiguity of NOCS, we incorporate text descriptions that explicitly define the object's canonical coordinate frame, which also enhances generalization to unseen object categories. Furthermore, we curate a high-quality dataset with consistently aligned orientations and corresponding NOCS text definitions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method robustly generates novel views with accurate and consistent orientations from arbitrary unposed images across diverse categories, achieving state-of-the-art image quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [54] [Diagnosing Aerial-View Object Detectors with Foundational Image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02718)
*Stanislav Panev,Minhyek Jeon,Vaishnavi Khindkar,Ahish Deshpande,Celso M de Melo,Shuowen Hu,Shayok Chakraborty,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可控合成数据诊断航拍车辆检测模型的方法，通过文本引导生成、属性编辑和自动验证构建合成测试集，能够揭示真实检测模型在不同场景下的性能弱点，并指导高效的数据补充，在三个检测架构和三个真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模图像生成模型在航拍和遥感领域作为诊断工具的价值尚未被探索，现有真实数据集难以隔离不同场景和环境条件进行细粒度评估。

Method: 结合文本引导生成、属性控制编辑和自动属性验证，构建可控合成测试集，用于对预训练检测器进行细粒度评估。

Result: 合成场景性能趋势与真实世界弱点高度一致；基于诊断结果的目标性补充小样本真实数据，可在远少于非目标增强样本的条件下，实现AP50提升高达13%。

Conclusion: 可控合成探测能够预测真实域性能差距并指导高效数据收集；该框架模块化设计，可随生成模型和视觉-语言模型能力演进而升级。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale image generative models enable photorealistic scene synthesis with controllable attributes. Beyond data augmentation, their potential as diagnostic tools for trained vision systems remains unexplored in the aerial and remote sensing domains. We introduce a synthetic diagnostic framework for aerial-view vehicle detection that combines text-guided generation, attribute-controlled editing, and automated attribute verification to construct a controllable synthetic testbed. This enables fine-grained evaluation of pretrained detectors under diverse scene types and environmental conditions that are difficult to isolate in real datasets. Across three detection architectures and three real aerial datasets, synthetic scene-wise performance trends closely match real-world weaknesses. Guided by these diagnostics, targeted supplementation with small real datasets from the identified weak categories yields improvements of up to 13% AP50 while requiring substantially fewer additional samples than non-targeted augmentation. Our results show that controlled synthetic probing can predict real-domain performance gaps and guide efficient data collection. The proposed diagnostic framework is modular and can incorporate alternative generative or vision-language models as capabilities evolve. Our code and datasets are available here: https://humansensinglab.github.io/AVODDiag/

</details>


### [55] [GRCD: Grounded Region Change Detection for Multi-Finding Chest X-Ray Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02719)
*OFM Riaz Rahman Aranya,Peyman Najafirad,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了GRCD框架，用于从胸部X光片对生成多发现、空间定位的随访报告，通过区域引导的变化标记模块和双路径策略，在文本生成和临床准确性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么缺乏时间上下文建模，要么有时间信息但缺乏空间定位，而少数结合时空推理的方法仅限于单发现描述，无法处理多发现且变化方向混合的报告生成问题。

Method: 1) 构建了严格清洗的时间胸部X光片对数据集，修正了原始标注中的两类系统错误；2) 提出区域引导的变化标记模块，编码每个解剖区域的时序变化；3) 采用双路径策略，将前置空间标记与门控交叉注意力相结合，将区域变化信号注入语言模型。

Result: 在多发现测试集上，GRCD在文本生成和临床准确性指标上优于现有基线，尤其在变化检测方面表现更优。消融实验证实双路径设计优于单一策略，区域级变化编码对多发现生成至关重要。

Conclusion: GRCD通过区域级变化编码和双路径注入策略，有效解决了多发现接地报告生成问题，为胸部X光片时序分析提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Radiologists routinely compare current and prior chest X-rays to track disease progression, producing follow-up reports that describe multiple findings, each localised to an anatomical region and annotated with a temporal change status. Existing automated methods either generate reports from a single image without modelling temporal context, or incorporate temporal information but do not ground their outputs spatially. The few approaches that combine temporal reasoning with spatial grounding are restricted to single-finding descriptions, leaving multi-finding reports with mixed change directions unaddressed. We present GRCD, a framework for grounded report generation from chest X-ray pairs in the multi-finding setting. We first construct a rigorously cleaned dataset of temporal chest X-ray pairs by identifying and correcting two systematic labelling errors in the source annotations. We then introduce a Region-Guided Change Token module that encodes per-region temporal change across anatomical structures and injects this signal into a language model through a dual-pathway strategy combining prepended spatial tokens with gated cross-attention. On a multi-finding test set, GRCD outperforms existing baselines on text generation and clinical accuracy metrics, with gains in change detection. Ablation studies confirm that the dual-pathway design outperforms either integration strategy in isolation on text and clinical metrics, and that region-level change encoding is necessary for multi-finding generation. Code is available at https://github.com/UTSA-VIRLab/GRCD

</details>


### [56] [Signal from Space: Detecting Schools and Towers to Bridge the Digital Divide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02724)
*Zakarya Elmimouni,Sandor Farkas,Fares Fourati,Vladimir Daigele,Walid Mathlouthi,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于卫星图像和迁移学习的视觉框架，用于同时检测学校和信号塔，从而评估学校互联网连接状况，并在莱索托地区展示了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数百万学龄儿童因学校未联网而无法接入互联网，Giga倡议致力于连接每所学校，但大规模联网需要高效的方法来测绘学校并评估周边网络基础设施，而现有第三方数据集稀疏且不可靠。

Method: 提出一个纯视觉的、可扩展的框架，利用高分辨率卫星图像和迁移学习，通过适配预训练的目标检测模型，在少量标注数据下同时检测学校和信号塔，并分析两者空间关系作为连接可用性的代理指标。

Result: 在莱索托的真实卫星图像上进行了验证，在该区域表现出强劲的性能。

Conclusion: 该纯图像驱动的流水线能够实现大规模基础设施测绘，减少对辅助数据的依赖，并支持数据驱动地优先投资于网络覆盖不足的地区。

Abstract: Reliable internet access is essential for modern education, yet millions of school-aged children especially in developing regions remain offline due to unconnected schools. The Giga Initiative aims to connect every school to the internet, but doing so at scale requires efficient methods to map schools and assess surrounding connectivity infrastructure without relying on sparse or noisy third-party datasets. In this work, we propose a scalable, vision-only framework that uses high-resolution satellite imagery and transfer learning to address both tasks simultaneously. By adapting pre-trained object detection models to new geographical regions with minimal labeled data, we detect schools and cell towers directly from space. We then analyze the spatial relationship between detected schools and nearby towers as a proxy for connectivity availability. This purely imagery-driven pipeline enables large-scale infrastructure mapping, reduces dependency on auxiliary data, and supports data-driven prioritization of connectivity investments in underserved areas. Our approach is demonstrated on real satellite imagery from Lesotho, showing strong performance across this region.

</details>


### [57] [Biomechanics-aware Multi-view Markerless Motion Capture of Dexterous Hand Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02796)
*Pouyan Firouzabadi,J. D. Peiffer,Kunal Shah,Anton Sobinov,Lee E. Miller,R. James Cotton,Wendy M. Murray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度优化的生物力学重建方法（端到端方法），用于无标记手部运动捕捉。与传统的两阶段方法（先三角化2D关键点再施加生物力学约束）相比，该方法在121段视频中全部成功收敛（传统方法15%失败），且在手部运动学合理性、遮挡鲁棒性方面表现更优，尤其适用于物体操作等复杂任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标记运动捕捉在人体生物力学分析中广泛应用，但在复杂手部运动的应用相对滞后。本研究旨在评估一种将梯度优化与生物力学模型相结合的方法，以实现对灵巧、无约束手部运动的自动追踪。

Method: 使用定制8相机系统采集6名参与者、11种任务（6种手势+5种物体操作）的121段视频。提出端到端MMC流水线，采用梯度优化方法并将生物力学模型置于循环中；与两阶段方法（先三角化2D关键点至3D，再通过约束逆运动学施加生物力学约束）进行对比。性能评估采用定性视觉检查和定量计算机视觉指标。

Result: 提出方法在121段视频中全部收敛；两阶段方法在15%的录制中未能收敛。在剩余视频中，提出方法生成的手部运动学更符合生物力学合理性，且在涉及物体的任务中对遮挡效应更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 端到端方法在利用2D关键点信息方面更有效，相对更鲁棒。该方法有望支持临床评估、康复监测及人类运动控制研究等领域。

Abstract: Markerless motion capture (MMC) techniques have been widely beneficial in biomechanical analysis of human movement; however, application to complex motions of the hand lags other musculoskeletal systems. The primary goal of this study was to evaluate the performance of a biomechanical reconstruction method that implements a gradient-based optimization approach with a biomechanical model in the loop for tracking dexterous, unconstrained hand movements using MMC. Using a custom, 8-camera setup, we acquired 121 video recordings from 6 participants performing 11 different tasks that spanned 6 hand postures, 5 object manipulation tasks, and involved motion of the proximal upper limb joints. Performance of the proposed MMC pipeline was directly compared to a more commonly adopted two-stage reconstruction method that first triangulates 2D keypoints from computer vision pose estimation algorithms to 3D and then enforces biomechanical constraints by solving a constrained inverse kinematics problem. Relative performance was assessed qualitatively by visual inspection and quantitatively using a computer vision metric. Our method generated solutions for all 121 video recordings; the two-stage method did not converge for 15% of the recordings. Across the remaining videos, our method produced more biomechanically plausible hand kinematics than the two-stage method and was more robust to occlusion effects during tasks that involved objects. The relative robustness of the end-to-end method suggests that it is more effective in utilizing the available 2D digital keypoint information. Automatic and biomechanically meaningful tracking of hand kinematics during dexterous movements has the potential to support clinical evaluation, rehabilitation monitoring, and studies of human motor control.

</details>


### [58] [Conversational Human Audio-visual Talking Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02799)
*Junhao Song,Lluis Guasch,Xilin He,Zhongyu Yang,Yingfang Yuan,Weicheng Xie,Linlin Shen,Haijun Lin,Shizhe Liu,Wei Pang,Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHAT框架，利用大语言模型和说话人脸模型从单文本提示生成配对、互响应的人机对话视频片段，替代昂贵且伦理敏感的真实对话数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模双人交互视听对话数据集是开发交互式虚拟代理的关键资源，但直接采集耗时、昂贵且存在伦理敏感问题。

Method: CHAT框架统一了大型语言模型与说话人脸模型，结合交互式音频和人脸行为精炼模块，从单文本提示生成内容多样、身份可变的配对双人对话片段。

Result: CHAT在客观和主观评估上均优于同类任务现有方法；合成数据集CHAT-AVD-50k作为下游交互式头部生成的预训练数据，持续提升PerFRDiff和ReactDiff在REACT 2024上的性能。

Conclusion: CHAT提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，弥补了真实双人交互数据采集成本高、伦理敏感的不足。

Abstract: Large-scale dyadic interactive audio-visual dialogue (DIAD) datasets provide fundamental data resources for developing humanoid interactive virtual agents and digital humans. However, collecting such data is time-consuming, expensive, and ethically sensitive. To address this, we propose CHAT, a new dyadic interactive audio-visual dialogue generation (DIADG) framework that generates diverse, paired, and mutually responsive speech-face dialogue clips from a single textual prompt. CHAT unifies large language models and talking face models with interactive audio and facial behaviour refinement modules, enabling the generation of aligned dyadic dialogue clips with diverse contents and facial identities. Experiments show that CHAT outperforms existing related methods designed for similar tasks under both objective and subjective evaluations. Moreover, our synthesised CHAT-AVD-50k dataset serves as effective pre-training data for downstream interactive head generation, consistently improving PerFRDiff and ReactDiff on REACT 2024. CHAT offers a scalable alternative to the costly and ethically sensitive collection of real dyadic interaction data.

</details>


### [59] [Prior Bias in Vision Language Models on UML Diagram Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02853)
*Zaiyu Cheng,Khai-Nguyen Nguyen,Antonio Mastropaolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个UML类图基准测试，通过对比一致性与冲突性箭头方向的图表对，发现VLM严重依赖预训练先验知识而非真正理解图表，箭头反转导致方向准确率平均下降33.48%（开源模型），即使GPT-5.4系列也存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在软件工程UML类图理解任务中，是否真正读取图表视觉符号，还是仅依赖预训练先验知识（如类间典型关系）来作答尚不明确，需要系统性诊断。

Method: 设计UML基准测试：为每个符合先验的图表构建一个先验冲突的对照版本——保持相同类名和布局，仅反转关系箭头方向。评估8个开源VLM（InternVL3.5和Qwen3系列）和2个闭源前沿模型（GPT-5.4及Mini版）。

Result: 开源模型在箭头反转后关系方向准确率平均下降33.48%；GPT-5.4 Mini仍有10%的差距。在三类条件下，开源模型准确率骤降45.28%，GPT-5.4系列也下降18.62%。模型规模扩展带来的改进有限且依赖模型家族。

Conclusion: 现有VLM在理解UML类图时存在严重的先验驱动失败问题，无法有效进行基于图表的软件理解，所提基准可作为诊断工具。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to software engineering artifacts, especially UML class diagrams whose meaning depends on visual notation. Yet, it is unclear whether VLMs actually read such diagrams or instead answer from pretrained priors about how classes typically relate. We introduce a controlled UML benchmark in which each prior-conforming diagram is paired with its prior-conflicting counterpart that (1) preserves the same class names and layout while (2) reverses only the relation arrow. We evaluate eight open-source VLMs from two model families, InternVL3.5 and Qwen3, alongside two closed-source frontier models GPT-5.4 and GPT-5.4 Mini. Across the eight open-source models, reversing the arrow reduces relation-direction accuracy by 33.48% on average, while GPT-5.4 Mini retains a 10% gap. In the harder three-class condition, accuracy drops sharply by 45.28% for open-source models, and even 18.62% for the GPT-5.4 family on average. Scaling provides only limited improvements and is family-dependent. Our benchmark presents a diagnostic prior-driven failure in diagram-grounded software understanding. Our artifact is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UMLKnowledgeConflict-8461.

</details>


### [60] [Cancelable Biometric Template Protection Based on Multi-Instance Fusion: A Contralateral Iris Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02860)
*Jittarin Chaivong,Nicha Vikromrotjananan,Teekatat Piriyapittaya,Waree Kongprawechnon,Suradej Duangpummet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需外部令牌的、基于对侧虹膜融合的可撤销模板保护方案，通过多数投票融合、基于注册ID的盐置换和左右眼XOR操作，满足ISO/IEC 24745的不可逆性、不可链接性和机密性要求，在三个数据集上取得了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物特征模板（如虹膜）在未受保护存储时易被窃取，且虹膜一旦泄露无法重新发放。现有可撤销生物特征方案大多需要外部密钥或令牌，引入了额外的攻击面。因此，需要一种无需单独令牌的可撤销虹膜模板保护方法。

Method: 1) 对每只眼睛的三次注册样本使用多数投票融合生成稳定模板；2) 基于用户的注册ID对左右眼模板分别进行基于盐的比特置换；3) 将置换后的左右眼模板进行按位异或（XOR），生成单个受保护融合模板。攻击者需要同时获取左右眼虹膜码和两个盐才能恢复有用信息。

Result: 在CASIA-IrisV4-Interval、CASIA-IrisV2（两个设备）和CASIA-Iris-Thousand数据集上，EER分别为0.36%、4.88%、10.80%和3.35%。最高值对应更具挑战性的跨设备场景。该方法优于未保护的基线，与最先进的可撤销方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 本文首次将无令牌多实例融合与对侧绑定结合，满足ISO/IEC 24745标准。对侧虹膜融合由于左右眼虹膜模式统计独立，提高了系统精度；双密钥空间大于单虹膜方案。消融实验证实了该方法在识别性能和安全性的双重优势。

Abstract: Biometric templates are vulnerable to theft if stored without protection. Unlike passwords, a compromised iris cannot be reissued. Although existing cancelable biometric schemes address this problem, most still require an external key or token, introducing an additional attack surface. This paper proposes a cancelable contralateral iris template protection scheme that eliminates the need for a separate token or stored secret, satisfying the three requirements of ISO/IEC 24745: irreversibility, unlinkability, and confidentiality. The method fuses three enrollment samples per eye using Majority Vote Fusion to produce a stable template, and applies a salt-based bit permutation derived from the subject's enrollment ID. Combining the left- and right-permuted templates via a bitwise XOR produces a single Protected Fused Template. Since left and right iris patterns are statistically independent, fusing contralateral irises improves the accuracy of the system. An attacker must possess both iris codes and both salts to recover any useful information, yielding a larger effective key space than single-iris schemes. Experiments on three datasets, CASIA-IrisV4-Interval, CASIA-IrisV2 (two devices), and CASIA-Iris-Thousand, yield EERs of $0.36$\%, $4.88$\%, $10.80$\%, and $3.35$\%, respectively; the highest value reflects the more challenging cross-device scenario. These results demonstrate that our contralateral approach outperforms unprotected baselines while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art cancelable methods. %In addition, an ablation study confirms the benefit on both recognition performance and security. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first scheme to combine tokenless multi-instance fusion and contralateral binding under ISO/IEC 24745.

</details>


### [61] [E-TraMamba: A New Paradigm for Efficient Long-Term 3D Feature Tracking with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02866)
*Juwei Shen,Yujie Wu,Changwen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: E-TraMamba 是首个基于 Mamba 框架的 3D 特征跟踪模型，通过线性状态空间模型实现高效长程建模，结合轻量级仿射变换预测器处理运动模糊和遮挡，并在大规模合成数据集 EvD-PointOdyssey 上训练，在事件基准测试中达到 SoTA，特征寿命提升 2 倍以上，适用于低延迟视觉里程计和实时 SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CNN 和 Transformer 跟踪器在稀疏、嘈杂的事件流中难以捕获长程时空依赖关系，尤其在实时和效率约束下表现不佳。

Method: 提出 E-TraMamba，采用线性状态空间模型（Mamba）进行高效长程建模，集成轻量级仿射变换预测器处理运动模糊和遮挡，设计多尺度融合方案整合局部时空块、相关图和位置嵌入为统一表示，并构建大规模合成数据集 EvD-PointOdyssey 用于训练与评估。

Result: 在事件基准测试中达到 SOTA，在严格精度阈值（如 0.1 m）下特征寿命提升 2 倍以上，跟踪特征比率更高，RMSE 更低。

Conclusion: E-TraMamba 成为低延迟视觉里程计、实时 SLAM 和交互式机器人的有力候选方案。

Abstract: Event-based 3D tracking enables low-latency and high-speed perception, while existing CNN- and Transformer-based trackers struggle to capture long-range spatiotemporal dependencies in sparse, noisy event streams, especially under real-time and efficiency constraints. To address these challenges, we present E-TraMamba, the first Mamba-based framework for 3D feature tracking on event data. This new framework adopts a linear state-space model for efficient long-range modeling and integrates a lightweight affine-transform predictor to maintain stable tracking under motion blur and occlusion. We also design an effective scheme to fuse multi-scale cues -- local spatiotemporal patches, correlation maps, and positional embeddings -- into a unified representation that enables stable and smooth 3D tracking. We construct a large-scale synthetic dataset, named EvD-PointOdyssey, which is generated with monocular rendering and provides synchronized event streams, depth maps, and accurate 3D trajectories for training and evaluating event-based 3D tracking models. Extensive experiments on event-based benchmarks demonstrate that E-TraMamba achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering over $2\times$ longer feature lifetimes under strict accuracy thresholds (e.g., 0.1 m), with higher tracked-feature ratios and lower RMSE than all baselines. These results make E-TraMamba a strong candidate for low-latency visual odometry, real-time SLAM, and interactive robotics.

</details>


### [62] [SPLIT: Training-Free AI-Generated and Partially Edited Video Detection via Spatial Patch-Level Incoherence and Temporal Roughness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02886)
*Jongyeop Hyun,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV
