TL;DR: SPLIT是一种无需训练的视频AI生成检测器，通过空间补丁级不连贯性和时间粗糙度两个互补信号，在极低假阳性率下实现高效检测，并在三个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景部署AI生成视频检测器需要极低的假阳性率，避免误拒真实内容，而AUROC等标准指标无法反映这一极端工况下的实际性能。

Method: 提出SPLIT检测器，包含两个互补信号：两步时间粗糙度（TTR）通过一步和两步特征变化对比捕捉非平滑补丁轨迹；局部空间运动不连贯性（LSMI）通过特征空间运动场的梯度测量空间不一致的时间变化。两者通过伽马校正进行乘法融合以锐化真实-伪造分离。同时提出服务对齐的评估协议，基于固定FPR下的Fake Recall，采用仅真实阈值校准和跨真实阈值迁移。

Result: 在FakeParts、GenVideo和ViF-Bench三个基准上，SPLIT在FPR=0.1%时实现了最高的Fake Recall，大幅优于有监督和无监督基线，且对后处理具有鲁棒性，计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: SPLIT作为一种无需训练的检测器，通过精心设计的空间-时间不连贯性特征，在严格的低假阳性率要求下实现了卓越的检测性能，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Deploying AI-generated video detectors in real-world services demands an ultra-low false positive rate (FPR) on real videos to avoid falsely rejecting authentic content, a regime where standard metrics such as AUROC fail to reflect actual operating behavior. We introduce Spatial Patch-Level Incoherence and Temporal Roughness (SPLIT), a training-free detector that operates on patch tokens from a frozen vision encoder to detect both fully generated and partially edited videos. SPLIT computes two complementary signals: Two-step Temporal Roughness (TTR), capturing non-smooth patch trajectories via one-step and two-step feature variation contrast, and Local Spatial Motion Incoherence (LSMI), measuring spatially inconsistent temporal changes through gradients of a feature-space motion field. The two are fused multiplicatively with gamma correction to sharpen real-fake separation at strict thresholds. We further propose a service-aligned evaluation protocol based on Fake Recall at fixed FPR with real-only threshold calibration and cross-real threshold transfer. Across three benchmarks (FakeParts, GenVideo, and ViF-Bench), SPLIT achieves the highest Fake Recall at FPR = $0.1\%$, substantially outperforming supervised and training-free baselines while remaining robust to post-processing with negligible overhead. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/mldljyh/SPLIT .

</details>


### [63] [ProLaViT: Learning Progressive Latent Visual Thoughts in Structured Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02907)
*Peiming Li,Yifan Wang,Xiaotian Zhang,Zhiyuan Hu,Shiyu Li,Zheng Wei,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProLaViT提出了一种在连续潜空间中进行结构化视觉推导的框架，通过自蒸馏机制、程序化合成流水线以及两种推理范式（粗到细因果链和辩证推理链），有效提升了多模态大模型在复杂视觉推理任务上的性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在需要多步感知和逻辑推导的复杂视觉推理任务上表现不足；显式视觉生成计算成本过高，而现有隐式方法要么依赖外部专家模型，要么缺乏严谨的认知逻辑。

Method: 提出ProLaViT框架，包含：(1) 内源自蒸馏机制，利用模型自身视觉编码器监督潜在思维；(2) 可扩展的程序化合成流水线，使模型内化算法精度；(3) 两种推理范式：粗到细因果链（空间任务）和辩证推理链（逻辑任务）；(4) 距离加权多样性损失，施加拓扑感知约束防止特征退化。

Result: 在视觉中心基准测试上，ProLaViT优于基线方法，在准确性和可解释性上均取得更好表现，且具有高计算效率。

Conclusion: ProLaViT通过潜空间中的结构化视觉推理，无需外部专家模型和推理时工具，即可实现高效、准确且可解释的复杂视觉推理。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress but still struggle with complex visual reasoning tasks requiring multi-step perception and logical deduction. While explicit visual generation incurs prohibitive computational costs, existing latent approaches often rely on external experts or lack rigorous cognitive logic. In this paper, we introduce ProLaViT (Progressive Latent Visual Thought), a framework empowering MLLMs to perform structured visual derivation in the continuous latent space. Unlike works dependent on heterogeneous external models, ProLaViT leverages an endogenous self-distillation mechanism, utilizing the model's own visual encoder to supervise latent thoughts. To facilitate this, we construct a scalable programmatic synthesis pipeline enabling the model to internalize algorithmic precision without inference time tools. We design two reasoning paradigms: (1) Coarse-to-Fine Causal Chain for spatial tasks, guiding attention from global context to local targets. (2) Dialectical Reasoning Chain for logical tasks, incorporating counter-factual thinking for verification. Furthermore, we propose a Distance-Weighted Diversity Loss to impose topology-aware constraints, preventing feature degeneration by enforcing semantic distinctiveness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ProLaViT outperforms baselines on vision-centric benchmarks, achieving superior accuracy and interpretability with high efficiency.

</details>


### [64] [Holo-Captioning: Toward the Text Equivalent of 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02908)
*Kun-Yu Lin,Chengke Bu,Zhenguo Li,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出全息字幕生成（holo-captioning）新任务，旨在为3D场景生成结构化文本描述，涵盖所有实体的语义标签、空间位置、属性及相互关系。作者构建了包含15K+场景的基准数据集，提出HoloScribe模型（采用实例感知解耦流水线和锚点感知实例链接），以及HoloScore评估指标。实验表明HoloScribe显著优于现有3D密集字幕生成器和3D LLM通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解工作缺乏对场景内所有实体及其关系的全面结构化描述，亟需一种能够生成文本等价物（即结构化全息字幕）的方法来完整表征3D场景。

Method: 1) 构建包含15K+场景的大规模基准数据集；2) 提出HoloScribe模型，采用实例感知解耦流水线生成结构化全息字幕，并引入锚点感知实例链接机制识别关系实例对；3) 提出综合评估指标HoloScore，并提供人工筛选测试集。

Result: HoloScribe在3D密集字幕生成和3D LLM通用任务上显著超越当前最优方法。

Conclusion: 全息字幕生成任务及HoloScribe模型有效提升了3D场景的结构化语义理解能力，为3D场景到文本的映射提供了新范式。

Abstract: This work introduces holo-captioning, a novel task that strives to seek the text equivalent of 3D scenes. As the initial step, we formulate holo-captioning as generating a structured textual description that comprehensively depicts all entities within a 3D scene -- including their semantic tags, spatial locations, attributes, and inter-entity relations. To tackle this challenging task, we first develop an effective captioning engine to produce detailed descriptions of individual entity instances and instance pairs, and contribute a large-scale benchmark comprising over 15K scenes for training and evaluation. Building upon this foundation, we propose HoloScribe, a novel model that features an instance-aware decoupled pipeline for generating structured holo-captions, and further incorporates anchor-aware instance linking to identify relational instance pairs. Additionally, we propose a comprehensive evaluation metric named HoloScore, and provide a human-curated test set to ensure reliable model assessment. Experimental results demonstrate that HoloScribe significantly outperforms state-of-the-art 3D dense captioners and 3D LLM generalists, underscoring the effectiveness of our approach. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/holocap/

</details>


### [65] [Learning Taxonomic Trees with Hierarchical Representation Regularization for Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02909)
*Hulingxiao He,Zhi Tan,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对大多模态模型（LMMs）缺乏分类学知识导致层级视觉识别不一致的问题，本文提出分层表示正则化方法（HiR²），通过语义感知的视觉树构建和两种互补的损失函数（分类学蕴含损失和判别分散损失），有效提升了LMMs的层次一致性，是一种即插即用的正则化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多模态模型虽然能力强大，但在层级视觉识别任务中缺乏分类学知识，导致层级一致性较低。现有模型仅依赖语言建模目标进行微调，缺乏显式的分类学感知正则化。

Method: 提出HiR²正则化器，包含：(1) 语义感知的视觉树构建框架，从中间LLM层提取由文本引导的由粗到细的视觉特征；(2) 分类学蕴含损失，利用Lorentz模型中的双曲蕴含锥强制层级结构；(3) 判别分散损失，在单位球面上提升语义相似嵌入的角分离度，同时不干扰径向层级结构。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiR²能够有效捕获分类学结构，适用于多种LMMs和微调方法，显著提升了层级一致性。

Conclusion: HiR²作为一种即插即用的正则化方法，能够有效弥补LMMs在分类学知识方面的不足，提升层级视觉识别的一致性，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Taxonomies provide key information about the semantic relationships between concepts and the inherent organization of vision and language. Despite their impressive capabilities, large multimodal models (LMMs) often lack taxonomic knowledge, leading to low hierarchical visual recognition (HVR) consistency. These models typically only rely on language modeling objectives during fine-tuning and lack explicit taxonomy-aware regularization. To address this, we propose Hierarchical Representation Regularization ($HiR^2$), a simple plug-and-play regularizer that improves hierarchical consistency in LMMs. Specifically, we introduce a semantic-aware visual tree construction framework that extracts coarse-to-fine visual features from intermediate LLM layers guided by textual cues. The regularizer combines two complementary objectives: a taxonomic entailment loss that enforces hierarchy via hyperbolic entailment cones in the Lorentz model, and a discriminative dispersive loss that promotes angular separation of semantically similar embeddings on the unit sphere without disturbing the radial hierarchical structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $HiR^2$ effectively captures taxonomic structures across diverse LMMs and fine-tuning methods. Code is available at https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/HiR2_ICML2026.

</details>


### [66] [See the Emotion: A Facial Emoji Proxy Modeling for EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02912)
*Jingjing Hu,Guo Dan,Haofan Cheng,Ying Zeng,Zhan Si,Jinxing Zhou,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Facial Emoji Proxy Modeling框架，将EEG情绪识别从特征归因转变为跨模态生成任务，通过生成匿名化面部表情符号来可视化大脑情绪状态，在EAV和MMER基准上达到最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG情绪识别模型是黑箱，缺乏神经特征与可解释情绪状态之间的语义关联。

Method: 提出Facial Emoji Proxy Modeling框架：包含FMENet骨干网络建模表达相关空间协同，以及Facial Emoji Learning Branch (FELB)将表情符号重构作为结构化语义正则化项。

Result: 在EAV和MMER基准上，EEG-only模型中达到最优准确率，同时生成语义保真的面部动画。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了可解释、保护隐私的脑电情绪可视化，让用户能够直接"看到"神经信号中的情绪。

Abstract: Despite the high accuracy of EEG-based emotion recognition, existing models remain opaque "black boxes", lacking semantic grounding between abstract neural features and human-interpretable states. In this paper, we reframe EEG explainability as a cross-modal generation task, shifting the paradigm from feature attribution to behavioral visualization. We introduce Facial Emoji Proxy Modeling, a novel framework that translates high-dimensional EEG signals into identity-anonymized facial emojis. Guided by the neuroscientific inspiration of neural-facial association, this approach grounds neural representations in the manifold of observable facial dynamics. Technically, our framework integrates FMENet, a specialized backbone modeling expression-relevant spatial synergies, and the Facial Emoji Learning Branch (FELB), which treats emoji reconstruction as a structured semantic regularizer. Extensive experiments on EAV and MMER benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among EEG-only models. Crucially, it generates semantically faithful facial animations that provide a transparent, privacy-preserving window into the brain's emotional evolution, effectively allowing users to "see the emotion" directly from neural signals. Code is available at https://github.com/xian-sh/SeeEmotion

</details>


### [67] [R3D: Quantitative 3D Spatial Reasoning for Egocentric Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02921)
*Maxwell Horton,Wei Lu,Quan Tran,Yury Astashonok,Kirmani Ahmed,Babak Damavandi,Anuj Kumar,Xiao Zhang,Seungwhan Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R3D-Bench基准（含3,033个定量3D空间推理问题）和R3D框架（模型无关的空间工具调用框架），在自然第一人称RGB-D视频上进行定量3D空间推理，R3D+Qwen3-VL 235B达到73.5%平均相对准确率，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能反映自然第一人称视频、带位姿的RGB-D视频输入以及挑战性定量3D空间推理问答的难度，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建基于Aria Digital Twin上57段第一人称视频序列的R3D-Bench基准（3,033个问题，涵盖15种类型）；提出R3D框架，通过分割和深度提升的目标表示构建3D场景，并利用八个可组合空间工具向LLM提供信息。

Result: R3D+Qwen3-VL 235B达到73.5%平均相对准确率，优于深度增强基线CuTR+Tools（61.9%）和仅RGB基线Gemini 3 Flash（46.5%）。

Conclusion: R3D框架在定量3D空间推理任务上显著优于现有方法，验证了模型无关的空间工具调用范式的有效性。

Abstract: Quantitative 3D spatial reasoning from egocentric RGB-D video is a critical capability for next-generation wearable assistants. Yet existing benchmarks do not reflect the challenges of handling (1) natural egocentric video, (2) posed RGB-D video inputs, and (3) challenging quantitative 3D spatial reasoning Q&A. To fill this gap, we introduce R3D-Bench (Reasoning in 3D), a benchmark of 3,033 quantitative spatial reasoning questions across 15 types -- spanning multiple-choice, distance-based, and volumetric reasoning questions -- built on top of 57 egocentric video sequences from Aria Digital Twin. To set a strong baseline on this dataset, we introduce R3D, a model-agnostic spatial tool-calling framework. In contrast to existing approaches that directly embed 3D information into the model's input representation, R3D constructs a 3D scene from video using segmentation and depth-lifted object representations. It provides this information to an LLM through eight composable spatial tools. On R3D-Bench, R3D with Qwen3-VL 235B achieves 73.5% mean relative accuracy, substantially outperforming the best depth-enabled baseline (CuTR+Tools, 61.9%) and the best RGB-only baseline (Gemini 3 Flash, 46.5%).

</details>


### [68] [STAC: Selective Spatiotemporal Aggregation and Compression for Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02922)
*Syed Ariff Syed Hesham,Yun Liu,Guolei Sun,Jing Yang,Henghui Ding,Xue Geng,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STAC方法，利用状态空间模型的线性递归实现上下文感知的时空标记压缩，在视频推理分割中达到85%标记缩减和1.8倍加速，同时超越无压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理分割中，密集时空标记的二次自注意成本使得长视频处理不可行；现有标记压缩方法缺乏时间上下文，无法可靠评估内容冗余；而全分辨率获取上下文又会带来相同的二次计算成本。

Method: 提出选择性时空聚合与压缩（STAC），采用解耦双向空间扫描和因果时序扫描，利用状态空间模型线性递归产生的冗余特征，结合分割目标优化的自适应阈值进行层次化压缩。

Result: 实现85%的标记缩减和1.8倍加速，在零样本流式兼容设置下超越无压缩基线方法。

Conclusion: 状态空间模型的线性递归能够以O(T)代价为每个标记提供时间上下文，使内容冗余变得可评估，从而有效指导标记压缩，实现高效长视频推理分割。

Abstract: Video reasoning segmentation demands pixel-accurate object tracking across hundreds of frames under complex natural language queries, producing dense spatiotemporal tokens whose quadratic self-attention cost makes long-video processing prohibitive. Existing methods address this through token compression, yet typically operate on encoder features lacking temporal context, constraining selection before content redundancy can be reliably assessed. Informed compression requires contextual awareness, but acquiring that awareness at full resolution incurs the same quadratic cost compression aims to reduce. State-space models resolve this constraint, as their linear recurrence selectively conditions each token on temporal context at $\mathcal{O}(T)$ cost, producing representations where content redundancy becomes assessable. Building on this, Selective SpatioTemporal Aggregation and Compression (STAC) enriches features via decoupled bidirectional spatial and causal temporal scanning, leveraging recurrence-derived redundancy for hierarchical compression with adaptive thresholds optimised with segmentation objective. STAC achieves 85% token reduction and 1.8$\times$ speedup while surpassing compression-free baselines on reasoning segmentation benchmarks in a zero-shot streaming-compatible setting. Code is available \href{https://github.com/MCG-NKU/nku-video}{here}.

</details>


### [69] [VideoSearcher: Empowering Video Deep Research with Multi-Tool Agentic Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02927)
*Zhenkun Gao,Yicheng Bao,Jinlong Peng,Xueheng Li,Theo Huang,Bangwei Liu,Kunquan Li,Zhenye Gan,Tao Hu,Chengjun Xie,Mingqian Yang,Xuanhua He,Zhizhong Zhang,Xin Tan,Chengjie Wang,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoSearcher，一个用于视频深度研究（VDR）的闭环智能体框架，通过多工具推理、强化学习算法BiSPO和新基准VideoSearch-QA，显著提升了开放世界视频信息定位与多模态搜索推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态搜索智能体主要针对静态图像，而当前的VDR基准依赖于文本中心检索，丢失了关键的视觉信息，无法满足视频理解向开放世界证据探索范式发展的需求。

Method: 提出VideoSearcher闭环智能体框架，统一时间定位、空间聚焦和多模态搜索于单一推理轨迹中；提出BiSPO双分支序列策略优化算法，将工具调用优化与答案准确率优化解耦；构建VideoSearch-QA基准用于评估开放世界视频信息定位和多模态搜索推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，VideoSearcher在多种面向搜索和多模态理解基准上显著优于之前的开源智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: VideoSearcher通过多工具推理与强化学习优化，有效实现了开放世界视频深度研究中的证据锚定、检索与综合，填补了视觉信息在VDR中被忽略的空白。

Abstract: Video understanding is moving beyond closed-context perception toward open-world evidence exploration, a paradigm formalized as Video Deep Research (VDR). However, existing multimodal search agents primarily target static images, and the current VDR benchmark relies on text-centric retrieval that discards crucial visual information. To address these limitations, we propose VideoSearcher, a closed-loop agentic framework that empowers Vision-Language Models with multi-tool reasoning for VDR. VideoSearcher unifies temporal localization, spatial focusing, and multimodal search within a single reasoning trajectory, enabling agents to progressively ground visual clues, retrieve relevant evidence, and synthesize answers. To optimize knowledge-intensive reasoning trajectories, we propose Bi-branch Sequence Policy Optimization (BiSPO), a reinforcement learning algorithm that decouples tool-invocation optimization from answer-accuracy optimization. This design provides stable learning signals for both evidence-grounded reasoning and purposeful tool use. Furthermore, we construct VideoSearch-QA, the first benchmark designed to evaluate open-world video information grounding and multimodal search-based reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VideoSearcher significantly outperforms prior open-source agentic baselines across various search-oriented and multimodal understanding benchmarks.

</details>


### [70] [CL-Anomaly: Layer-Adaptive Mixture-of-Experts with Multimodal Large Language Model for Continual Learning in Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02930)
*Wen Dong,Zhao Wang,Shuangqing Zhang,Kai Sun,Ben Li,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan,Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CL-Anomaly框架，通过隔离-共享协作的参高效微调方法，实现多模态大语言模型在异常检测上的持续学习，解决任务间语义纠缠问题，在类增量、跨域、跨模态三个持续学习场景中均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）全参数重训练计算成本高，现有持续学习方法存在任务间参数空间语义纠缠问题，尤其在异常检测（AD）中面临模态、领域、缺陷尺度的三重异质性，阻碍多任务知识迁移。

Method: 提出CL-Anomaly框架：1) 任务私有专家PrivLoRA，物理隔离任务特定子空间以防止语义纠缠；2) 层自适应共享专家，在统一特征空间中维护跨任务表示；3) 基于动量的层自适应知识迁移策略，自动选择并动态更新各任务的关键共享专家。

Result: 在类增量、跨域、跨模态三个持续学习异常检测场景中，CL-Anomaly均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 隔离-共享协作的参高效微调框架能有效解决MLLMs在异常检测持续学习中的语义纠缠问题，实现跨任务知识迁移，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in diverse vision tasks, but full-parameter retraining is computationally expensive as real-world knowledge evolves. Existing continual learning methods often suffer from semantic entanglement in parameter spaces across tasks, impeding the continuous deployment of models. This challenge is especially pronounced in Anomaly Detection (AD), which exhibits triple heterogeneity across modalities, domains, and defect scale variability, significantly complicating multi-task knowledge transfer. In this paper, we propose CL-Anomaly, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework based on an isolation-sharing collaboration to enable continual learning for anomaly detection with MLLMs. We introduce the task-private expert PrivLoRA, which physically isolates task-specific subspaces in the parameter space to prevent semantic entanglement of anomaly knowledge in diverse scenarios. The Layer-Adaptive Shared Experts maintain cross-task representations within a unified feature space, enabling knowledge sharing between previous and new tasks. Furthermore, we propose a Layer-Adaptive Knowledge Transfer strategy that automatically selects and dynamically updates the layer-wise key shared experts of each task via a momentum-based mechanism, promoting effective knowledge transfer across related anomaly detection tasks. Extensive experiments across three continual learning scenarios for anomaly detection, including class-incremental, cross-domain, and cross-modal, demonstrate that CL-Anomaly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/WenDongyp/CL-Anomaly.

</details>


### [71] [MatPhaseBench: A Semantics-Guided Benchmark for Materials Phase Diagrams Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02934)
*Hanwen Wang,Sihan Liang,Zhiwei Liu,Yangang Wang,Wei Yan,Yuqin Liu,Zongguo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MatPhaseBench基准，一个针对材料科学相图这一复杂科学图像理解的高质量、高可靠性评测数据集，包含200个来自3681篇论文的图表-文本对。实验表明当前视觉语言模型在相图理解上远落后于专家水平，仅限于表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在需要深层机制解释的逻辑复杂科学图像上的系统评估有限，材料相图作为检验其能力的理想测试平台，需要建立专门的高质量基准来推动多模态AI在科学领域的可信发展。

Method: 从3681篇经典材料科学期刊论文中筛选出200个高质量相图-文本对，覆盖189个材料体系和70种元素。基准设计包含三个关键特征：针对复杂科学图像理解的开放任务、保留完整语义信息的图文对齐、以及经过严格人工验证的高质量文本采集。

Result: 当前视觉语言模型表现远低于专家水平，主要局限在表面视觉感知，缺乏基于热力学机制的深度推理，领域意识和专家分析经验有限，在多图或复合图表设置中区分细粒度差异的能力较差。

Conclusion: MatPhaseBench构成了一个具有挑战性的研究级基准，为复杂科学图像理解、相图分析和科学领域的可信多模态AI提供了基础平台。

Abstract: Materials phase diagrams are a core knowledge representation in materials science, encoding temperature,composition, phase stability, and phase transformation pathways, with their full understanding requiring thermodynamic mechanism analysis and scientific reasoning. Although VLMs have shown promise in scientific image understanding, their systematic evaluation on such logically complex images demanding deep mechanistic interpretation remains limited, and phase diagrams provide a challenging testbed for this purpose. We introduce MatPhaseBench, a high-quality, high-reliability benchmark for complex scientific image understanding, focused on materials phase diagrams. MatPhaseBench is constructed from 3681 papers in classical materials science journals, from which 200 high-quality diagram-text pairs were selected, covering 189 material systems and 70 elements. The benchmark has three key features: (1)targeting complex scientific image understanding-it moves beyond simple objective tests to open-ended tasks requiring deep comprehension; (2)comprehensive image-text alignment-semantic information associated with images is fully preserved during literature mining and matching; (3) high-quality human-supervised text acquisition-all descriptions undergo strict manual validation. Experimental results show that current VLMs remain substantially behind expert-level understanding: they are largely limited to surface visual perception, lack deep reasoning grounded in thermodynamic mechanisms, have limited domain awareness and expert analytical experience, and perform poorly in distinguishing fine-grained differences in composite or multi-diagram settings. Overall, MatPhaseBench constitutes a challenging research-grade benchmark, providing a foundational platform for complex scientific image understanding, phase diagram analysis, and trustworthy multi-modal AI in science.

</details>


### [72] [Pooling-Based Context Modeling for Convolution-Free Deep Image Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02952)
*Gihyun Kim,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pool-DIP，一种基于池化对比建模的无卷积架构，替代传统卷积用于深度图像先验，在去噪、超分辨和修复任务中取得竞争性能，同时大幅减少参数和计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图像先验（DIP）虽能仅从单张含噪图像恢复图像，但其过度参数化的卷积架构在优化过程中容易拟合噪声，影响去噪效果。

Method: 提出Pool-DIP，用基于池化的对比建模替代卷积操作来捕获空间上下文信息，构建无卷积架构，降低参数量和计算复杂度。

Result: 在多个数据集（包括真实世界基准）上取得与卷积DIP相当的竞争性能；频谱分析表明Pool-DIP能稳定优化过程中高频成分的演化并抑制错误的高频信号。

Conclusion: Pool-DIP不仅在图去噪任务上表现优异，还能泛化到超分辨率和图像修复等其他图像恢复任务，验证了池化对比建模作为卷积替代方案的潜力。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) achieve strong denoising performance by exploiting spatial context from neighboring pixels. Deep Image Prior (DIP) leverages this property to restore images from a single noisy input without requiring large datasets. However, the over-parameterized architecture of DIP often leads to noise fitting during optimization. In this paper, we propose Pool-DIP, a convolution-free architecture that incorporates pooling-based contrast modeling to capture spatial context efficiently. Pool-DIP improves denoising performance while significantly reducing the number of parameters and computational complexity compared to convolution-based DIP models. Experimental results show that Pool-DIP achieves competitive performance across multiple datasets, including a real-world benchmark. Spectral analysis further reveals that Pool-DIP stabilizes the evolution of high-frequency components during optimization and suppresses erroneous high-frequency signals. The proposed architecture also generalizes well to other image restoration tasks such as super-resolution and inpainting.

</details>


### [73] [MORE: A Multilingual Document Parsing Benchmark and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02956)
*Long Xu,Binghong Wu,Tinghao Yu,Hao Feng,Zhenyu Huang,Haoqing Jiang,Yunhao Wang,Shuo Huang,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MORE，一个覆盖149种语言的大规模多语言文档解析基准，通过结构化元素（代码块、表格、目录）评估和真实数据标注，填补了现有基准对低资源语言评估的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准主要聚焦英语和中文等高资源语言，对模型在其他语言上的性能存在评估盲区；虽然视觉语言模型声称支持数百种语言，但缺乏真实标注数据来验证这些能力。

Method: 构建了覆盖149种语言的大规模基准MORE，采用模型辅助+人工精炼的标注流程从真实文档中采集样本，评估范围从纯文本扩展到代码块、表格、目录等结构化元素。

Result: 对现有最先进的视觉语言模型进行了评估，建立了长尾语言的新性能基线，验证了基准在诊断模型处理真实多样化场景能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: MORE作为最具语言多样性的文档解析基准，能够有效评估和诊断模型在低资源语言和复杂结构上的表现，填补了多语言评估的重要空白。

Abstract: Multilingual documents encapsulate rich regional cultures, scientific discoveries, and historical records. Parsing this content into structured, machine-readable formats is critical for unlocking global knowledge. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on high-resource languages like English and Chinese, creating an evaluation blind spot concerning model performance on other languages. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) claim support for hundreds of languages, the lack of ground truth makes it impossible to empirically verify these capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MORE, a large-scale benchmark designed for multilingual document parsing evaluation. MORE distinguishes itself through three key dimensions: (1) Unprecedented Scale: It covers 149 languages, making it the most linguistically diverse benchmark to date; (2) Structural Complexity: Unlike previous works, it extends evaluation beyond plain text to include structural elements such as code blocks, tables, and catalogs; and (3) Data Authenticity: All samples are curated from real-world documents via a model-assisted, human-refined annotation pipeline. We evaluate state-of-the-art models using MORE, establishing new performance baselines for long-tail languages and validating the benchmark's effectiveness in diagnosing model capabilities in realistic, diverse scenarios. The MORE dataset will be available at https://github.com/zimoqingfeng/MORE.

</details>


### [74] [ReLo-IRR: Reflection-Guided LoRA Framework for Image Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02957)
*Chaoqun Wang,Yuehuan Wei,Haoxiang Cao,Shaobo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLo-IRR，一种基于反射引导的LoRA框架，利用轻量级估计器预测反射强度描述符，并结合时间条件机制实现与去噪过程一致的动态LoRA调制，从而在不同反射强度下实现鲁棒的单图像反射去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的SIRR方法采用图像无关的适配策略（如微调或ControlNet），对所有图像进行统一抑制，导致强反射残留、弱反射细节丢失，缺乏对反射严重程度的自适应能力。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级估计器预测反射强度描述符，提供反射主导性的显式先验，实现图像相关的LoRA调制；2) 引入时间条件机制，将反射描述符与时间步嵌入融合，使LoRA调制随粗到细的去噪过程协同演化。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准测试上，ReLo-IRR取得了优越的去反射性能和鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模反射强度和去噪动力学，ReLo-IRR实现了对不同反射条件的鲁棒抑制，有效解决了现有方法在强/弱反射下的失衡问题。

Abstract: Single-image reflection removal (SIRR) aims to recover the clean transmission layer from a reflection-contaminated image. Although recent methods achieve promising results with large diffusion models, they rely on image-agnostic adaptation strategies, e.g., fine-tuning or ControlNet, that enforce uniform suppression regardless of reflection severity. As a result, heavy reflections often leave residuals, while weak ones suffer from detail loss. To this end, we propose ReLo-IRR, a reflection-guided LoRA framework built upon the rectified flow model. First, a lightweight estimator is designed to predict the reflection strength descriptor, providing an explicit prior of reflection dominance for each image and enabling image-dependent LoRA modulation. Second, we introduce a time-conditioned mechanism that fuses this reflection descriptor with timestep embeddings, enabling LoRA modulation to evolve consistently with the coarse-to-fine denoising process. By jointly modeling reflection strength and denoising dynamics, our ReLo-IRR achieves robust suppression of diverse reflection conditions. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks validate the effectiveness of ReLo-IRR, demonstrating superior dereflection performance and robust generalization. The code is released at https://github.com/KONGBAI-8080/ReLo-IRR.

</details>


### [75] [Incentivizing Vision Language Models to Search for Long Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02959)
*Harsh Goel,S P Sharan,Sahil Shah,Minkyu Choi,Joungbin An,Kristen Grauman,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSeek是一个将长视频问答从被动单次感知转变为多轮检索的智能体框架，通过神经符号方法实现可验证奖励的强化学习后训练，在长视频理解基准上将Pass@1提升8%、Pass@4提升15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频问答方法通常采用被动单次感知方式，无法有效在长视频中定位相关上下文；同时强化学习后训练虽然推动了数学、代码等符号领域的推理突破，但缺乏可验证奖励信号，难以应用于长视频理解任务。

Method: 提出VSeek框架：(1) 将LVQA转化为自然语言驱动的多轮检索过程；(2) 采用神经符号方法将复杂用户查询编译为时序逻辑规约，系统分解为原子视觉基元（关键对象、活动及时序关系）的检查清单；(3) 利用这些形式化的基础事件作为密集可验证奖励信号进行强化学习后训练。

Result: 在长视频理解基准上，与基础模型相比，VSeek将Pass@1分数提升高达8%，Pass@4分数提升15%。

Conclusion: 通过显式优化可验证的证据搜索行为，VSeek有效提升了长视频问答的性能，验证了神经符号方法结合强化学习在长视频理解领域的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce VSeek, an agentic framework that transforms long-video question answering (LVQA) from a passive, single-pass perception task into a multi-turn retrieval process. VSeek utilizes a natural language-driven search to identify relevant context within long videos and is post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to jointly formulate targeted search queries and reason over retrieved clips for LVQA. While RL post-training has revolutionized reasoning in symbolic domains such as mathematics and code, its application to long-video understanding remains hindered by a lack of verified rewards. To ensure that the retrieved context is relevant, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic approach that bridges open-ended natural language with discrete visual verification. Specifically, complex user queries are compiled into formal temporal logic specifications for systematically decomposing natural language questions into a definitive checklist of required atomic visual primitives, such as key objects and activities, along with their temporal ordering. These systematically derived grounding events provide the critical feedback signal for RL post-training, enabling dense, verifiable rewards based on the successful retrieval of these specific visual elements rather than relying entirely on outcome-only answer accuracy. By explicitly optimizing for this verifiable evidence-seeking behavior, VSeek improves Pass@1 scores by up to 8% and Pass@4 scores by 15% on long-video understanding benchmarks compared to base models. We open-source our code at https://utaustin-swarmlab.github.io/VSeek.

</details>


### [76] [Overloading Large Vision-Language Models for Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02961)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yangyang Guo,Mohan Kankanhalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"信息过载"的新型越狱攻击方法，通过递归式图像排版布局将大量文本和多维图像攻击组合，指数级增加多模态信息复杂性，从而破坏LVLM的安全对齐。在开源模型上平均攻击成功率（ASR）达88.6%，在商业LVLM上平均ASR达84.0%，超出最佳基线48.7%。实验结果揭示了复杂图像排版组合会加剧跨模态处理并降低模型拒绝响应的确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM越狱攻击依赖简单设计（如短文本、分布外图像），但随着大语言模型骨干和多模态机制的进步，这些攻击的可迁移性大幅下降。作者旨在克服这一局限性，提出一种更具鲁棒性和迁移性的越狱攻击方法。

Method: 提出信息过载方法，包含两部分：1）大量文本攻击与多维图像攻击的组合；2）采用递归式图像排版布局（recursion-based image-typography layouts），将上述组件排列其中，以指数级方式增加多模态信息复杂性，从而放大跨模态处理需求，破坏LVLM的安全对齐机制。

Result: 在开源LVLM上平均ASR达88.6%，在商业LVLM上平均ASR达84.0%，超出最佳基线48.7%。基于开源替代模型优化的提示在不同模型家族间具有良好的迁移性。通过探测受害LVLM中的安全关键信息流，发现复杂图像排版组合会导致跨模态处理加剧，并降低模型生成拒绝响应的确定性。

Conclusion: 信息过载是一种实际且新兴的LVLM安全风险，对现实部署构成威胁，亟需针对复杂多模态越狱输入设计更强的防御机制。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) exhibit remarkable vision-language capabilities and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications such as personal assistants, document analysis systems, and embodied agents. However, their dual-modal attack surfaces make them vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing LVLM jailbreaks rely on simple designs, e.g., short text and out-of-distribution images. Nevertheless, recent advancements in both large language model backbones and multimodal mechanisms undermine these attacks, particularly their transferability among model architectures. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel information overloading method that is equipped with both extensive text and multi-dimensional image attacks. These components are arranged in recursion-based image-typography layouts to exponentially increase multimodal information complexity. This overloading approach amplifies the cross-modal processing required, which undermines the safety alignment in LVLMs. Extensive experiments on both open-sourced and commercial LVLMs establish our method as a new state-of-the-art LVLM jailbreak attack. On open-source models, our method achieves an average ASR of 88.6%; on commercial LVLMs, it reaches an average ASR of 84.0%, exceeding the best baseline by 48.7%. Moreover, our prompts optimized on open-source surrogate models transfer effectively across model families. Beyond empirical results, we probe the safety-critical information flows within victim LVLMs. Our observations reveal that complex image-typography compositions induce intensified cross-modal processing and reduce the model's certainty in generating refusal responses. Together, these findings highlight information overloading as a practical and emerging safety risk for real-world LVLM deployments, underscoring the need for stronger defenses against complex multimodal jailbreak inputs.

</details>


### [77] [Parallelized Autoregressive Decoding for Omni-Modal Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02963)
*Wenzheng Zeng,Siyi Jiao,Chen Gao,Hwee Tou Ng,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了并行化自回归框架PadCaptioner，通过利用跨事件弱依赖关系重构因果依赖图，实现无损并行解码，在密集视频描述任务中同时提升生成效率和时序定位质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频大语言模型在密集视频描述中逐token生成，推理效率极低，且随着视频长度和事件密度增加难以扩展。亟需一种既能保持生成质量又能大幅提升效率的并行化方案。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) 潜在全局规划机制，自动学习事件级结构并生成紧凑token编码全局事件间因果关系，同时自适应聚合音频-视觉语义；(2) 事件因子化并行解码机制，平衡局部关注与全局事件间感知，对弱依赖的跨事件token并行解码，对事件内紧密耦合token保持序列解码。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法在生成效率和时序定位描述性能上均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过重构因果依赖图实现跨事件无损并行解码是提升密集视频描述效率与性能的有效途径，验证了利用事件间弱局部依赖性进行并行生成的理论可行性。

Abstract: Dense video captioning aims to generate temporally grounded descriptions of video events, benefiting both event-level video understanding and generation. In this domain, autoregressive video large language models have emerged as a prevalent paradigm due to their strong generative and cross-modal modeling capacity. However, generating dense captions under the token-by-token paradigm severely limits inference efficiency and hinders scalability as video length and event density increase. In this work, we propose a parallelized autoregressive framework that not only improves generation efficiency but also enhances temporally grounded captioning performance. Our key insight is to exploit the weak local dependencies across temporally distinct events to restructure the causal dependency graph, thereby enabling lossless parallel generation. Specifically, tokens with weak cross-event dependencies can be decoded in parallel, while tightly coupled tokens within each event retain sequential decoding to preserve local semantic coherence. To realize this insight, we introduce two key components for lossless parallel decoding: (1) a latent global planning mechanism that automatically learns the event-level structure and produces compact tokens encoding global inter-event causality while adaptively aggregating event-level audio-visual semantics, guiding subsequent dependency restructuring and parallel decoding; and (2) an event-factorized parallel decoding mechanism that effectively balances local focus with global inter-event awareness. Experiments on various benchmarks demonstrate the clear advantage of our approach in both efficiency and performance in omni-modal event grounding and captioning. Project website: https://github.com/showlab/PadCaptioner.

</details>


### [78] [Awakening Diffusion Transformers: Eliciting Stronger Generation and Understanding via Massive Activation Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02968)
*Chaofan Gan,Zicheng Zhao,Yuanpeng Tu,Xi Chen,Ziran Qin,Tieyuan Chen,Supavadee Aramvith,Mehrtash Harandi,Weiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散Transformer（DiT）中的大规模激活（MA）现象，发现其分布在图像token上但集中在固定特征维度，这些维度与AdaLN残差缩放因子对齐并受去噪时间步调控。基于此，提出EMA框架，通过MA驱动的细节引导（DG）提升生成质量，通过MA调制的表示提取（MREP）增强表示能力，无需额外训练即可同时改善DiT的生成与表征性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型中的大规模激活（MA）现象已被广泛观察，但在扩散Transformer（DiT）中的结构和功能作用尚不清楚。本文旨在系统分析DiT中MA的结构特性及其对生成和理解任务的差异化影响。

Method: 首先分析DiT中MA的空间分布与维度集中特性，揭示其与AdaLN残差缩放因子及去噪时间步的关联。提出EMA框架，包括：（1）MA驱动的细节引导（DG），通过抑制MA维度构建细节缺失的反事实预测，引导采样生成更精细细节；（2）MA调制的表示提取（MREP），利用AdaLN通道级调制降低MA方向主导性，并拼接空间归一化MA图保留结构信息。

Result: 实验表明，EMA框架在无需额外训练的情况下，一致提升了DiT的生成质量（细节更精细）和表示能力（特征判别性更强）。DG支持高效部分前向推理、与无分类器引导的集成以及局部token级细节优化；MREP有效缓解了原始DiT特征因共享高幅度方向而导致的token间过度相似问题。

Conclusion: 大规模激活在DiT中具有明确的结构化特征和功能角色，可作为统一的调制信号来同时优化生成和表示任务。EMA框架提供了一种轻量级、训练免费的改进方案，为理解和使用DiT中的MA现象提供了新视角。

Abstract: Massive Activations (MAs) have been widely observed in Transformer-based models, yet their structure and functional roles in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we systematically analyze MAs in representative DiTs and find that they are spatially distributed across image tokens while concentrated in a small set of fixed feature dimensions. We further show that these dimensions are closely aligned with AdaLN residual scaling factors and are primarily modulated by the denoising timestep rather than text conditions. This structure leads to two task-dependent effects: for generation, MAs are critical for fine-grained detail synthesis while having limited influence on global semantics; for understanding, their shared high-magnitude directions make raw DiT features overly similar across spatial tokens and weaken dense feature discrimination. Based on these findings, we introduce Eliciting Massive Activation (EMA), a training-free framework that leverages Massive Activations (MAs) as a unified modulation signal to improve both generative and representational capabilities of DiTs. For generation, EMA proposes MA-driven Detail G}uidance (DG), which suppresses MA dimensions to construct a detail-deficient counterfactual prediction and guides sampling toward finer visual details. DG further supports efficient partial-forward inference, integration with classifier-free guidance, and token-level Local DG for refining selected image regions. For understanding, EMA introduces MA-modulated REPresentation extraction (MREP), which uses pretrained AdaLN channel-wise modulation to reduce MA directional dominance and concatenates spatially normalized MA maps to preserve useful spatial structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EMA consistently improves both the generation quality and representation capability of DiTs.

</details>


### [79] [RePos: Relative-to-Absolute Output Factorization for Cross-Environment WiFi-Based 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02986)
*Zhangcheng Hou,Tomoaki Ohtsuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RePos，一种将根相对姿态估计与根定位分离的分解框架，用于提升WiFi CSI人体姿态估计的跨环境泛化能力。在MM-Fi协议下，RePos的MPJPE为254.4-296.1 mm，比现有WiFi方法降低10-21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于WiFi CSI的无设备3D人体姿态估计方法在单一环境下表现良好（如RePos-D在Person-in-WiFi-3D上达到86.9 mm MPJPE），但跨环境泛化能力差。这是因为CSI测量不像图像那样具有空间局部对应性，且受多径传播影响严重，导致回归绝对姿态的模型将人体结构与特定环境的位置线索纠缠在一起。

Method: 提出RePos分解框架：(1) 将CSI特征分组为身体部位感知的潜在token，通过骨架引导建模精炼为根相对姿态；(2) 独立的基于幅值的网络通过可微分空间分解模块估计根位置。防止绝对姿态监督影响结构分支，从而学习环境不变姿态表示。

Result: 在严格MM-Fi跨环境协议下，MPJPE为254.4-296.1 mm，比现有WiFi方法降低10-21%。在活动协议、留一环境分割和无泄漏小样本迁移设置下改进一致。分析表明相对姿态表示与位置无关，根定位仍依赖环境。

Conclusion: 将绝对姿态分解为根相对姿态和根定位的因子化方法能有效提升跨环境泛化能力，结构分支与定位分支呈现不同的泛化行为。

Abstract: Device-free 3D human pose estimation using commodity WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) enables privacy-preserving and illumination-robust human sensing, but its deployment is limited by poor cross-environment generalization. Unlike images, CSI measurements do not have a spatially localized correspondence to body parts and are heavily affected by multipath propagation, causing models that regress absolute poses to entangle body structure with environment-specific location cues. Within a single environment this coupling is benign: an end-to-end absolute-pose variant, RePos-D, already achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on Person-in-WiFi-3D (86.9 mm MPJPE, a 3.4% gain over the previous best WiFi method, DT-Pose). Across environments, however, the same model overfits position and suffers significant performance degradation. We therefore propose RePos, a factorized framework that separates root-relative pose estimation from root localization. By preventing absolute-pose supervision from affecting the structure branch, RePos learns environment-invariant pose representations. Specifically, it groups CSI features into body-part-aware latent tokens that skeleton-guided modeling refines into the pose, while a separate amplitude-based network estimates the root position through a differentiable spatial-decomposition module. Under the strict MM-Fi cross-environment protocol, RePos achieves MPJPEs of 254.4-296.1 mm, a 10-21% reduction over existing WiFi-based methods. The improvement remains consistent across activity protocols, leave-one-environment-out splits, and leakage-free few-shot transfer. Analysis of the learned features shows that relative-pose representations remain largely position-agnostic, while root localization retains environment dependence, indicating distinct generalization behavior for structure and localization.

</details>


### [80] [Cross-device Collaborative Test-time Adaptation with Zeroth-order Optimization and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02988)
*Yu Mitsuzumi,Akisato Kimura,Yasuhiro Fujiwara,Hisashi Kashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合零阶优化与模型融合的方法，在跨设备协作测试时自适应框架下，使资源受限设备也能高效执行模型更新，无需反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法依赖反向传播进行模型更新，计算和内存开销大，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。

Method: 将零阶优化（ZOO）与模型融合相结合，在CDC-TTA框架下，资源受限设备仅需前向计算即可更新模型；同时通过模型融合将高维优化问题降维到融合系数的低维优化，并引入预处理策略剪除非影响权重、减少信息冗余。

Result: 在常见图像损坏和风格迁移基准上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功降低了测试时自适应的计算和内存需求，使其适用于混合资源设备场景，且通过零阶优化与模型融合的协同设计克服了高维优化难题。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) mitigates domain shifts by using incoming test data to update a model on the fly. The majority of TTA methods require resource-intensive backpropagation (BP) for model updates, particularly demanding large memory sizes, which makes it infeasible to deploy them on resource-limited devices (e.g., edge devices). To address this issue, we integrate two different techniques, zeroth-order optimization (ZOO) and model merging, under the recently established cross-device collaborative TTA (CDC-TTA) framework, where the system is composed of a mixture of resource-abundant and resource-limited devices, and the model information (e.g., model weights obtained on each device) is shared across the devices. Our method is executable on resource-limited devices by introducing ZOO, which requires only forward processing and bypasses the resource-intensive BP optimization. Concurrently, to mitigate the high-dimensional optimization difficulty caused by the side effect of ZOO, we incorporate model merging of the shared multiple models and set the merge coefficients as the optimization objective, which successfully reduces the optimization dimension. In addition, to enhance the synergistic combination of ZOO and model merging, we propose a unique preprocessing strategy that trims intra-model non-influential weights and reduces the inter-model information redundancy. We empirically confirmed the effectiveness of our method using common corruption and style-transferred image benchmarks.

</details>


### [81] [GuideMe: Multi-Domain Task Guidance and Intervention in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02991)
*Fang Liu,Jinpeng Chen,Ke Xu,Yuhao Liu,Huankang Guan,Xudong Lu,Bo Yang,Gerhard Hancke,Rui Liu,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GuideMe，首个面向流式视频的多领域基准，用于训练和评估多模态大模型在闭环交互任务指导中的能力。通过2458个视频、47775个交互样本及三维评估框架，发现现有MLLM在提供指令方面表现出色，但在识别执行错误和提供纠正反馈方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在离线视频理解上表现优异，但它们能否作为实时程序性教练（即持续监控执行、检测错误、提供纠正指导的闭环交互系统）尚不清楚，缺乏相应的评估基准。

Method: 构建了GuideMe多领域基准（含2458个视频、223.7小时、47775个交互样本），覆盖烹饪、物体操作、日常生活指导和健身等场景；并设计了三维评估框架：时间-语义二分图匹配（序列级对齐）、行为分类（干预时机）、LLM-as-a-Judge（内容质量）。

Result: 现有MLLM在提供指令方面表现优异，但在识别执行错误和提供纠正反馈方面持续失败，存在显著的性能不对称性。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM距离作为实时程序性教练仍有较大差距，尤其在错误检测和纠正反馈环节能力不足，GuideMe为未来研究提供了评估标准和发展方向。

Abstract: While multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at offline video understanding, an interesting question of how far they are from serving as a real-time procedural coach remains unknown. Such a role typically requires an MLLM to continuously monitor the execution, detect mistakes, and provide corrective guidance in a closed-loop interaction. In this paper, we construct GuideMe, the first multi-domain benchmark for streaming video that supports training and evaluation of MLLMs for closed-loop interactive task guidance. It comprises 2,458 videos spanning 223.7 hours across diverse domains (\eg, cooking, object manipulation, daily-life guidance, and fitness), with 47,775 interaction samples covering next-step instructions, completion feedback, error detection, and corrective guidance. To evaluate existing models on GuideMe, we design a three-component assessment framework to measure the capabilities of representative MLLMs, which consists of temporal-semantic bipartite matching for sequence-level alignment, behavioral classification for intervention timing, and LLM-as-a-Judge for content quality. Extensive experiments highlight a critical performance asymmetry: despite excelling at providing instructions, existing MLLMs consistently fail to identify execution errors and respond with corrective feedback. Code and data are released at https://fawnliu.github.io/project/guideme.

</details>


### [82] [VISTA: Auditing Semantic Divergence in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02995)
*Junchi Liao,Jiawen Deng,Fuji Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一种黑盒交叉模型审计方法，通过语义熵和基于分布的散度检测视觉语言模型中的视觉概念条件性发散行为，在受控实验和6个模型、19个主题的大规模审计中成功识别出选择性拒绝等异常模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型可能存在视觉概念条件性发散行为（即对于包含人口统计特征、公司标志或意识形态符号的图像，模型会产生异常均匀且与其他模型不同的输出），而纯文本审计无法检测这类问题，因为视觉概念无法像文本token那样被隔离或替换。

Method: 提出VISTA（Visual Inconsistency Screening Through Analysis）框架，采用黑盒交叉模型审计方式，结合语义熵（semantic entropy）与基于分布的散度（distribution-based divergence）来标记模型特定的异常行为。

Result: 在受控实验中，通过微调在小规模偏见数据集上向三个VLM植入概念条件性立场，VISTA成功检测到这些植入行为。对6个VLM在19个主题上的审计中，VISTA识别出142个高度可疑案例（占1.2%），并发现了先前未报告的发散模式——选择性拒绝（selective refusal），即模型对不同群体的人口统计查询拒绝率在0%到65%之间变化。

Conclusion: VISTA能够有效检测视觉语言模型中的视觉概念条件性发散行为，包括人为植入的偏见和自然存在的选择性拒绝模式，为VLM的跨模型审计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models can exhibit visual concept-conditioned divergence: given images containing demographic features, corporate logos, or ideological symbols, some models produce unusually uniform responses that differ from what peer models say about the same input. These behaviors evade text-only audits because visual concepts cannot be isolated or substituted the way text tokens can. We present VISTA (Visual Inconsistency Screening Through Analysis), a black-box cross-model audit that couples semantic entropy with distribution-based divergence to flag model-specific anomalies. In a controlled study, we implant concept-conditioned stances in three VLMs via fine-tuning on small biased datasets and confirm that VISTA detects them. Auditing six VLMs across 19 topics, VISTA surfaces 142 high-suspicion cases (1.2%) and identifies selective refusal as a previously unreported divergence pattern, where models refuse demographic queries at rates varying from 0 to 65% across groups.

</details>


### [83] [CONFLUX: A Latent Diusion Model for 3D Chest-CT Synthesis with RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02998)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CONFLUX是一种用于胸部CT的3D潜变量扩散模型，结合变分自编码器和整流流变压器，通过结构化放射学元数据条件生成高质量3D医学图像，并采用在线强化学习后训练提升对临床属性的控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控的3D医学图像生成模型需要同时满足高保真度、原生3D结构以及对指定条件的忠实性，现有方法在这三方面存在不足。

Method: 采用3D变分自编码器压缩体积数据，整流流变压器在潜空间生成；通过自适应层归一化对结构化放射学元数据（18种异常发现、性别、年龄、重建核）进行条件化；引入在线强化学习后训练（GRPO）奖励分类器对生成样本的发现恢复可靠性。

Result: 在三平面FID上优于强基线（FID 32.3 vs. MAISI 74.6）；后训练消除了47%的相对于真实扫描可靠性的短差。

Conclusion: CONFLUX实现了高保真、原生3D的可控胸部CT生成，强化学习后训练显著提升了对临床属性的控制可靠性，并开源模型及约20万合成数据集。

Abstract: Controllable generative models of 3D medical images can synthesize volumes with specified clinical attributes, but this demands samples that are simultaneously high-fidelity, natively 3D, and faithful to the requested conditioning. We present CONFLUX, a latent diffusion model for chest computed tomography (CT): a 3D variational autoencoder compresses each volume, and a rectified-flow transformer generates in the latent space. Generation is conditioned on structured radiological metadata (18 abnormality findings, sex, age, and reconstruction kernel) through adaptive layer normalization. The model leads strong volumetric baselines on tri-planar Frechet distance (FID 32.3 vs. 74.6 for MAISI) while exposing direct control over clinical attributes. To strengthen that control we add an online reinforcement-learning post-training stage (group-relative policy optimization) that rewards how reliably a classifier recovers the requested findings from each generated volume. Judged by a separate, independent classifier, post-training removes 47% of the shortfall relative to real-scan reliability. We release the model and a ~200k synthetic chest-CT dataset with conditioning metadata spanning a wide variety of clinical findings.

</details>


### [84] [REAL-OW: Rehearsal-free Open World Object Detection with Low-Rank Adaptation and Dual-Stage Objectness Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03004)
*Huazhong Zhang,Xiaowen Fu,Yang Zhang,Linlin Shen,Jinbao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REAL-OW 提出了一种基于LoRA协作适配器架构的无重放开放世界目标检测框架，通过通用适配器和专用适配器分别处理跨任务通用表示和任务特定知识，并引入双阶段目标建模（DSOM）和校准高斯负对数似然（CG-NLL）距离来解决无重放条件下的表示漂移问题，在检测精度和未知发现方面超越了现有基于重放的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWOD方法依赖样本重放来缓解灾难性遗忘，但在实际应用中存储原始数据存在数据访问限制、数据暴露风险以及显著的内存开销问题。

Method: 1) 基于LoRA的协作适配器架构：骨干网络中的通用适配器（GAs）进行跨任务通用表示的重要性感知优化，解码器中的专用适配器（SAs）为任务特定知识提供正交存储。2) 双阶段目标建模（DSOM）：交替进行特征聚合和边界巩固以稳定目标分布，同时保持已知和未知类别的分离。3) 校准高斯负对数似然（CG-NLL）距离：针对无重放场景中分散的特征分布定制。

Result: 在检测精度和未知发现方面均达到最先进性能，超越了现有的样本重放方法。

Conclusion: REAL-OW为无重放开放世界目标检测建立了新的基线，证明了无需存储原始数据即可实现增量知识解耦和类别增量学习的可行性。

Abstract: Open-World Object Detection (OWOD) requires detectors to identify previously unseen objects as unknown and incrementally incorporate them into the set of known categories, while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Existing frameworks rely heavily on exemplar replay to mitigate catastrophic forgetting, but in some real applications, storing raw data conflicts with data access restrictions and leads to data exposure risks, while incurring significant memory overhead. In this paper, we propose REAL-OW, a novel rehearsal-free framework that decouples incremental knowledge through a collaborative adapter architecture based on Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Specifically, we deploy General Adapters (GAs) in the backbone to enable the significance-aware refinement of cross-task universal representations, while Specific Adapters (SAs) in the decoder provide orthogonal storage for task-specific expertise. To resolve representation drift in objectness modeling under rehearsal-free constraints, we introduce Dual-Stage Objectness Modeling (DSOM), which alternates between feature aggregation and boundary consolidation to stabilize objectness distributions while maintaining the separation between known and unknown categories. Furthermore, DSOM is supported by a Calibrated Gaussian Negative Log-Likelihood (CG-NLL) distance tailored for the dispersed feature distributions inherent in rehearsal-free settings. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that REAL-OW achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing existing exemplar replay methods in both detection precision and unknown discovery. Our approach establishes a new baseline for rehearsal-free OWOD.

</details>


### [85] [PosterHarness: Turning Scientific Poster Generation into an Auditable Instruction-Following Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03006)
*Tianyi Yang,Dawei Fu,Youpeng Wu,Zixun Kou,Linrui Chen,Ruobing Jiang,Zijian Wang,Qiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: POSTERHARNESS是一个可审计的框架，将海报生成重构为可测量的指令遵循任务，采用占位符优先协议分离视觉摘要设计与数据图形生成，实现可量化评估并杜绝虚构科学图形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本丰富的图像模型能够设计海报级布局，但缺乏衡量其是否遵守科学传播契约（可读标签、规定宽高比、尤其是避免生成虚构科学图形）的方法。

Method: 提出POSTERHARNESS框架，采用占位符优先协议：模型负责视觉摘要设计（排版、阅读路径、色彩、背景），但绝不绘制数据图形；每个图形区域必须是空的带标签占位符，由确定性组合器在检测到的坐标处插入真实源论文图形。定义了可量化指标：占位符数量与ID准确性、空白度、宽高比合规性、拒绝合成图形、公开文本卫生、源图形来源，并将失败记录为显式拒绝而非隐藏于看似合理的输出中。

Result: 在12篇论文（6篇高能物理，6篇AI/ML相关）上实例化该框架，发现：（i）反事实探测显示占位符协议使VLM计数的合成图形从34降至0；（ii）失败分类法识别出阻塞性契约：占位符几何、占位符QA、模板批评者、公开文本；（iii）与Paper2Poster相比，POSTERHARNESS产生更高分辨率伪影、更低空白画布比例和更强VLM视觉偏好，确定性基线保留稍多PosterQuiz风格信息且运行更快。

Conclusion: POSTERHARNESS将科学海报生成重构为可审计的指令遵循任务，通过占位符协议实现可测量性，所有伪影、提示、清单和审计脚本均作为可复用评估组件发布。

Abstract: Text-rich image models can now design poster-scale layouts, but we lack ways to measure whether they honor scientific communication contracts: legible labels, prescribed aspect ratios, and -- above all -- abstaining from fabricated scientific figures. We present POSTERHARNESS, an auditable harness reframing poster generation as measurable instruction-following tasks, with a pilot benchmark and failure taxonomy. POSTERHARNESS uses a placeholder-first contract to separate two jobs models otherwise conflate. The model performs visual-summary design: typography, reading path, color, and background -- but never draws data-bearing figures. Every figure region must be an empty labeled placeholder; a deterministic compositor inserts real source-paper figures at detected coordinates. This makes properties measurable: placeholder count and ID accuracy, blankness, aspect-ratio compliance, abstention from synthesized graphics, public-text hygiene, and source-figure provenance -- with failures logged as explicit rejections, not hidden in plausible-looking output. We instantiate the harness on 12 papers (6 HEP, 6 AI/ML-adjacent) and report three findings. (i) A counterfactual probe shows the placeholder contract drives VLM-counted synthesized figures from 34 to 0 across three papers. (ii) A failure taxonomy identifies blocking contracts: placeholder geometry, placeholder QA, template critic, and public text. (iii) Comparison with Paper2Poster shows a trade-off: PosterHarness yields higher-resolution artifacts, lower white-canvas fraction, and stronger VLM visual preference; the deterministic baseline retains slightly more PosterQuiz-style information and runs faster. We report this as regime characterization, not a superiority claim. All artifacts, prompts, manifests, and audit scripts are released as a reusable evaluation component.

</details>


### [86] [HyperVAttention: Efficient Sparse Attention with Spatio-Temporal Clustering for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03012)
*Dongyeun Lee,Amir Zandieh,Vahab Mirrokni,Junmo Kim,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyperVAttention (HVA) 是一种免训练的稀疏注意力框架，通过3D局部窗口聚类、混合聚类策略和硬件感知的聚类合并，解决了视频扩散Transformer中自注意力二次复杂度和聚类开销大、CTA利用率低的问题，在文本到视频生成任务上实现了高达2.13倍的端到端延迟降低，同时提升了生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer (VDiTs) 虽在高质量视频生成上表现优异，但自注意力机制的二次复杂度从根本上限制了生成长视频的能力。现有的基于聚类的稀疏注意力方法受限于两个瓶颈：聚类开销大和聚类导致的不规则块结构导致CTA利用率低。

Method: 1) 3D局部窗口聚类：利用视频token的时空局部性，将质心搜索限制在固定局部邻域内，并基于Triton自定义内核高效实现；2) 混合聚类策略：在锚定步进行全聚类，中间步仅更新子集token，利用去噪步间聚类分配的时域稳定性；3) 硬件感知聚类合并：通过并行凝聚合并最小化CTA对齐的执行成本，利用空闲tile容量提高块密度和近似保真度。

Result: 在文本到视频生成任务上，HVA建立了训练免稀疏注意力新的帕累托前沿，端到端延迟降低高达2.13倍，同时相比现有训练免稀疏注意力基线提升了生成保真度。

Conclusion: HVA通过联合解决聚类开销和CTA利用率两个瓶颈，有效提升了长视频生成中稀疏注意力的效率和质量，为视频扩散模型的加速提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (VDiTs) have demonstrated significant capabilities in high-fidelity video generation. However, their ability to produce long-duration videos is fundamentally constrained by the quadratic complexity of the self-attention mechanism. Recent clustering-based sparse attention methods improve the quality-speed trade-off by grouping semantically similar tokens, but their practical efficiency remains limited by two bottlenecks: substantial clustering overhead and low CTA utilization caused by irregular cluster-induced blocks. We propose HyperVAttention (HVA), a training-free sparse attention framework that addresses both bottlenecks jointly. To reduce clustering overhead, we introduce 3D local-window clustering, which exploits the spatio-temporal locality of video tokens to restrict centroid search to fixed local neighborhoods, and implement it with a custom Triton kernel for efficient execution. We further propose a hybrid clustering strategy that performs full clustering only at anchor steps and updates only subset tokens at intermediate steps, leveraging the temporal stability of cluster assignments across denoising steps. To improve CTA utilization, we present hardware-aware cluster merging that minimizes CTA-aligned execution cost through parallel agglomerative merging, improving block density and approximation fidelity by utilizing idle tile capacity. Together, these components reduce clustering overhead, avoid redundant updates, and better align sparse attention with the fixed tile structure of modern GPU kernels. Experiments on Text-to-Video generation show that HVA establishes a new Pareto frontier for training-free sparse attention in video diffusion, reducing end-to-end latency by up to $2.13\times$ while improving fidelity over existing training-free sparse attention baselines.

</details>


### [87] [MambaLIE: Scene Light Intensity-Boosted Low-Light Image Enhancement with State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03013)
*Wanshu Fan,Xiangyu Li,Cong Wang,Kin-man Lam,Xin Yang,Haiyan Zhang,Dongsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MambaLIE是一种基于状态空间模型（SSM）的低光图像增强方法，通过引入场景光强度和局部增强状态空间模型（LESSM），在保持线性计算复杂度的同时实现长距离依赖建模与局部特征增强，在多个基准上超越了CNN和Transformer方法，适用于资源受限设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费电子设备拍摄的图像常因低光环境而质量下降，现有CNN方法无法建模长距离依赖，Transformer虽能解决但计算成本高，因此需要一种兼顾效率与性能的低光图像增强方法。

Method: 提出MambaLIE方法：1) 引入场景光强度改善光照结构分布，并与低光输入进行门控融合；2) 提出LESSM（局部增强状态空间模型），包含SSM分支（线性时间复杂度下建模长距离依赖）和局部增强分支（增强局部特征表示）。

Result: 在4个合成基准和5个真实世界基准上，MambaLIE在准确性、速度和模型大小方面均优于当前最先进的CNN和Transformer方法。

Conclusion: MambaLIE作为一种基于SSM的低光图像增强方法，在计算效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡，适合在资源受限设备上实际部署。

Abstract: Images captured by consumer electronic devices, such as mobile phones and digital cameras, often suffer from low-light degradation due to sensor limitations and imaging pipelines, which degrades visual quality and affects downstream vision tasks.
  Existing methods based on Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Transformers have dominated current low-light image enhancement (LIE) due to their excellent ability to model hierarchical features.
  However, CNNs operate in local receptive fields that cannot model long-range dependencies, while Transformers overcome this problem but incur substantial computational costs.
  To address these challenges, we propose MambaLIE, a Scene Light Intensity-Boosted Low-Light Image Enhancement method based on a State Space Model (SSM).
  We first introduce scene light intensity to improve the structural distribution of illumination, which is then gated with the low-light input to guide enhancement.
  To better model the illumination while maintaining computational efficiency, we propose the Locally Enhanced State Space Model (LESSM) for efficient light enhancement.
  Our LESSM contains two branches: an SSM branch and a Local Enhanced branch, where the former is used to model the long-range dependencies with linear time complexity, while the latter is used to enhance local feature representations.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that MambaLIE outperforms state-of-the-art CNN-based and Transformer-based LIE methods on four widely used synthetic benchmarks and five publicly available real-world benchmarks in terms of accuracy, speed, and model size, making it suitable for practical deployment on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [88] [$C^3$ASD: Multi-Level Consistency-Driven Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03018)
*Jin Hong,Jisoo Park,Junseok Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出C³ASD框架，通过嵌入级、序列级和预测级三级一致性约束，提升主动说话人检测在多种现实噪声下的鲁棒性，同时保持干净数据性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频融合方法在干净数据上表现良好，但在现实噪声（背景噪音、遮挡、多模态同时退化）下性能显著下降，原因是缺乏显式的一致性约束来促进鲁棒的语义对齐表示，模型容易学习脆弱的模态特定捷径。

Method: 提出C³ASD多级一致性驱动框架：(1) 嵌入级跨模态一致性：对齐说话时的音视频表征；(2) 序列级模态内一致性：通过轨迹感知对比学习分离说话与非说话聚类；(3) 预测级一致性：通过知识蒸馏稳定融合。

Result: 在多种音频、视觉及联合噪声场景下显著提升性能，同时在干净数据上保持有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 显式的多级一致性约束能有效提升主动说话人检测模型在现实噪声下的鲁棒性，为构建更可靠的音视频融合模型提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Active Speaker Detection determines whether a visible person in a video is speaking at each moment. While recent audio-visual fusion methods perform well on clean data, they degrade under real-world corruptions such as background noise, occlusion, or simultaneous modality degradation. We attribute this limitation to the absence of explicit consistency constraints that promote robust, semantically aligned representations across modalities. Without such guidance, models tend to learn fragile modality-specific shortcuts that fail under corrupted conditions. We propose $C^3$ASD, a multi-level consistency-driven framework with three complementary constraints: embedding-level inter-modality consistency aligns audio-visual representations during speech; sequence-level intra-modality consistency separates speaking and non-speaking clusters via track-aware contrastive learning; and prediction-level consistency stabilizes fusion through knowledge distillation. Extensive experiments demonstrate significant improvements under diverse audio, visual and joint corruptions, while maintaining competitive performance on clean data.

</details>


### [89] [OmniDS: Dual-Stream Context Fusion for Omnidirectional Depth from Fisheye Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03038)
*Chaesong Park,Jihyeon Hwang,Muyeol Sung,Jongwoo Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniDS提出了一种迭代深度细化框架，通过动态上下文融合和共识感知的多视图相似性，解决多鱼眼相机深度估计中因宽基线导致的可见性冲突问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多鱼眼相机系统中，宽基线导致不同相机观察到同一物体的不同部分甚至不同面，将特征投影到固定ERP表示会产生歧义匹配，尤其在遮挡边界和薄结构附近。现有方法虽通过降低不可靠视图权重来缓解，但未解决根本矛盾。

Method: 提出OmniDS框架：1）双流编码器（轻量CNN+冻结DINOv3语义先验）；2）通过学习的视图权重和带几何畸变偏置的可变形交叉注意力，在每次细化步骤中将特征重投影到ERP空间；3）多视图共识体积通过分组相关性和特征方差捕捉全局跨相机一致性，并用3D U-Net正则化；4）将双流表示蒸馏到单MobileNet编码器以提高部署效率。

Result: 在OmniThings、OmniHouse和Sunny基准上达到SOTA性能，同时保持有竞争力的推理速度。

Conclusion: 动态上下文融合与共识感知多视图相似性相结合，能够有效解决多鱼眼深度估计中的可见性冲突问题，且可通过知识蒸馏实现高效部署。

Abstract: Omnidirectional depth estimation from multi-fisheye camera rigs is complicated by visibility conflicts: wide baselines cause different cameras to observe different portions, or even different faces, of the same object, so aggregating their features into a unified equirectangular (ERP) representation under fixed projection produces ambiguous matching evidence near occlusion boundaries and thin structures. Although existing methods mitigate this by down-weighting unreliable views, they do not resolve the underlying discrepancy because context formation and cross-view fusion remain tied to rigid fisheye-to-ERP sampling. We present OmniDS, an iterative depth refinement framework that replaces rigid aggregation by combining dynamic context fusion with consensus-aware multi-view similarity. A dual-stream encoder pairs a lightweight CNN for geometric detail with a frozen DINOv3 for semantic priors; their features are reprojected into ERP space at each refinement step via learned view weighting and deformable cross-attention with geometric distortion bias. In parallel, a multi-view consensus volume captures global cross-camera agreement through group-wise correlation and feature variance, regularized by a 3D U-Net. For efficient deployment, we distill the dual-stream representation into a single MobileNet-based encoder. OmniDS achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OmniThings, OmniHouse, and Sunny benchmarks while maintaining competitive inference speed. Project page and codes are available at https://parkchaesong.github.io/omnids.

</details>


### [90] [Natural Language Camera Movement Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03043)
*Yuwen Tan,Joey Huang,Jin Huang,Haoxiang Li,Boqing Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有视觉语言模型在理解自然语言描述的摄像机运动方面存在严重缺陷（如混淆平移与旋转、左右方向、物体运动与摄像机运动），为此建立了摄像机运动理解作为独立研究任务，提出了两级电影摄影分类体系、包含真实与合成视频的原子级基准数据集，并通过定向摄像机运动增强构建大规模多源训练集。微调的VLM-8B在真实和合成视频上分别超越Gemini 3.1 Pro达10%和11%，但与人类表现仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在摄像机运动理解任务上表现不佳，常混淆平移与旋转、左右方向、物体运动与摄像机运动，限制了视频生成模型的训练与评估等应用。

Method: 建立自然语言摄像机运动理解作为独立研究任务；设计两级电影摄影分类体系；构建包含真实和合成视频的大规模原子级基准数据集；通过定向摄像机运动增强技术扩充多源训练集；对VLM-8B进行微调。

Result: 微调后的VLM-8B在真实视频上超越Gemini 3.1 Pro 10%，在合成视频上超越11%。

Conclusion: 尽管微调模型取得显著提升，但与人类表现仍有较大差距，表明该任务仍需进一步研究推进。

Abstract: Understanding camera movement in natural language is critical for training and evaluating video generation models, among other applications. However, we demonstrate that existing vision-language models (VLMs) fail this task in surprising ways, frequently confusing translation with rotation, left with right, and object movement with camera movement. To address these limitations, we establish natural language camera movement understanding as a standalone research task. We introduce a two-level cinematographic taxonomy and an extensive, atomic benchmark featuring both real and synthetic videos. Furthermore, we curate a large-scale, multi-source training set enhanced by targeted camera movement augmentation. Our fine-tuned VLM-8B outperforms Gemini 3.1 Pro by 10% and 11% on our benchmark's real and synthetic videos, respectively. Despite these gains, a significant gap remains relative to human performance, underscoring the need to promote and facilitate future research on natural language camera movement understanding.

</details>


### [91] [CURE: Controllable Unified Image Restoration for Complex Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03044)
*Boseong Kim,Donghyeon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CURE是一个统一框架，通过解耦可调表示学习，在复合退化场景中实现可控图像恢复，支持选择性恢复和联合融合恢复，在复合退化基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合退化中退化因素之间存在复杂且相互依赖的交互作用，即使已知退化类型，由于效果交织且恢复过程中需要选择性控制，准确恢复图像仍然困难。

Method: CURE框架包含四个互补目标：(1)身份嵌入与重建约束确保模型在无需恢复时能复现输入图像；(2)比率控制机制通过用户调节的混合比率融合身份嵌入与特定退化嵌入，实现恢复强度的连续控制；(3)中间损失监督逐步输出，每步仅去除复合退化的单一因素；(4)置换不变损失确保无论退化处理顺序如何，模型都能达到一致的恢复质量。CURE仅修改训练策略，不改变底层网络架构。

Result: 在复合退化基准上达到SOTA性能，能够通过灵活调节嵌入比率实现选择性恢复和联合融合恢复。

Conclusion: CURE通过创新的训练策略实现了对复合退化的可控恢复，且可无缝集成到现有可控恢复模型中，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: The presence of composite degradations poses a significant challenge, since the underlying corruption factors exhibit complex and interdependent interactions. Even when the degradation types are known, accurately restoring the image remains difficult due to the intertwined nature of their effects and the need for selective control during the recovery process. To address this, we introduce CURE, a unified framework that enables controllable restoration in complex degradation settings by learning disentangled and adjustable representations. CURE is driven by four complementary objectives. First, an identity embedding is incorporated, along with a reconstruction constraint, to ensure that the model can reproduce the input image when restoration is unnecessary. Second, the ratio control mechanism blends the identity embedding with degradation-specific embeddings using user-regulated mixing ratios, allowing continuous control over restoration intensity. Third, an intermediate loss is applied to supervise stepwise outputs, each encouraged to tackle the removal of only a single degradation factor within a composite mixture. Finally, a permutation-invariant loss ensures that the model achieves consistent restoration quality regardless of the order in which multiple degradations are addressed. Since CURE modifies only the training strategy and not the underlying network architecture, it can be seamlessly integrated into existing controllable restoration models. Experiments demonstrate that CURE delivers state-of-the-art performance on composite degradation benchmarks, while enabling both selective and jointly fused restoration through flexible modulation of embedding ratios. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/bo-oseng/CURE.

</details>


### [92] [Text-to-Image Generation for Projector-Camera System Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03046)
*Xinyu Chen,Yuqi Li,Jiabao Li,Pinyan Tang,Chong Wang,Aditi Majumder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度神经网络的方法，通过文本提示生成具有丰富空间特征的单一自然图像，用于提高投影仪-相机系统的像素匹配精度，同时保持投影内容的视觉自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影仪-相机配准方法存在两大局限：结构化光模式虽然精度高但效率低且视觉信息不友好；自然图像虽美观但特征分布不足导致精度不够。此外，现有方法难以应对表面纹理和环境亮度变化等干扰因素。

Method: 基于深度神经网络，从文本提示生成兼具真实感和丰富空间特征的单一自然图像；在模拟几何和光度失真的合成数据集上训练网络，预测投影仪与相机图像之间的对应关系。

Result: 该方法在多种投影仪-相机配置下显著提升了配准精度，同时减少了投影内容的视觉干扰。用户研究证实，相比现有方法，本技术在感知自然性和可用性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化自然性与特征丰富性，该方法在不牺牲精度的前提下最小化了视觉干扰，在实际应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Establishing correspondence between projector and camera images in a procam (projector + camera) system is essential for achieving high-resolution pixel matching, referred to as procam registration. The highest accuracy is typically obtained using structured light patterns (e.g., stripes or blobs). However, these methods are often inefficient and lack meaningful information for human viewers. Although some have explored the use of natural images, these often fail to provide a sufficient distribution of features to achieve comparable accuracy. Additionally, existing methods struggle to cope with environmental factors such as surface textures and variations in brightness due to ambient light or changes in camera exposure. To address these limitations, we propose a method based on deep neural networks. Our approach aims to generate a single natural image from text-based prompts that not only appears realistic but also possesses rich spatial features to enhance registration accuracy in procam applications. We have developed a deep neural network trained on a synthesized dataset that simulates potential geometric and photometric distortions encountered in a procam system illuminating a relatively smooth object (see Figure 1). Our trained network predicts the correspondence between projector and camera images, significantly improving registration accuracy across various procam configurations. By jointly considering the naturalness and feature richness of the projector images, our method minimizes visual disruptions in projected content without sacrificing precision. A user study confirms that our technique enhances perceived naturalness and usability compared to existing methods, validating its practical utility in real-world applications.

</details>


### [93] [RIGS-Refiner: Risk-Guided Recursive Refinement in Prediction Space for Colonoscopy Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03058)
*Jiachi Zhang,Zhuoyu Wu,Wenqi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIGS-Refiner，一种轻量级后精炼插件，通过风险引导递归修正预测空间中的局部残差错误，仅增加+519参数和+0.631 GFLOPs，在息肉分割任务中一致提升PraNet和SegFormer-B0的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后精炼方法依赖额外的修正头或多阶段流水线，参数或计算开销大。结肠镜图像中，主机预测整体合理但边界和局部结构存在残差错误，需要轻量的局部修复而非重处理整个掩码。

Method: RIGS-Refiner：提取轻量图像先验和预测线索，通过风险引导递归更新，借助共享递归单元将残差修正写回预测空间。仅增加+519参数和+0.631 GFLOPs。

Result: 在Kvasir-SEG训练，Kvasir、ClinicDB、ColonDB、ETIS上评估，以PraNet和SegFormer-B0为冻结主机，一致取得增益，效率-精度权衡优于代表性后精炼方法。

Conclusion: RIGS-Refiner通过极轻量的递归精炼插件有效修正息肉分割中的局部边界错误，实现高效部署。

Abstract: Post-refinement can improve colonoscopy segmentation after host inference, but many designs still rely on extra correction heads or multi-stage pipelines with non-negligible parameter or computational cost. For polyp segmentation, host predictions are often already reasonable globally, with remaining errors clustered around ambiguous boundaries and difficult local structures. These residual errors matter in colonoscopy images because useful masks need correct lesion coverage and clean contour delineation across subtle mucosal transitions. This setting favors selective local repair in prediction space over reprocessing the entire mask. We therefore propose RIGS-Refiner, a lightweight post-refinement plugin for risk-guided recursive refinement in prediction space. Starting from a frozen host anchor prediction, RIGS-Refiner extracts lightweight image priors and prediction cues, applies risk-guided update, and writes back residual corrections through a shared recursive cell. The module adds only +519 parameters and +0.631 GFLOPs, keeping the refinement path compact for deployment. Experiments use Kvasir-SEG for training and Kvasir, ClinicDB, ColonDB, and ETIS for evaluation under two frozen hosts, namely PraNet and SegFormer-B0. Results show consistent gains on both hosts and a favorable efficiency-accuracy trade-off against representative post-refinement methods. Code is available at https://github.com/tyui99/RIGS-Refiner.

</details>


### [94] [Lightweight Polyp Segmentation via a Gain-Aware Prediction-Space Recursive Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03062)
*Jiachi Zhang,Zhuoyu Wu,Quanjun Wang,Wenhui Ou,Wenqi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的预测空间递归校正控制器，在固定递归预算下直接对骨干网络logits进行校正，无需升级骨干或复杂解码器，即可在保持低部署开销的同时提升息肉分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级息肉分割方法的性能提升常归因于骨干编码器升级、复杂解码器或重度精炼分支，难以判断轻量级校正机制本身是否有效。因此需要一种可隔离评估的、纯粹且轻量的校正方法。

Method: 提出递归控制器，在固定递归预算下直接对骨干logits进行操作：聚合不一致性和不确定性证据、更新跟踪近期校正效用的紧凑状态、施加加性残差logit校正。校正路径保持小型、主机可移植、部署显式。

Result: 在Kvasir-SEG源域上一致提升，在多个轻量骨干上取得稳定改进；与训练侧基线和更重的结构精炼器相比具有竞争力；在三个迁移数据集上获得选择性增益，静态开销极小。

Conclusion: 所提出的轻量递归校正控制器本身有效，可作为独立模块为轻量分割网络带来一致性能提升，同时保持低部署成本。

Abstract: While lightweight polyp segmentation is highly desirable for low-cost deployment, reported performance gains often stem from upgraded backbone encoders, complex decoders, or heavy refinement branches. Consequently, it remains difficult to isolate whether a lightweight correction mechanism is inherently effective on its own. We address this limitation by formulating refinement as a prediction-space recursive correction task, introducing a recursive controller that operates directly on backbone logits. Under a fixed recursion budget, this controller aggregates discrepancy and uncertainty evidence, updates a compact state tracking recent correction utility, and applies additive residual logit corrections. By design, this correction path remains small, host-portable, and deployment-explicit. Utilizing a unified Kvasir-trained protocol, we evaluate our approach across seven lightweight backbones on Kvasir-SEG and three transfer datasets, measuring segmentation accuracy (Dice/IoU) alongside deployment efficiency (parameters, GMACs, and peak memory). The controller yields consistent improvements in the source domain, achieves competitive performance against both training-side baselines and heavier structural refiners on representative hosts, and delivers selective transfer gains with minimal static overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/tyui99/Gain-Aware-Prediction-Space-Recursive-Controller.

</details>


### [95] [PixCon: Clean-Positive Contrastive Learning for Foundation-Model Semi-Supervised Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03068)
*Ebenezer Tarubinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixCon提出一种干净阳性像素对比学习框架（PixCon），通过构造性保证阳性集零污染（ρ_F=0）来提升基于基础模型的半监督语义分割性能，在多个数据集上匹配或超越强基线UniMatch V2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督语义分割（SSSS）长期关注“哪些伪标签值得信任”，并通过越来越精细的置信过滤来回答。但在DINOv2等基础模型骨干出现后，严格阈值已能保留98%干净的伪标签集，因此精度提升的关键不在于过滤机制，而在于如何通过类别结构组织嵌入空间。

Method: 提出PixCon，一个干净阳性像素对比学习框架。核心组件包括：（1）每类内存库，仅存入模型已正确分类的标记像素，从构造上保证阳性集无污染（ρ_F=0）；（2）单分支一致性骨干架构，无推理参数增加且无需库特定阈值。通过一阶分析监督InfoNCE梯度，揭示污染危害机制：假阳性项按ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)比例缩放。

Result: 在Pascal VOC、Cityscapes和ADE20K上，PixCon在计算匹配的单切换协议下匹配或提升了基于DINOv2的强基线UniMatch V2。在Pascal上改进每个1/8种子（每种子约+0.2 mIoU），三种子均值达87.90，与已发表的UniMatch V2-B持平。

Conclusion: 在基础模型（如DINOv2）作为教师的情况下，伪标签污染已降至极低水平（如Pascal上ρ_F=0.018），此时PixCon的ρ_F=0保证主要作为教师弱化时的鲁棒性保障，而精度提升来源于更干净的阳性监督。实验表明，在三个数据集（Pascal VOC、Cityscapes、ADE20K）上，PixCon匹配或提升了强基线UniMatch V2的性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised semantic segmentation (SSSS) has long turned on one question, which pseudo-labels to trust, and answered it with ever more careful confidence filtering. Foundation backbones change the regime: with a DINOv2 teacher a strict threshold already retains a measured 98%-clean pseudo-label set, so the accuracy that remains lives not in the filter but in how the embedding space is structured by class. We propose PixCon, a clean-positive pixel-contrastive framework. PixCon maintains a per-class memory bank that admits only labeled pixels the student already classifies correctly, guaranteeing a contamination-free positive set ($ρ_F=0$) by construction, unlike prior contrastive SSSS banks (ReCo, U$^2$PL) built from confidence-filtered pseudo-labels. It is a single branch over a consistency backbone, adds no inference-time parameters, and needs no bank-specific threshold. A first-order analysis of the supervised-InfoNCE gradient explains why contamination hurts: its false-positive term scales as $ρ_F/(1-ρ_F)$, which we measure (0.018 on Pascal, 0.106 on ADE20K) rather than assume. Across Pascal VOC, Cityscapes, and ADE20K, PixCon matches or improves a strong DINOv2-based UniMatch V2 baseline in a compute-matched one-switch protocol: it improves every Pascal-1/8 seed (a per-seed gain of about +0.2 mIoU) and its three-seed mean reaches 87.90, the published UniMatch V2-B figure. Because contamination is already rare under foundation-model teachers, our analysis indicates the $ρ_F=0$ guarantee acts chiefly as robustness as teachers weaken, while the accuracy gain comes from cleaner positive supervision, making clean-positive contrast a robust, low-cost default for foundation-model SSSS.

</details>


### [96] [SafeGuard: A Multi-Agent Perception-Reasoning Framework for Social-Risk AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03069)
*Wenlin Wu,Sheng Zhou,Peipei Song,Wenhao Wang,Junbin Xiao,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架SafeGuard和基准SafeVid，通过协同取证感知与语义推理弥合AI生成视频检测中的感知-推理鸿沟，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测基准偏向感知伪影，导致模型仅能捕捉低级统计异常而缺乏语义推理能力（感知-推理鸿沟），无法应对需要物理规律、结构连贯性和社会逻辑推理的场景。

Method: 提出SafeGuard多智能体框架，包含层次化感知求解器（提取细粒度取证证据）和自我反思验证器（强制语义推理与物理合理性一致），形成可解释证据链；同时构建SafeVid基准（20K视频，10个社会风险类别）。

Result: 在SafeVid上准确率提升+18.7%，在四个公开基准上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体协作融合取证感知与语义推理是弥合感知-推理鸿沟的有效途径，SafeGuard具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: As video generation paradigms evolve from localized manipulation to full-scene synthesis, AI-generated video detection becomes increasingly challenging, as forgeries exhibit coherent global structure and high perceptual realism. However, existing benchmarks are biased toward perceptual fidelity and primarily evaluate detectors based on perceptual artifacts, providing limited coverage of scenarios that require reasoning about violations of physical laws, structural coherence, or social logic. This dataset bias shapes current approaches and results in a Perception-Reasoning Gap: artifact-centric models capture low-level statistical irregularities yet lack semantic inference, whereas vision-language models perform semantic reasoning but remain insensitive to fine-grained forensic cues. To bridge this gap, we propose SafeGuard, a multi-agent framework that enables collaborative specialization between forensic perception and semantic reasoning. A hierarchical perceptual solver extracts fine-grained forensic evidence, while a self-reflective verifier enforces consistency between semantic inference and physical plausibility, forming an interpretable evidence chain.
