Finally, a large set of objective metrics is systematically evaluated in terms of global prediction accuracy, local agreement in ambiguous quality regions, and robustness across distortion families. The results show that several metrics achieve strong agreement for coding-only stimuli, but their performance consistently drops when view synthesis distortions are included. The analysis further highlights the importance of view-pooling strategy in the design of future light field quality metrics. The work provides a reproducible and standardization-ready framework for fine-grained light field QA, while identifying key limitations of current objective metrics under emerging coding pipelines. The subjectively annotated dataset is publicly available at https://plenodb.jpeg.org/lfqa/objectivecfp.

</details>


### [131] [Flex-Forcing: Towards a Unified Autoregressive and Bidirectional Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03509)
*Xinyin Ma,Julius Berner,Chao Liu,Arash Vahdat,Weili Nie,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flex-Forcing 是一个统一训练和推理框架，通过在时间轴和去噪步骤上定义灵活的分块机制，使视频扩散模型能够同时支持双向生成（全局结构规划）和自回归生成（高效细粒度合成），在多个基准测试中实现了更优的视频质量、长视频稳定性和更快的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法存在两难：双向扩散模型全局一致性和视觉保真度高但推理慢；自回归模型生成高效但缺乏长程一致性且存在曝光偏差。作者希望统一这两种范式，兼顾全局规划与高效生成。

Method: 提出 Flex-Forcing 框架，核心是在时间轴和去噪步骤上联合定义灵活分块机制。该机制允许：(1) 根据设备预算灵活分块；(2) 块间双向推理进行全局结构规划，块内自回归生成进行细粒度合成；(3) 实现任意顺序、任意时间步的自回归生成，不受严格因果约束。

Result: 在多个视频生成基准测试上，Flex-Forcing 相比具有固定推理调度的强基线方法，在视频质量和长视频稳定性上取得了一致更好的结果，同时推理速度更快。

Conclusion: Flex-Forcing 成功统一了双向扩散和自回归两种生成范式，打破了现有方法的刚性推理限制，为视频生成提供了一种兼具全局一致性与高效推理的灵活解决方案。

Abstract: Recent progress in large-scale generative models has substantially advanced video generation, yet existing methods remain constrained by a rigid inference paradigm. Bidirectional diffusion models excel at global coherence and visual fidelity but suffer from slow inference, while autoregressive models offer efficient and streaming generation at the cost of long-range consistency and exposure bias. We introduce Flex-Forcing, a unified training and inference framework that enables a video diffusion model to seamlessly operate under both bidirectional and autoregressive generation regimes. The core idea is a flexible chunking mechanism jointly defined over the temporal axis and denoising steps. This design allows the model to (1) perform flexible chunking according to different device budgets, (2) perform bidirectional inference across chunks for global structure planning, while generating frames autoregressively within each chunk for efficient and fine-grained synthesis, and (3) perform any-order, any-timestep autoregressive generation without the strict causal constraint. Extensive experiments on multiple video generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flex-Forcing achieves consistently better video quality, long-video stability than strong baselines with a rigid inference schedule, while offering faster inference.

</details>


### [132] [Perceptual Flow Matching for Few-Step Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03524)
*Chuyang Zhao,Yifei Song,Hongfa Wang,Jianlong Yuan,Yuan Zhang,Siming Fu,Zhineng Chen,Huilin Deng,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PFM通过在感知特征空间而非传统VAE隐空间中进行流匹配监督，将采样步数从35-50步降至4-8步，无需教师模型或辅助网络，在图像/视频生成和编辑任务上均取得高质量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型需要大量采样步数（35-50步）才能生成高质量结果，虽然存在加速和蒸馏方法但需要教师模型或辅助评分网络，增加了训练复杂度和计算成本。本文旨在探索一种简单有效的流匹配模型少步生成框架。

Method: 使用预训练感知模型提取特征空间，在该感知特征空间中执行流匹配的velocity回归，替代传统的VAE隐空间回归。PFM无需教师模型或辅助网络，可直接集成到标准流匹配训练管线中。

Result: 采样步数从35-50步降至4-8步，生成质量保持。在图像生成、视频生成和图像编辑任务上均优于现有蒸馏方法，产生更少的伪影。感知监督使回归从均值寻求转向模式寻求，偏向于流形上的模式。

Conclusion: 标准流匹配训练在合适表征空间监督下可自然产生高质量少步生成器，无需复杂的蒸馏或加速框架。这一发现为高效生成建模中的表征感知目标函数设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose Perceptual Flow Matching (PFM), a simple yet effective framework for few-step generation in flow-matching models. Rather than performing velocity regression in the conventional VAE latent space, PFM supervises flow matching in a perceptual feature space using pretrained perceptual models. This simple change substantially improves the few-step generation capability of flow-matching models, reducing the number of sampling steps from 35-50 to 4-8 while preserving generation quality. Unlike existing acceleration and distillation approaches, PFM requires neither teacher models nor auxiliary score networks and can be integrated into standard flow-matching training pipelines with minimal modifications. Extensive experiments on image generation, video generation, and image editing tasks demonstrate that PFM consistently produces high-quality results while producing fewer artifacts than existing distillation-based methods. We further show that perceptual supervision shifts the regression minimizer from mean-seeking to mode-seeking, biasing predictions toward on-manifold modes that remain accurate under coarse few-step integration. Our results reveal that standard flow-matching training can naturally yield high-quality few-step generators when supervised in an appropriate representation space. We hope this insight inspires future research into representation-aware objectives for efficient generative modeling.

</details>


### [133] [iVISION-2DCD: A Long-Term Change Detection Dataset for Large-Scale Outdoor Construction Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03553)
*Dayou Mao,Yuchen Lin,Ashkan Ebadi,John Zelek,Alexander Wong,Yuhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对建筑工地2D变化检测缺乏开放基准数据集的问题，提出了iVISION-2DCD——一个从密集LiDAR点云合成的大规模数据集，包含逼真的输入图像和精确的地面真值标注，并开发了新颖的视图合成技术和半自动语义分割方法，为计算机视觉和机器人社区提出了新的研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化中需要从任意相机视角检测施工进度变化，但现有研究缺乏2D变化检测与建筑自动化交叉领域的开源基准数据集，且在实际工地使用无人机采集数据存在安全隐患和视角受限问题。

Method: 基于密集LiDAR点云合成大规模数据集，开发新颖的视图合成技术以克服双时相对齐和视角多样性挑战，实施半自动语义分割与变化标签生成，同时保留具有挑战性的真实场景案例。

Result: 构建了iVISION-2DCD数据集，并利用最先进的2DCD算法进行基准评估，证明了该数据集为计算机视觉和机器人社区带来了新的研究挑战。

Conclusion: iVISION-2DCD填补了建筑工地视点鲁棒2D变化检测领域的数据空白，其合成数据生成方法论可推广至其他类似场景的基准数据集构建。

Abstract: Automation in construction is essential for reducing costs and human errors in large-scale projects. We approach the construction progress monitoring from the aspect of detecting changes in construction sites. As construction buildings continue to evolve in geometry and appearance over time, change detection need to be performed from arbitrary camera viewpoints. This necessitates developing 2D Change Detection (2DCD) algorithms that operate robustly across diverse camera perspectives at construction sites. While developing and evaluating such systems is data-intensive, no open-source benchmark dataset exists at the intersection of 2D change detection and construction automation research. Data collection using Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) is gaining its popularity in outdoor large-scale surveying. However, in active construction sites conducting drone missions equipped with high-end sensors imposes safety concerns. Flight trajectory and collected camera viewpoints can be significantly limited. To address this critical gap, we introduce iVISION-2DCD, a large-scale synthetically generated dataset from dense LiDAR point clouds with photorealistic input images and accurate ground truth annotations. Our dataset formally defines the problem of viewpoint-robust 2DCD at construction sites and captures the inherent complexities of real-world deployment. In this paper, we present our systematic methodology for synthetic data generation, developing novel view synthesis techniques to overcome bi-temporal alignment and viewpoint diversity challenges, and implementing semi-automated semantic segmentation with change label generation while preserving challenging real-world cases. Benchmark evaluations using state-of-the-art 2DCD algorithms demonstrate that iVISION-2DCD poses novel research challenges for the computer vision and robotics communities.

</details>


### [134] [Latent Clarity: Bridging World-Model Kinematics to Semantic Manifolds for Video Anomaly Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03558)
*Abu Anas Ibn Samad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PULS提出了一种预测性统一潜在空间管道，包含KSD桥（4.9亿参数）和预期状态预测器ASP（1680万参数），通过将视频运动特征映射到文本对齐的超球面，在不使用MIL或层次融合的情况下，在UCF-Crime上达到0.8994的AUROC，在XD-Violence上达到0.8162。ASP通过预期未来潜在表示，在零样本VQA中达到44.5%准确率（比观察基线高+9.6pp），并通过Latent Clarity Hypothesis验证了预期表示比观察表示更具语义可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测方法主要依赖于反应式多实例学习（MIL），将时空特征压缩为标量分数，缺乏对视频语义的连续预测建模能力。

Method: 提出PULS管道：(1) KSD桥将V-JEPA 2物理张量映射到Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B的2048维文本对齐超球面；(2) ASP预期状态预测器对未来潜在表示进行锐化；(3) 引入Latent Clarity Hypothesis，利用JEPA时间预测器丢弃像素噪声而保留运动学信息，使预期表示比观察表示更可分离；(4) 三重轨迹提前时间协议配合L1惊奇门控实现时序异常检测。

Result: UCF-Crime上块级AUROC为0.8994，XD-Violence上（分布外）为0.8162；ASP在14类零样本VQA中达到44.5%准确率（+9.6pp）；将ASP应用于观察张量时准确率降至7.3%（随机水平）；T-0.5s时预期优势峰值达+8.9pp（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 验证了Latent Clarity Hypothesis，证明预期与观察占据不同的子流形；牛顿不变的运动学表示能够泛化到分布外数据；预测性世界模型在视频异常检测中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Continuous video anomaly detection is dominated by reactive Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) that collapses spatiotemporal features into scalar scores. We introduce PULS (Predictive Unified Latent Space), a continuous semantic world-model pipeline comprising two modules: a 490M-parameter KSD Bridge (Kinematic-to-Semantic Distillation) and a 16.8M-parameter Anticipatory State Predictor (ASP). The KSD Bridge maps V-JEPA 2 physical tensors into the 2048-d Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B text-aligned hypersphere, trained on a subset of UCF-Crime. This translation alone yields a chunk-level AUROC of 0.8994 for UCF-Crime and 0.8162 for out-of-distribution XD-Violence without MIL or hierarchical fusion.
  We introduce and validate the Latent Clarity Hypothesis: because JEPA's temporal predictor discards aleatoric pixel noise while preserving kinematics, anticipated future representations are more semantically separable than observed presents. The ASP sharpens these anticipated future latents, achieving 44.5% mean 14-way zero-shot VQA accuracy (exceeding observation baseline by +9.6 pp). Applying the ASP to Observation Tensors collapses accuracy to 7.3% (random chance), proving Anticipation and Observation occupy distinct sub-manifolds. A Triple-Track Lead-Time protocol with an L1-surprise gate yields a peak +8.9 pp anticipatory advantage at T-0.5s (p < 0.001, N = 1,000 permutation), separating physical anticipation from static scene priors. Zero-shot transfer to XD-Violence confirms that Newtonian-invariant kinematic representations generalize out-of-distribution.

</details>


### [135] [XPlainVerse: A Million-Scale Benchmark for Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03562)
*Abhijeet Narang,Kartik Kuckreja,Shreya Ghosh,Muhammad Haris Khan,Jianfei Cai,Abhinav Dhall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XPlainVerse是一个百万级的大规模基准数据集，用于联合深度伪造检测与可解释性。它包含来自5个来源的真实图像和12种伪造模型生成的图像，并提出Edit-Check过滤流水线确保操作有效性。提供技术型和简化型两种解释风格，并引入EntityScore和EvidenceScore等新指标评估推理忠实度。人工验证表明数据集质量可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测模型虽能生成自然语言解释，但其推理往往缺乏对视觉伪影的充分依据，降低了可靠性和用户信任。已有基准主要评估分类准确率，忽视了解释是否反映真实操作。这阻碍了可部署、可解释的深度伪造检测系统的发展。

Method: 构建包含100万张图像（5个来源真实图像+12种伪造模型生成图像）的基准数据集XPlainVerse；提出多阶段过滤流水线Edit-Check验证操作是否满足预期编辑；提供技术型和简化型两种互补解释风格；提出EntityScore和EvidenceScore两个新指标，通过检查解释是否正确识别被操纵实体和视觉证据来评估推理忠实度。

Result: 在2,000对解释上进行人工标注，验证了数据集质量与人类判断的一致性。该基准能够将解释质量确立为深度伪造检测的一个可量化维度。

Conclusion: XPlainVerse将建立有依据的解释质量作为深度伪造检测的可衡量维度，支持可信、可解释模型的可扩展研究，推动可部署、可解释的深度伪造检测系统发展。

Abstract: As deepfake detection models increasingly produce natural language explanations, their reasoning often remains weakly grounded in visual artifacts, limiting reliability and user trust. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate classification accuracy, overlooking whether explanations reflect the actual manipulations. This gap hinders progress toward deployable, explainable deepfake detection systems. To this end, we introduce XPlainVerse, a large-scale benchmark designed for joint deepfake detection and human-centered explanation. XPlainVerse comprises one million real and manipulated images, pairing authentic images from five established sources with forgeries generated by twelve off-the-shelf image editing and synthesis models. We further propose a multi-stage filtering pipeline, Edit-Check, to verify if manipulations satisfy their intended edits, enabling reliable reasoning supervision at scale. Beyond dataset scale, XPlainVerse provides two complementary explanation styles: technical explanations for expert analysis and simplified explanations optimized for non-technical users. To evaluate explanation quality beyond surface similarity, we propose novel metrics, EntityScore and EvidenceScore, that measure reasoning fidelity by checking whether explanations correctly identify manipulated entities and visual evidence. Human annotations on 2,000 explanation pairs validate our dataset quality against human judgment. We believe XPlainVerse will establish grounded explanation quality as a measurable dimension of deepfake detection and support scalable research on trustworthy, interpretable models.

</details>


### [136] [EPRA U-Net: An Efficient Pyramid Residual Attention Framework for Accurate Infarct Segmentation in Diffusion-Weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03568)
*Hasan Ulutas,Muhammet Emin Sahin,Mustafa Fatih Erkoc,Esra Yuce,Turker Tuncer,Sengul Dogan,Serkan Kiranyaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EPRA U-Net，一种融合EfficientNet编码器、残差-循环模块、空洞空间金字塔池化和双重注意力机制的网络，用于DWI图像中急性缺血性梗死的高效准确分割，在167名患者的4895张切片上取得了优于UNet++、DeepLabV3+和TransUNet的性能，Dice达0.8984，漏检率分别降低16%、25%和29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWI图像上急性缺血性梗死的准确识别是可靠病灶量化和有效临床决策支持的关键前提，现有方法在分割精度和效率上仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出EPRA U-Net架构：1) 基于EfficientNet的编码器作为分层特征提取器，最小化参数量；2) 集成残差-循环模块(R2 block, t=2)和空洞空间金字塔池化(ASPP)增强空间依赖建模；3) 引入双重注意力机制突出病灶激活、抑制无关背景响应；4) 采用Tversky损失函数优化，优先提升检测敏感性而非特异性。

Result: 在167名患者、4895张DWI切片数据集上，EPRA U-Net取得像素级Dice 0.8984、样本级Dice 0.9469、IoU 0.8155、Recall 0.8887、病灶F1 0.9378、HD95 11.62 px；相比UNet++、DeepLabV3+和TransUNet，漏检率分别降低16%、25%和29%。

Conclusion: EPRA U-Net在DWI梗死分割任务上优于当前主流方法，在降低漏检率方面具有显著临床意义，可作为可靠的病灶量化工具支持脑血管事件的临床决策。

Abstract: Objective: Accurate identification of acute ischemic infarcts on diffusion-weighted magnetic resonance imaging (DWI) is a critical prerequisite for reliable lesion quantification and effective clinical decision support in the management of cerebrovascular events. Methods: This study presents EPRA U-Net (Efficient Pyramid Residual Attention U-Net), a task-specific integrated architecture for efficient and accurate infarct segmentation of DWI images. In the proposed architecture, an EfficientNet-based encoder was used as a hierarchical feature extractor with a minimized parameterization. In addition, a Residual-Recurrent (R2) block (recurrent unrolling step t = 2, following the original formulation) and Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling (ASPP) were integrated to enhance the performance of spatial dependency modeling. Additionally, a dual attention mechanism was incorporated to highlight lesion-related activations while concurrently enabling the suppression of extraneous background responses. To prioritize lesion detection consistent with clinical imperative, a Tversky loss function was adopted, emphasizing the sensitivity of detection over its specificity during the optimization process. Results: Experimental evaluations were conducted utilizing an in-house dataset comprising 167 patients with 4,895 DWI slices; subsequently, the performance of the proposed EPRA U-Net was assessed in comparison with state-of-the-art models, specifically UNet++, DeepLabV3+, and TransUNet. The experimental results suggest that EPRA U-Net attained superior performance, evidenced by a pixel-aggregated Dice of 0.8984, a per-sample Dice of 0.9469, an IoU of 0.8155, a Recall of 0.8887, a Lesion F1 of 0.9378, and an HD95 of 11.62 px. Furthermore, a clear reduction in the rate of missed lesions, specifically by 16%, 25%, and 29%, was observed when compared with UNet++, DeepLabV3+, and TransUNet, respectively.

</details>


### [137] [PLGSA-Transformer: Periocular Landmark-Guided Attention with Occlusion-Adaptive Cosine Thresholding for Cross-Modal Masked and Unmasked Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03581)
*Dana A Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PLGSA-Transformer框架，通过眼周地标引导的空间注意力、混合CNN-Transformer分支和遮挡自适应余弦阈值，在遮挡面部识别任务上达到97.22%的配对验证准确率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口罩的广泛使用使传统人脸识别系统在面部遮挡场景下性能严重下降，现有基于固定余弦阈值、非自适应CNN和纯数据驱动特征的方法无法泛化至真实遮挡环境，存在实验室性能与实际部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：1) PLGSA模块，利用MediaPipe眼周地标生成高斯热力图，通过可学习残差门与EfficientNetB3特征融合，引导注意力聚焦于可见判别区域；2) 混合CNN-Transformer分支，将特征图重塑为token后经两层多头自注意力编码器处理，建模跨区域依赖关系；3) OACT遮挡自适应余弦阈值头，根据预测遮挡严重程度动态调整匹配阈值。

Result: 在858张图像（60% Zenodo MDMFR、25% Kaggle CelebA-HQ、15%自采集）上评估，配对验证准确率97.22%，ROC AUC达1.0000，显著优于VGG-16 MUFM（95.0%）、HOG分类器（85.0%）和特征结构度量（86.61%）。

Conclusion: 将眼周几何结构编码为注意力机制，结合Transformer建模与遮挡自适应阈值，可构建鲁棒且可扩展的跨模态口罩人脸识别解决方案。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of facial masks, accelerated by COVID-19 and mandated in security-sensitive settings, has exposed limitations of conventional face recognition systems. Existing approaches relying on fixed cosine thresholds, non-adaptive CNNs, and purely data-driven features fail to generalize when facial regions are occluded, creating a gap between lab performance and real-world deployability. This paper proposes PLGSA-Transformer, a cross-modal face matching framework with three contributions. First, Periocular Landmark-Guided Spatial Attention (PLGSA) uses MediaPipe landmarks to compute Gaussian heatmaps over the eye, brow, and forehead regions, fusing them with EfficientNetB3 features via a learnable residual gate to direct attention toward discriminative visible regions. Second, a Hybrid CNN-Transformer Branch reshapes feature maps into tokens processed by a two-layer Multi-Head Self-Attention encoder, enabling cross-regional dependency modelling. Third, the Occlusion-Adaptive Cosine Threshold (OACT) is a jointly trained head that raises the matching threshold in proportion to predicted occlusion severity. The model is evaluated on 858 images from Zenodo MDMFR (60%), Kaggle CelebA-HQ masked collection (25%), and author-collected images (15%), spanning both genders, ages 21-75, with varied mask types, trained via a unified loss combining contrastive verification, identity classification, and occlusion cross-entropy. PLGSA-Transformer achieves 97.22% pair verification accuracy with ROC AUC 1.0000, surpassing VGG-16-based MUFM (Abdullah et al., 2025; 95.0%), HOG classifiers (Adnan et al., 2020; 85.0%), and Feature-based Structural Measure (Shnain et al., 2017; 86.61%). These results confirm that encoding periocular geometry into attention, with Transformer modelling and occlusion-adaptive thresholds, yields a robust, scalable solution for cross-modal masked face recognition.

</details>


### [138] [Vision Non-Causal Trapezoidal Mamba: Eliminating Directional Scanning in Vision SSMs with Second-Order Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03589)
*Anvitha Ramachandran,Dhruv Parikh,Haoyang Fan,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VNCT提出了一种二阶非因果状态空间模型，消除了视觉SSM中方向性扫描的需要，在实现低推理延迟的同时，通过单次所有图像令牌交互，提升了方向鲁棒性和边界分割性能，在ImageNet、COCO和ADE20K上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉状态空间模型（SSM）继承自因果序列建模的方向性令牌扫描，导致空间偏差和方向敏感表示，限制了模型在视觉任务中的鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 提出Vision Non-Causal Trapezoidal Mamba (VNCT)，一种二阶非因果视觉SSM，允许所有图像令牌在单次前向中交互，消除方向性扫描。

Result: 在图像旋转和翻转下性能下降更少；Boundary IoU提升最多3.7个点；在ImageNet-1K分类、COCO检测与分割、ADE20K语义分割上持续优于方向扫描SSM和一阶非因果SSM。

Conclusion: 方向性扫描对于高性能视觉SSM并非必要，二阶非因果状态空间建模提供了一种简单、高效且鲁棒的视觉识别替代方案。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) have emerged as an alternative to Vision Transformers, yet most vision SSMs inherit directional token scanning from causal sequence modeling. While effective for sequential data, directional scanning introduces spatial bias and orientation-sensitive representations. We present Vision Non-Causal Trapezoidal Mamba (VNCT), a second-order non-causal vision SSM that enables all image tokens to interact in a single pass, eliminating direSctional scanning and achieving low single-image inference latency. VNCT exhibits more orientation-robust representations, showing reduced performance degradation under image rotations and flips, while improving Boundary IoU by up to 3.7 points, leading to more accurate boundary preservation and object localization. Across ImageNet-1K classification, COCO object detection and instance segmentation, and ADE20K semantic segmentation, VNCT consistently outperforms both directional-scanning vision SSMs and first-order non-causal SSMs. These results show that directional scanning is unnecessary for high-performance vision SSMs and that second-order non-causal state-space modeling offers a simple, efficient, and robust alternative for visual recognition.

</details>


### [139] [Responsibility Distribution Estimation in Ego-View Accident Videos with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03591)
*Ryosei Tamura,Andrew Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向第一视角交通事故视频的责任分配估计新任务，通过构建LLM辅助的标注流程并微调多模态大语言模型，在不同输入设置下建立了初始基准，证明多模态LLM能够有效进行基于约束的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通事故理解研究依赖基础设施摄像头、卫星图像或结构化事故记录，这些数据源部署和维护成本高，且无法客观反映驾驶员在事故前的实际观察情况。第一视角事故视频能直接代表驾驶员视觉视角，适合推理事故的可避免性和驾驶员责任，因此需要开发该方向的分析方法。

Method: 构建了LLM辅助的责任标注流程，对第一视角事故视频进行责任比例标注；微调多模态大语言模型，在多种输入设置下（原始帧、分割增强输入、文本描述）进行实验。

Result: 实验建立了强初始基准，证明多模态大语言模型能有效执行这一细粒度、基于约束的推理任务。

Conclusion: 第一视角事故视频为超越传统事故分类和解释任务、实现具有社会和法律意义的多模态推理提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Recent studies on multimodal traffic accident understanding have mainly relied on infrastructure-camera footage, satellite imagery, or structured crash records. However, such data sources are costly to deploy and maintain at large scale, and they cannot objectively capture what the driver was actually able to observe before the accident. In contrast, ego-view accident videos directly represent the driver's visual perspective, making them suitable for reasoning about avoidability and driver responsibility. In this paper, we introduce responsibility distribution estimation for ego-view traffic accident videos, a new task in which a model predicts the percentage of responsibility assigned to each involved agent. We construct an LLM-assisted responsibility annotation pipeline and fine-tune multimodal large language models under multiple input settings, including raw frames, segmentation-enhanced input, and textual descriptions. Experimental results establish a strong initial benchmark, demonstrating that multimodal LLMs can effectively perform this nuanced, constraint-based reasoning task. Our findings suggest that ego-centric accident videos provide a promising foundation for socially and legally meaningful multimodal reasoning beyond conventional accident classification and explanation tasks.

</details>


### [140] [Token-Based Affordance Grounding with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03595)
*Seung Il Lee,Qinqian Lei,Daguang Xu,Dong Yang,Robby T. Tan,Yixin Chen,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TokAG，一种利用大视觉语言模型(LVLM)中token级别语义-空间信号的零样本affordance grounding框架，通过空间感知的token选择机制将LVLM的注意力图转化为零样本affordance热力图，无需外部监督即可定位与动作相关的图像区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督方法在视觉模糊的exocentric图像上表现不佳，且难以区分语义相似的动作，因为仅依赖简短动作短语缺乏丰富语义细节。LVLM虽隐含动作语义和空间线索，但无法直接提供动作特定的空间定位。

Method: 提出TokAG框架，包含空间感知的token选择机制，系统评估每个输出token的注意力图，选取对目标物体呈现主导激活的token，将其注意力图转化为零样本affordance热力图。

Result: 在AGD20K的unseen分割上NSS提升10.7%，在HICO-IIF上提升29.7%，一致优于先前的弱监督方法。

Conclusion: 零样本框架TokAG通过利用LVLM的token级语义-空间信号有效实现了动作相关区域的定位，无需外部监督，性能显著优于弱监督方法。

Abstract: Affordance grounding aims to localize image regions that support a specific action, serving as a core capability for physical intelligence and embodied perception. Previous studies have primarily relied on weakly supervised learning with action labels from exocentric images. However, these methods often struggle with visually ambiguous exocentric images containing co-occurring actions; moreover, they fail to distinguish semantically similar actions because existing methods typically rely on brief action phrases that lack rich semantic details for action-specific localization. Although large vision-language models (LVLMs) encode rich action semantics and their action-conditioned textual outputs implicitly contain spatial cues, they do not directly provide action-specific spatial localization. To address these problems, we propose TokAG, a zero-shot affordance grounding framework that exploits the token-level semantic-spatial signals in LVLMs to localize action-relevant regions without external supervision. We observe that attention maps associated with different LVLM output tokens vary significantly, with many attending to irrelevant regions such as the background. Thus, we introduce a spatial-aware token-selection mechanism to systematically evaluate each output token and select the one whose attention maps exhibit dominant activation over the target object, instead of relying on arbitrary attention maps. By extracting these object-focused attention maps, we transform the LVLM's implicit semantic signals into zero-shot affordance heatmaps. Our zero-shot framework consistently outperforms prior weakly supervised approaches across multiple benchmarks, improving NSS by 10.7% on the unseen split of AGD20K and by 29.7% on HICO-IIF. The code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [141] [A Step Towards Robust Unsupervised Domain Adaptation via Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03600)
*Sushant Dagaji Desale,Rahul Mishra,Ashutosh Kumar Sinha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFT+RL，一种两阶段鲁棒无监督域适应（UDA）框架，结合监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL），在CLIP视觉编码器上通过PGD对抗微调、置信度引导的伪标签策略及混合对抗训练，在三个基准数据集上平均提升10.2%的干净准确率和15.8%的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督域适应中的对抗鲁棒性面临两大挑战：噪声伪标签和源域与对抗扰动目标域之间的分布偏移。现有方法难以平衡鲁棒性与准确性，因为域适应模型生成的伪标签在对抗攻击下易引入分类错误。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) SFT阶段：基于CLIP预训练视觉编码器，部分解冻投影层，在标注源域上使用PGD对抗扰动进行线性分类器的对抗微调；(2) RL阶段：采用置信度引导的伪标签策略，通过衰减置信度阈值逐步过滤未标注目标样本，结合干净源样本和高置信度目标样本构建复合数据集，并对干净与对抗样本的混合批次进行对抗训练。

Result: 在OfficeHome、PACS和VisDA三个基准数据集上，SFT+RL平均干净准确率提升10.2%，对抗鲁棒性提升15.8%，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的SFT+RL框架通过两阶段设计有效解决了UDA中的对抗鲁棒性问题，兼顾了分布适应与对抗防御，实验证明了其显著优势。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness in Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) remains a significant challenge due to noisy pseudo labels and inherent distributional shifts between the clean source and adversarially perturbed target domains. Existing approaches often fail to achieve an optimal trade-off between robustness and accuracy, as pseudo-labels generated by domain-adapted models tend to introduce classification errors under adversarial attacks. In this work, we propose \textbf{SFT+RL}, a two-stage robust UDA framework that integrates Supervised Fine Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) on top of CLIP's pre-trained visual encoder. In the SFT stage, we adversarially fine-tune a linear classifier using PGD-based perturbations over the labelled source domain while partially unfreezing CLIP's projection layer. It allows adaptation to adversarial noise while preserving CLIP's rich semantic priors. We introduce a confidence-guided pseudo-labeling strategy in the RL stage to annotate unlabeled target samples progressively. Pseudo labels are filtered using a decaying confidence threshold to balance quality and coverage, and the model is trained on a composite dataset formed by combining clean source samples with high-confidence target samples. Adversarial training is applied to mixed batches of clean and adversarial examples to enhance cross-domain robustness. Comprehensive evaluations on three benchmark datasets OfficeHome~\cite{tomm-ude}, PACS~\cite{pacs}, and VisDA~\cite{visda} demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Notably, \textbf{SFT+RL} achieves average improvements of \textbf{10.2\%} in clean accuracy and \textbf{15.8\%} in adversarial robustness across all three datasets, outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [142] [SAF3R: Dynamic Sparse Attention for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03612)
*Jianing Deng,Yuanzhe Li,Jialu Wang,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen,Huanrui Yang,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对前馈3D重建(F3R)Transformer中全局注意力二次复杂度导致的长序列瓶颈问题，通过分析发现注意力模式具有高度异质、动态和极端稀疏的特性，提出了无需训练的动态稀疏注意力框架SAF3R，结合离线头部分析和在线自适应策略，在保持重建质量的同时实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: F3R Transformer在长图像序列上缩放困难，因为跨视图全局注意力的二次复杂度成为主要计算瓶颈。现有方法通过压缩或稀疏注意力提升效率，但未能充分利用全局注意力的固有稀疏性和动态行为。

Method: 首先对多个F3R Transformer的全局注意力模式进行全面分析，发现注意力在层和注意力头之间具有高度异质、动态且极端稀疏的特性。基于此发现，提出SAF3R框架，包含定制的稀疏注意力机制、离线头部分析和高效的在线自适应策略，以匹配输入依赖的注意力行为。

Result: SAF3R在保持相机姿态估计和3D重建质量的同时实现了高稀疏率，相比现有方法在F3R Transformer上获得了显著的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 提出了SAF3R框架，通过训练无关的动态稀疏注意力有效解决了F3R Transformer在长序列上的计算瓶颈问题，验证了注意力模式高度稀疏这一发现的有效性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction (F3R) transformers have recently achieved remarkable success. However, scaling them to long image sequences remains challenging, as the quadratic complexity of cross-view global attention quickly becomes the dominant computational bottleneck. While recent efforts attempt to improve efficiency through compressed or sparse attention, they fail to fully exploit the inherent sparsity and dynamic behavior of global attention. In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of global attention across multiple F3R transformers and reveal that attention patterns are highly heterogeneous, dynamic, and extremely sparse across layers and attention heads. Motivated by these findings, we propose SAF3R, a training-free dynamic sparse attention framework tailored to F3R transformers. SAF3R integrates tailored sparse attention mechanisms with offline head profiling and an efficient online adaptation strategy to match input-dependent attention behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAF3R achieves high sparsity ratios while preserving camera pose estimation and 3D reconstruction quality, translating into substantial end-to-end speedup on F3R transformers compared to existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/jndeng/SAF3R

</details>


### [143] [IDEAL-Bench: Indoor Dataset and Evaluation suite for Analyzing 3D Layout reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03614)
*Yuening Cai,Junwei Zhou,Youran Qu,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDEAL-Bench 是一个用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）从单张图像推断结构化3D布局能力的基准测试，包含10种房间类型的1000个逼真室内场景，并通过5个数值维度和渲染-比较协议进行评分。对15个主流VLM的评估显示，最强模型仅得62.1/100分，所有模型在目标识别与几何回归之间存在显著不对称性，且模型排名与传统QA基准部分脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间问答（QA）范式低估了VLM在整体3D布局推断方面的空间智能，缺乏对从单张图像预测每个可见物体的姿态和范围的结构化评估方法。

Method: 构建了IDEAL-Scenes数据集（1000个可重渲染的Blender室内场景，含10种房间类型），并提出IDEAL-Bench评估套件，通过5个数值维度（位置、旋转、尺寸等）和基于渲染的感知比较协议来量化VLM的结构化3D布局预测能力。

Result: 15个主流VLM在IDEAL-Bench上表现不佳，最佳模型仅62.1/100分；所有模型在物体识别与几何回归能力上存在显著不对称；模型排名与QA基准及原始基元重建基准存在部分差异，中游模型排名变化明显。

Conclusion: IDEAL-Bench揭示了当前VLM在真正空间理解上的不足——它们倾向于描述场景而非测量场景，该基准可作为诊断VLM是否具备真实空间智能（而非语言近似）的标准化工具。

Abstract: Spatial question answering is the dominant paradigm for evaluating spatial intelligence in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but it leaves a complementary axis of spatial competence under-evaluated: holistic 3D layout inference, which predicts every visible object's pose and extent from a single image in a structured form. To this end, we introduce IDEAL-Bench, an evaluation suite that requires VLMs to predict structured 3D layouts on photorealistic indoor scenes across 10 room types, scored along five numerical dimensions and a perceptual render-and-compare protocol. By operating on semantically realistic scenes with full asset substitution under controlled lighting and viewpoint, IDEAL-Bench moves beyond CLEVR-style simple geometric primitives so that any image-space discrepancy reflects spatial reasoning alone. The benchmark is built on IDEAL-Scenes, a procedurally generated dataset of 1,000 re-renderable Blender environments with ground-truth layouts. Evaluating 15 prominent VLMs reveals three findings: the task remains substantially unsolved, with the strongest model reaching only 62.1/100 overall; all models exhibit a sharp asymmetry between object recognition and geometric regression, indicating that current VLMs are trained to describe scenes rather than to measure them; model rankings partially diverge from those on QA-based and primitive-reconstruction benchmarks: top-tier consensus holds, but mid-tier rankings shift substantially. Collectively, these findings establish IDEAL-Bench as a diagnostic suite, targeting the geometric and structural competencies that QA-based evaluation cannot surface, and paving the way towards more rigorous evaluation of spatial intelligence in next-generation VLMs. Together, these findings position IDEAL-Bench as a principled diagnostic for whether future VLMs achieve genuine spatial understanding rather than linguistic approximations of it.

</details>


### [144] [A Vision Based System for Guided and Collaborative Reconstruction of Fragmented Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03621)
*Oliver Krumpek,Diana Leo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合协作机器人（cobot）与AI视觉方法的协同系统，用于文化遗产纸质碎片的实时重建。系统采用真空吸附装置实现碎片精确定位（重复精度0.57mm/8cm²），支持手动与全自动两种操作模式。通过评估多种局部特征匹配方法，最终选择SE2-LoFTR作为最优方案，在旋转、尺度鲁棒性及碎片损伤程度方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化遗产保护中破碎纸质文档的修复是一项精细且耗时的工作，传统人工修复效率低且易对脆弱材料造成二次损伤，亟需一种自动化、高精度且保护原材料的重建系统。

Method: 1）设计真空吸附式cobot末端执行器，实现轻柔精确的碎片拾取与放置；2）提供手动（视觉引导）与全自动两种定位模式；3）应用AI图像分割与定位方法，评估多种局部特征匹配算法（包括SE2-LoFTR）在旋转、尺度鲁棒性及损伤程度下的性能。

Result: 真空吸附装置实现0.57mm的定位重复精度（8cm²碎片）；SE2-LoFTR在损伤及光学畸变档案材料的重建中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 该协同系统有效结合了cobot的精确实操能力与AI的视觉理解能力，为文化遗产纸质文档的自动化修复提供了可行方案，SE2-LoFTR是该场景下特征匹配的首选方法。

Abstract: This paper presents the development and evaluation of a collaborative system for real-time reconstruction of fragmented paper documents in the context of cultural heritage preservation. The developed system includes a collaborative robot, or cobot, that can fully manage the positioning of paper fragments using a specially designed vacuum-based suction attachment. This attachment enables gentle and precise positioning, ensuring the preservation of fragile materials. With this device, we are able to achieve a positioning repeatability of 0.57mm for fragments of 8cm^2. The system offers users the flexibility to choose between manual positioning, with visual guidance, or fully automated positioning performed by the cobot. To further improve the reconstruction process, AI methods for image interpretation, specifically for segmentation and positioning tasks, were applied and evaluated for their applicability to template-based reconstruction of damaged paper fragments. Our investigation provides critical insights into the performance of different local feature matching methods under different document types, taking into account rotation, scale robustness, and the degree of damage to the fragments. With a focus on the reconstruction of damaged and optically altered archival material, SE2-LoFTR, a detector-free local feature matching method, was chosen as the preferred method for the system due to its robust performance in our experiments.

</details>


### [145] [RADIO1D: Elastic Representations for Condensed Vision Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03624)
*Greg Heinrich,Mike Ranzinger,Collin McCarthy,Natan Bagrov,Eugene Khvedchenya,Bryan Catanzaro,Jan Kautz,Andrew Tao,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RADIO1D，通过多教师知识蒸馏和自编码器设计，将图像压缩为可变的1D token序列，挑战了VLM依赖固定patch-based 2D视觉特征的假设，实现更高效灵活的场景理解与多模态基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战VLM必须使用固定patch-based 2D视觉特征的假设，探索视觉编码器在VLM训练中的抽象化与空间连贯性变化，并基于此设计更高效的表示方法。

Method: 1) 分析微调视觉编码器发现表征变得抽象且空间连贯性降低，SigLIP2等模型出现少量专门化全局摘要token；2) 提出RADIO1D，采用多教师知识蒸馏与自编码器设计，将图像压缩为紧凑、可变长度的1D token序列。

Result: RADIO1D的表示具有强层次化摘要能力，单token即可实现准确场景理解，支持改进的组合感知图像检索；在VLM中通过可调token数量提供灵活的精度-效率权衡，以更低计算开销在多样化多模态基准上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 固定patch-based 2D视觉特征并非VLM的必要条件，RADIO1D的1D token序列方法在效率和准确性上均具竞争力，为VLM视觉表示提供了新方向。

Abstract: This paper challenges the assumption that vision-language models (VLMs) require fixed patch-based 2D vision features. Analyzing fine-tuned vision encoders, we find that representations become increasingly abstract and less spatially coherent during VLM training. Notably, models trained with image-text alignment (such as SigLIP2) develop a small number of specialized tokens that effectively summarize global image content. Building on this, we introduce RADIO1D, which compresses images into a compact, variable-length 1D token sequence using multi-teacher knowledge distillation and an autoencoder design. The resulting representations exhibit strong hierarchical summarization, enabling accurate scene understanding - even with a single token - and support improved composition-aware image retrieval. In VLMs, RADIO1D provides flexible accuracy-efficiency tradeoffs through adjustable token counts, delivering competitive performance on diverse multimodal benchmarks with lower computational overhead and better accuracy.

</details>


### [146] [Probing Identity-Specific Motion Signatures: A Controlled Diagnostic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03633)
*Yingtie Lei,Fangxun Liu,Baicheng Wu,Colin Lee,Ziheng Zhang,Junke Yang,Zhiyuan Tao,Xuyan Huang,Shuheng Wang,William Koran,Kyle Park,Elijah H Buckwalter,Cheng-Hsuan Chiang,Tejas Naik,Daniel Yi,Wei-Lun Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过构建专业篮球运动员罚球动作数据集BALLER120，系统诊断了现代视频模型在身份识别中对运动特征与静态外观特征的使用偏好。研究发现，尽管运动特征（如脚步位置、肘部弯曲）具备身份区分性且可学习，但模型在RGB视频中倾向于依赖静态外观（面部、球衣）作为捷径；当通过轮廓或骨架输入抑制外观后，模型会转向利用运动微模式，且识别准确率相当、对表观变化鲁棒性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当身份特异性运动线索明确存在时，现代视频模型在身份识别任务中究竟在多大程度上使用了运动信息，揭示模型是否存在依赖静态外观捷径的偏差。

Method: 1) 构建BALLER120基准数据集：120名职业篮球运动员执行同一罚球动作，减少动作级差异和身份相关的采集偏差；2) 设计系统性诊断实验：对比RGB视频、仅轮廓（silhouette-only）和仅骨架（skeleton-only）三种输入条件下的身份识别性能；3) 进行定性和定量分析：评估准确率、对外观变化的鲁棒性，以及注意力分布。

Result: 1) RGB视频中模型可准确预测身份，但过度依赖面部和球衣区域等静态外观；2) 抑制外观后（轮廓/骨架输入），模型转向利用运动微模式（如脚步位置、肘部弯曲）；3) 外观抑制后的表征尽管视觉信息减少，但识别准确率与RGB相当，且对外观变化鲁棒性更强；4) 注意力分析显示外观抑制模型关注不同个体间有区分性的运动模式。

Conclusion: 身份特异性运动信号确实存在、信息丰富且可学习，但现代视频模型在RGB输入下倾向于利用更容易的静态外观捷径，除非显式抑制外观线索，否则模型会忽略运动信息。

Abstract: Identity recognition (e.g., person, animal re-identification) has traditionally relied heavily on static appearance cues. Yet motion--consistent, individual-specific dynamics--can provide a complementary and potentially more robust signature, especially when appearance is weak or variable. This raises a fundamental question: when identity-specific motion cues are clearly present, to what extent do modern video models use them for recognition? To investigate this question, we conduct a systematic diagnostic study and introduce BALLER120, a controlled benchmark of 120 professional basketball players performing free-throws. By focusing on the same multi-phase action across individuals, BALLER120 reduces action-level variation and identity-correlated acquisition biases, enabling fine-grained analysis of identity-specific kinematic patterns. We find that modern video models can predict identity accurately from RGB videos, but often rely on static appearance cues such as faces and jersey regions, even when informative motion cues are available. Strikingly, when appearance is suppressed through silhouette-only or skeleton-only inputs, the same model architectures shift toward motion micro-patterns (e.g., foot placement and elbow bending). Despite containing less visual information, appearance-suppressed representations achieve competitive accuracy and stronger robustness to appearance shifts. Our qualitative analyses further show that appearance-suppressed models attend to distinctive motion patterns across individuals. Overall, our study demonstrates that identity-specific motion signatures are present, informative, and learnable, but modern video models may overlook them in favor of easier static shortcuts unless appearance cues are explicitly suppressed.

</details>


### [147] [Moonstone: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Benchmark for Lunar Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03644)
*Ayush Prasad,Swarnalee Mazumder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Moonstone是首个月球遥感多模态基础模型基准，包含28通道全球月球预训练数据集、MG-MAE（模态分组掩码自编码器）和六个下游任务基准。MG-MAE在所有任务上显著优于各类基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球轨道任务积累了多模态遥感数据，但这些数据集分散在不同档案中，且缺乏评估机器学习在月球数据上表现的基准。

Method: (1) 构建了28通道、128像素/度（约237米）分辨率的全球月球预训练数据集，涵盖五个任务中七个仪器家族；(2) 提出MG-MAE，包含逐分组卷积分词器、共享Vision Transformer编码器、缺失模态的注意力掩码、异质空间覆盖的自适应掩码以及光谱连续性正则化；(3) 设计六个下游任务（分类、回归、分割）的基准。

Result: MG-MAE预训练特征在所有下游任务上超越从头训练基线，并大幅优于ImageNet预训练和vanilla MAE基线。

Conclusion: Moonstone为月球遥感多模态学习提供了标准化基准，MG-MAE方法有效整合了异质多模态数据，展示了预训练在月球科学中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Decades of orbital missions have produced multi-modal remote sensing data for the Moon, spanning optical imagery, spectroscopy, thermal emission, radar, gravity, and elemental composition. Yet these datasets remain fragmented across archives, and no benchmark exists for evaluating machine learning on lunar data. We introduce Moonstone, the first multi-modal foundation model benchmark for lunar remote sensing. Our contributions are: (1) a 28-channel, 128 pixels-per-degree (~237 m) global lunar pretraining dataset from seven instrument families across five missions, (2) MG-MAE, a modality-grouped masked autoencoder with per-group convolutional tokenizers, a shared Vision Transformer encoder, attention masking for missing modalities, coverage-adaptive masking for heterogeneous spatial coverage, and spectral continuity regularization for physically plausible reconstructions, and (3) a benchmark of six downstream tasks covering classification, regression, and segmentation. MG-MAE pretrained features outperform scratch baselines on all tasks and surpass both ImageNet-pretrained and vanilla MAE baselines by large margins. Data and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ayushprd/Moonstone and https://github.com/ayushprd/Moonstone .

</details>


### [148] [Do Medical Vision Language Models Actually See? A Counterfactual Grounding Framework and Hard-Negative Contrastive Training for Visually-Reliant Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03647)
*Anas Zafar,Leema Krishna Murali,Siddhant Bharadwaj,Ashish Vashist,Jia Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实评估框架来解耦视觉与文本贡献，并引入CORAL方法（基于对比检索增强学习的LoRA微调）来提升医学VLM的视觉 grounding 能力，在四个医学VQA基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLM虽然在问答任务上表现良好，但难以判断其是否真正基于视觉证据推理，还是利用文本捷径（shortcuts）来作答，缺乏解耦视觉与文本贡献的评估方法。

Method: 1) 提出反事实评估框架：用空白、像素打乱、无图像和CLIP检索的硬负样本替换输入图像；2) 定义视觉依赖分数(VRS)和视觉幻觉率(VHR)等指标；3) 提出CORAL：对Qwen2.5-VL-7B进行LoRA微调，使用对比grounding目标(CGO)惩罚在硬负样本图像替换下答案不变的情况。

Result: 在四个闭式医学VQA基准(PathVQA, PMC-VQA, SLAKE, VQA-RAD; n=400)上，CORAL相比Qwen2.5-VL-7B基线宏平均准确率提升+6.7 pp (P(Δ>0)=0.988)，VHR降低8.0 pp (P<0.001)；MedVLThinker的RL变体未取得显著提升。跨领域诊断显示：医学基准上图像替换代价仅≤6.5 pp，而通用领域为48-61 pp，说明CGO所针对的grounding差距。

Conclusion: CORAL有效提升了医学VLM的视觉grounding能力并降低了视觉幻觉；当前医学VLM存在显著的grounding gap，即对视觉证据的依赖不足；研究公开了框架、训练代码和模型权重以支持可复现的医学VLM grounding审计。

Abstract: Large vision language models (VLMs) report strong accuracy on medical question-answering, yet it remains unclear whether they reason from visual evidence or exploit textual shortcuts. We introduce a counterfactual evaluation framework that decouples visual and textual contributions by substituting input images with controlled surrogates blank, pixel-shuffled, image-absent, and CLIP-retrieved hard negatives and derive a suite of grounding metrics including the Visual Reliance Score (VRS) and Visual Hallucination Rate (VHR). We further introduce CORAL (COntrastive Retrieval-Augmented Learning), a 7B-parameter LoRA fine-tune of Qwen2.5-VL-7B trained with a Contrastive Grounding Objective (CGO) that penalises answer invariance under hard-negative image swaps. On a paired controlled evaluation across four closed-form medical VQA benchmarks (PathVQA, PMC-VQA, SLAKE, VQA-RAD; n=400 total), CORAL improves macro accuracy by +6.7 pp (P(Delta>0)=0.988) and reduces VHR by 8.0 pp (P<0.001) over the matched Qwen2.5-VL-7B base; neither MedVLThinker RL variant achieves a significant gain on either metric. Cross-domain diagnostics further reveal that image substitution costs only <=6.5 pp on medical benchmarks versus 48-61 pp on general-domain tasks, situating the grounding gap that CGO targets. We discuss evaluation limitations openly including train/eval benchmark overlap and underpowered secondary metrics and release our framework, training code, and model weights to support reproducible grounding audits of medical VLMs.

</details>


### [149] [ClinOCR-Bench: A Comprehensive Clinical Scanned Document Dataset for Optical Character Recognition Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03650)
*Enshuo Hsu,Jin Zhou,Kirk Roberts*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个面向临床文档OCR评估的公开基准数据集ClinOCR-Bench，包含384张扫描图像和6个子集，覆盖常见扫描伪影，并评估了当前先进视觉语言模型的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床OCR研究多基于私有数据，缺乏公开、系统性的评估基准；同时常见扫描伪影未被现有数据集覆盖，导致无法进行全面评估。

Method: 构建了ClinOCR-Bench数据集，包含6个子集（正常、手写、低质量、旋转、表格、混合伪影），采用模板感知的训练/测试划分，并基于开源和专有视觉语言模型进行基线评估。

Result: 发布了包含384张扫描图像、覆盖6种伪影类型的公开基准数据集，并给出了SOTA VLM模型的基线OCR性能结果。

Conclusion: ClinOCR-Bench填补了临床文档OCR公开基准的空白，为系统性评估VLM在医疗扫描文档上的OCR能力提供了标准化测试集。

Abstract: Extracting textual information from scanned medical documents, such as external laboratory reports and manually filled forms, has been a major challenge in modern electronic health records (EHRs). Recent advancements in vision language models (VLMs) have shown great promise over traditional OCR tools. However, at this point, most clinical OCR studies were conducted on private, institutional data. To our knowledge, there are few publicly available datasets for evaluating OCR models in the clinical domain. Furthermore, common scanning artifacts that undermine OCR performance are not reflected in those datasets, leaving a systematic evaluation unfeasible. Therefore, we release a publicly available, realistic-looking OCR benchmark dataset, ClinOCR-Bench, with 384 scanned images across 6 subsets: Normal, Handwriting, Poor Quality, Rotation, Tables, and Mix-artifacts. ClinOCR-Bench features: 1) diverse document types and layouts, 2) full coverage of common EHR scan artifacts, 3) protected health information-free, 4) template-aware train/test split, and 5) adequate sample size for OCR benchmarking. Baseline OCR performance was evaluated using state-of-the-art open-weight and proprietary VLMs. The dataset and documentation are available on GitHub (https://github.com/ClinOCR-Bench/ClinOCR-Bench).

</details>


### [150] [ViPo-MLLM: Visual-Pose Multimodal LLM for Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03657)
*Ahmed Abul Hasanaath,Bicheng Xu,Mir Rayat Imtiaz Hossain,Leonid Sigal,Hamzah Luqman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViPo-MLLM通过融合时空RGB特征和人体姿态特征，利用交叉注意力机制和结构化提示的大语言模型，在无标注手语翻译任务上取得了SOTA结果，性能接近有标注方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标注手语翻译（Gloss-free SLT）虽然避免了昂贵的标注成本，但需要对双手、身体和面部线索进行细粒度建模。现有方法多使用单模态或弱融合特征，性能受限。

Method: 提出ViPo-MLLM框架：1) 专用编码器分别建模RGB和姿态特征的模态内动态；2) 交叉注意力机制捕获模态间长程依赖；3) 融合表示通过结构化提示输入LLM，结合对比学习和语言建模目标联合训练。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily数据集上取得新SOTA结果，且性能接近基于标注（gloss-based）的识别方法。

Conclusion: 姿态线索和交叉注意力机制在无标注手语翻译中有效，多模态融合策略显著提升了翻译质量。

Abstract: Gloss-free Sign Language Translation (SLT) translates sign language videos into spoken-language sentences without gloss annotations, avoiding costly labeling but requiring fine-grained modeling of hands, body, and facial cues. Existing methods often use single-modality or weakly fused features, limiting performance. We propose ViPo-MLLM, a framework that integrates spatio-temporal RGB and human pose features. Dedicated encoders model intra-modal dynamics and cross-modal attention captures long-range dependencies. The fused representation is conditioned with a structured prompt and processed by an LLM trained with contrastive and language modeling objectives. The proposed model was evaluated on the PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily datasets and achieved new state-of-the-art results on both datasets. Moreover, the ViPo-MLLM model attained competitive performance compared to gloss-based recognition approaches, confirming the effectiveness of the proposed pose cues and cross-modal attention mechanisms.

</details>


### [151] [From Geometric Labels to Semantic Understanding of Indoor Building Components Using Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03661)
*Shuju Jing,Chao Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Building-MLLM是一个面向室内建筑组件的点云多模态大语言模型，通过点云信息增强器、几何保持正则化、固定文本前缀和多维LoRA四个机制，在简单识别、复杂描述和多工程问答三类任务上分别达到88.00%、65.10%和68.14%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点云的室内组件理解方法仅输出离散标签，无法解释组件功能或进行自然语言交互，缺乏具备语言理解能力的多模态模型。

Method: 提出Building-MLLM模型，包含四个核心机制：(1) 点云信息增强器提取任务相关语义；(2) 几何保持正则化防止几何信息丢失；(3) 固定文本前缀稳定领域输出；(4) 多维LoRA平衡识别与推理能力。同时开发多约束渐进式指令生成引擎，构建包含4198个物体、37782条指令对、47类别的合成点云-文本数据集。

Result: 在简单识别、复杂描述和多工程问答三类任务上分别达到88.00%、65.10%和68.14%的准确率，并在其他真实数据集上展现出初步的泛化迁移能力。

Conclusion: Building-MLLM实现了优越的室内组件语言理解能力，为点云在设施运维中的应用提供了具备自然语言交互能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Point cloud-based understanding has become an important enabler for facility operation and maintenance involving indoor building components. However, existing methods output only discrete labels without explaining component functions or natural language interactions. This paper proposes Building-MLLM, a point cloud-centered multimodal large language model (MLLM) for indoor components, which models point clouds and instructions to generate responses across Simple Recognition, Complex Captioning, and Multi-Engineering Question Answering tasks. Building-MLLM addresses semantic concentration through four domain-specific mechanisms: Point Information Enhancer for task-relevant semantics, Geometry-Preserving Regularization preventing geometric erosion, fixed textual prefix for domain stabilization, and multi-dimensional LoRA balancing recognition with reasoning. A multi-constraint progressive instruction-generation engine is developed to compile a synthetic point cloud-text dataset with 4198 objects, 37,782 instruction-following pairs, and 47 categories. Experiments show that Building-MLLM achieves 88.00%, 65.10%, and 68.14% on the three task types, respectively, demonstrating superior indoor component language understanding and providing initial generalizability in transfer inference on other real-world datasets.

</details>


### [152] [IPDiff: Diffusion-driven ORSI Salient Object Detection with Information Reconstruction and Multi-Prior Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03696)
*Gongyang Li,Zhen Bai,Runmin Cong,Dan Zeng,Weisi Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IPDiff，一种基于扩散模型的遥感图像显著目标检测方法，通过信息重构和多先验引导的动态优化策略，在三个公开数据集上超越46种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ORSI-SOD方法采用静态推理策略，测试阶段使用固定模型参数，生成的显著图存在误差也无法进一步优化。

Method: 将ORSI-SOD建模为条件扩散问题，设计先验网络通过信息重构驱动注意力模块提取显著性先验（提供位置信息）和层次先验（提供细节与语义信息），在去噪网络中利用这些先验对随机噪声进行迭代去噪，同时在空间域和频谱域通过混合损失函数进行监督。

Result: 在ORSSD、EORSSD和ORSI-4199三个公开数据集上，IPDiff超越了46种最先进方法，取得了最佳性能。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的动态优化策略能够有效迭代优化显著图，信息重构与多先验引导机制提升了遥感图像显著目标检测的精度。

Abstract: Existing Salient Object Detection in Optical Remote Sensing Image (ORSI-SOD) methods mainly adopt the static inference strategy, which uses fixed trained model parameters for saliency inference in the testing phase. This means that even if the generated saliency map has errors, it cannot be further optimized. In this paper, we propose the novel IPDiff, a Diffusion-driven ORSI-SOD method with Information Reconstruction and Multi-Prior Guidance. We build IPDiff based on a unique dynamic optimization strategy, which endows IPDiff with the ability to iteratively optimize saliency maps with a dynamic parameter. Specifically, we formulate ORSI-SOD as a conditional diffusion problem in IPDiff. IPDiff first extracts informative conditional priors from ORSIs, including the saliency prior and the hierarchical priors, in the prior network with the assistance of the information reconstruction-driven attention module. The saliency prior can provide positional information of salient objects, while the hierarchical priors can provide specific detail and semantic information of salient objects. Under the guidance of these priors, IPDiff then iteratively denoises random noise as the timestep dynamically changes in the denoising network, generating saliency maps that are close to ground truths. Notably, we simultaneously supervise IPDiff in both spatial and spectral domains through a hybrid loss function to achieve efficient network training. Comprehensive experiments on public ORSSD, EORSSD, and ORSI-4199 datasets demonstrate that our proposed IPDiff achieves the best performance compared to 46 state-of-the-art methods. The code and results of our method are available at https://github.com/MathLee/IPDiff.

</details>


### [153] [Leveraging Pathology Co-occurrence for Test-Time Adaptation in Chest X-Ray Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03715)
*Woojin Jeong,Yujin Choi,Dongbin Kim,Soyeon Park,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoWA方法，利用疾病共现模式作为可靠性信号，对多标签医学影像进行测试时自适应，在胸部X光基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法针对自然图像单标签分类设计，对多标签医学影像均匀应用熵最小化，忽略了病理之间具有结构化共现模式这一关键特性，导致领域偏移下性能下降。

Method: CoWA从模型预测中估计标签共现结构，对偏离期望共现模式的样本降低权重，使适应过程更多地依赖一致性强的预测，减少噪声样本的影响。

Result: 在胸部X光基准上，CoWA在领域偏移场景下相较于已有基线方法取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 利用疾病共现模式作为可靠性信号可以有效地指导多标签医学影像的测试时自适应，提高模型在新临床站点部署时的泛化能力。

Abstract: Medical imaging models often degrade when deployed at new clinical sites due to differences in imaging equipment, protocols, and patient populations. Test-time adaptation (TTA) addresses this by updating a pretrained model using only unlabeled target data, without access to source data. However, existing TTA methods were designed for single-label classification on natural image benchmarks, minimizing entropy uniformly across all samples without considering label dependencies. This overlooks a key property of multi-label medical imaging: pathologies do not occur independently but exhibit structured co-occurrence patterns. In this work, we propose Co-occurrence Weighted Adaptation (CoWA), which leverages disease co-occurrence patterns as a reliability signal for adaptation. CoWA estimates label co-occurrence structure from model predictions and downweights samples that deviate from expected patterns, enabling adaptation to rely more on consistent predictions while reducing the impact of noisy ones. We evaluate CoWA on chest X-ray benchmarks under domain shifts and demonstrate consistent improvements over established baselines.

</details>


### [154] [ProxyUp: Training-Free Proxy-Conditioned Video Generation for Controllable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03732)
*Zanwei Zhou,Jiazhong Cen,Jiemin Fang,Yumeng He,Chen Yang,Sikuang Li,Fanpeng Meng,Zhikuan Bao,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProxyUp，一种无需训练的视频生成框架，通过代理视频（来自物理模拟或真实记录）控制前景物体运动，结合区域隐变量加噪和随机流松弛（SFR）技术，在保持动态保真度的同时实现文本对齐的内容生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以通过文本提示精确控制复杂动态和物理合理的细粒度运动与交互。

Method: 提出ProxyUp框架：1) 将代理视频反转为中间隐变量表示；2) 应用区域隐变量加噪，保留运动关键区域的同时向文本驱动重建区域注入噪声；3) 提出随机流松弛（SFR），逐步将组合后的隐变量向模型学习到的分布松弛，缓解分布不匹配和前后景耦合弱的问题。

Result: 在模拟和真实代理视频上的实验表明，ProxyUp在动态保真度和文本对齐方面优于强视频编辑和运动迁移基线方法。

Conclusion: ProxyUp通过代理条件视频生成实现了对复杂动态的精确控制，且无需配对数据训练，具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Precise control over complex dynamics remains challenging for modern video generative models, as text prompts alone often cannot specify physically plausible, fine-grained motion and interactions. We introduce $\textit{proxy-conditioned video generation}$, where a coarse proxy video from physics-based simulation or real-world recording serves as a dynamics carrier to control foreground object motion. Given a proxy video and a text prompt, the goal is to synthesize a new video that preserves the proxy dynamics while generating novel content and plausible interactions aligned with the prompt. Since paired proxy-target videos are difficult to obtain, we propose $\textbf{ProxyUp}$, a training-free framework built on pretrained video generative models. ProxyUp first inverts the proxy video into an intermediate latent representation and applies $\textbf{region-wise latent noising}$, preserving motion-critical proxy latents while injecting noise into regions intended for text-driven regeneration. To mitigate the distribution mismatch and weak foreground-background coupling introduced by this heuristic latent composition, we further propose $\textbf{Stochastic Flow Relaxation (SFR)}$, which progressively relaxes the composed latent toward the model's learned distribution before ODE sampling. Experiments on both simulation and real-world proxies show that ProxyUp outperforms strong video editing and motion transfer baselines in dynamic fidelity and text alignment.

</details>


### [155] [Attending to Multimodal Generation One Token at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03738)
*Varun Gupta,Vineet Gandhi,Makarand Tapaswi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OTaT方法，通过逐token追踪多模态大语言模型在生成过程中的注意力动态，揭示了注意力在不同语义角色（图像、文本、指令、已生成token）间的规律性转移模式，并据此提出测试时干预方法显著提升多模态任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往可解释性研究聚焦于单层和电路（空间维度），忽略了多模态计算在生成过程中token级别的动态变化（时间维度）。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究模型在生成过程中注意力如何根据语义角色在不同模态间切换。

Method: 提出"One Token at a Time (OTaT)"方法，逐token追踪模型对图像、文本、指令和已生成token的注意力。设计需要单次响应内在视觉和文本上下文间显式切换的多模态任务。在两个主流模型家族的四个开源MLLM上进行实验，并通过因果注意力阻断干预验证注意力模式的函数作用。

Result: 发现一致模式：图像注意力在需要图像信息的token处达到峰值；指令token在任务转换期间被重新关注；已生成token的注意力随生成过程增加。因果干预验证了这些趋势的功能作用。基于注意力动态分析，提出测试时干预方法，显著提升多模态任务性能。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多模态大语言模型生成过程中的注意力动态规律，证明了注意力模式与语义角色之间的功能关联，并展示了利用这些规律进行测试时干预以提升性能的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate responses autoregressively, integrating visual and linguistic information in an evolving context. Prior work on interpretability has focused on individual layers and circuits (where), leaving the token-level dynamics of multimodal computation during generation (when) underexplored. We address this gap and study attention shifts as per semantic role; tracking model attention to image, text, instruction, and previously generated tokens, One Token at a Time (OTaT). We introduce multimodal tasks that require explicit switching between visual and textual context within a single response. Across two mainstream model families and four open-weight MLLMs of varying sizes, we establish consistent patterns: attention to image peaks at tokens requiring image-derived information, instruction tokens are revisited during task transitions, and attention to previously generated tokens increases as the generation progresses. Causal attention blocking interventions validate the functional role of these trends. We profile model behavior under disrupted attention and observe responses falling back to language priors, or exhibiting cross-modal leakage, denial, or recovery. Finally, informed of the attention dynamics through our novel analysis, we propose a simple test-time intervention to boost attention to the relevant modality at the right time, significantly improving multimodal task performance.

</details>


### [156] [Exploring SAM Supervision for Fine-Grained UAV Target Segmentation under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03754)
*Le-Anh Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SAM3作为伪标签生成器来训练轻量级UAV目标分割网络IPS-Seg，通过两阶段粗到细的伪标签生成策略，在保持计算效率的同时提升分割精度，验证了大型基础模型作为低标注视觉领域注释源的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV目标分割面临目标尺寸小、外观变化大、背景杂乱以及密集标注数据匮乏等挑战，这些问题限制了轻量级分割模型在真实UAV应用中的性能与部署。

Method: 提出两阶段SAM3引导的伪标签生成框架：第一阶段SAM3生成粗粒度掩码进行初始目标定位；第二阶段对裁剪的局部图像块再次应用SAM3生成细粒度掩码以改善边界质量并消除假阳性。基于此训练轻量级IPS-Seg网络，包含IdentityFormer骨干、ASPP模块和PixelShuffle解码器。

Result: 在多种监督设置下的实验表明，IPS-Seg在分割精度与计算效率之间取得了良好权衡，且所提伪标签生成策略能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 大型基础模型可作为有效的注释源来训练轻量级任务特定分割网络，在低标注视觉领域具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) target segmentation remains challenging due to the small size of objects, appearance variations, cluttered backgrounds, and the scarcity of densely annotated data. These factors hinder the performance and practical deployment of lightweight segmentation models in real-world UAV applications. To address this problem, this paper investigates the use of SAM3 (Segment Anything Model 3) as a pseudo-label generator for training compact segmentation networks. Specifically, two supervision paradigms are explored: (i) direct pseudo-supervision using unaltered SAM3-generated masks, and (ii) a refinement strategy that re-applies SAM3 to localized image patches for improved mask quality. Based on these paradigms, a two-stage SAM3-guided pseudo-label generation framework is proposed. In the first stage, SAM3 generates coarse masks for initial object localization. The localized regions are subsequently cropped into patches and processed by SAM3 again to generate fine masks with accurate object boundaries and discard false positives. The resulting coarse and fine masks are then used as pseudo-labels to optimize a lightweight network, termed IPS-Seg, which consists of three components: an IdentityFormer backbone for feature extraction, an Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling module for multi-scale context aggregation, and a PixelShuffle-based decoder for spatial resolution recovery. Extensive experiments under multiple supervision settings demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. The results show that IPS-Seg achieves a favorable trade-off between segmentation accuracy and computational efficiency while benefiting consistently from the proposed pseudo-label generation strategy. These findings highlight the potential of large-scale foundation models as annotation sources for training compact task-specific segmentation networks in low-label vision domains.

</details>


### [157] [GeoSAM-Lite: A Lightweight Foundation Model for Onboard Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03760)
*Yongcong Wang,Jie Zhang,Rui Jiang,Xubing Yang,Ting Yun,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoSAM-Lite是一种轻量级、无需提示的遥感图像分割框架，通过Geo-Init领域感知预训练和FFL特征融合层，在保持竞争精度的同时减少92.8%参数，适用于资源受限的边缘设备实时分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型（如SAM）在资源受限的地球观测平台上部署面临计算成本过高以及自然图像与遥感图像之间的领域偏移两大挑战，亟需轻量化且高效的遥感分割方案。

Method: (1) Geospatial-Domain Initialization (Geo-Init)：领域感知预训练策略，从专用教师模型中蒸馏地理空间先验知识以弥合领域差距；(2) Feature Fusion Layers (FFL)：重新校准空间特征并恢复高频边界线索，克服轻量级骨干网络的能力瓶颈。

Result: 在代表性数据集上，GeoSAM-Lite实现了与重型RSAM-Seg相当的精度，同时参数量减少92.8%，建立了效率与保真度之间的优越帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: GeoSAM-Lite为边缘设备上的实时分割提供了实用解决方案，有效平衡了计算效率与分割精度。

Abstract: The deployment of large-scale foundation models like Segment Anything Model (SAM) on resource-constrained Earth observation platforms is hindered by prohibitive computational costs and the domain shift between natural and remote sensing imagery. To address these challenges, we propose \textit{Geo}spatial \textit{S}egment \textit{A}nything \textit{M}odel-Lite (GeoSAM-Lite), a lightweight, prompt-free segmentation framework designed for efficient onboard remote sensing segmentation. GeoSAM-Lite incorporates two core innovations: (1) Geospatial-Domain Initialization (Geo-Init), a domain-aware pre-training strategy that distills geospatial priors from a specialized teacher to bridge the domain gap; and (2) Feature Fusion Layers (FFL), which recalibrate spatial features and restore high-frequency boundary cues to overcome the capacity bottlenecks of lightweight backbones. Experiments across representative datasets, with a primary focus on cloud scenarios to evaluate performance under extreme scale variations and complex boundaries, demonstrate that GeoSAM-Lite achieves competitive accuracy while reducing parameters by 92.8\% compared to the heavyweight RSAM-Seg. By establishing a superior Pareto frontier between efficiency and fidelity, GeoSAM-Lite offers a practical solution for real-time segmentation on edge devices.

</details>


### [158] [Sparse-View Surface Reconstruction using Gaussian Splatting through High-Confidence Depth Propagation with Normal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03765)
*Liang Han,Bangcai Wei,Junsheng Zhou,Yu-Shen Liu,Zhizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于法线引导的深度传播方法（DP-GS），用于从稀疏视图中实现高质量3D表面重建，通过将高置信度区域的深度信息扩展到低置信度区域，并结合异常深度边缘感知正则化，解决了高斯离散性导致的深度不连续问题，在DTU和Tanks-and-Temples数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图下的3D表面重建因几何线索有限和高斯的离散性而极具挑战性，现有基于3DGS的方法虽在新视图合成上表现优异，但难以重建高质量的几何表面。

Method: 提出法线引导的深度传播方法，将高置信度区域的深度信息扩展到低置信度区域以约束深度；同时提出异常深度边缘感知正则化，处理高斯离散性导致的深度不连续问题。

Result: 在DTU和Tanks-and-Temples数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在稀疏视图表面重建中优于最先进的现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文所提方法有效解决了稀疏视图下3DGS表面重建的难题，通过深度传播和边缘正则化显著提升了几何表面质量。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction from sparse views is a challenging task in 3D computer vision. Recent studies on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have achieved remarkable results with sparse views in novel view synthesis, yet reconstructing high-quality geometric surfaces from sparse views remains a challenge, due to the limited geometry clues and the discreteness of Gaussians. In this paper, we propose a novel 3DGS-based method for high-fidelity surface reconstruction from sparse views. Our key insight is to introduce a normal-guided depth propagation approach, which can extend depth information from high-confidence regions to constrain the depth in low-confidence areas. Additionally, we propose an abnormal depth edge-aware regularization to address depth discontinuities caused by the discreteness of Gaussians. Extensive experiments on DTU and Tanks-and-Temples datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in sparse view surface reconstruction. Project page: https://hanl2010.github.io/DP-GS.

</details>


### [159] [Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03770)
*Hyunsoo Kim,Jungmyung Wi,Soobin Um,Donghyun Kim,Suhyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MiPO方法，通过少数偏好优化微调扩散模型，增强对数据流形中低密度区域的覆盖，从而提升扩散分类器在零样本分类任务中對少數類別的感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散分类器在零样本分类中表现良好，但其性能严重依赖预训练数据分布：在高密度（多数）区域表现优异，在低密度（少数）区域准确率显著下降。现有少数采样工作聚焦于生成更多少数类图像，但少数采样在生成之外对感知能力的根本性提升尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MiPO（Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization），利用少数偏好奖励对预训练扩散模型进行微调。仅使用任意字幕数据，生成候选样本，奖励那些更好覆盖少数区域的样本，并通过LoRA和Group Relative Policy Optimization优化模型，无需额外图像数据、外部基础模型或外部奖励模型。

Result: MiPO实现了稳定、提示自适应的少数采样，将低密度生成覆盖转化为零样本扩散分类性能的提升，在五个标准数据集上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 扩散分类器的感知存在偏向多数区域的偏差；这种偏差可以通过少数偏好优化得到缓解；MiPO有效提升了扩散分类器在低密度区域的识别能力。

Abstract: Prior studies have demonstrated that diffusion classifiers achieve robust zero-shot classification performance. However, their effectiveness is strongly tied to the pretraining data distribution: they perform well in majority, high-density regions of the data manifold, but are significantly less accurate in minority, low-density regions. Although prior works on minority sampling have focused on generating more minority-like images, what minority sampling fundamentally enables beyond generation remains underexplored. In this paper, we reveal a direct relationship between minority sampling in generation and the perception capability of diffusion classifiers. Specifically, we show that enhancing minority sampling broadens the coverage of underrepresented regions on the data manifold, thereby improving diffusion-based recognition. To exploit this connection, we propose \textit{Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization} (MiPO), which fine-tunes a pretrained diffusion model using minority preference rewards. Using only arbitrary caption data, MiPO generates candidate samples, rewards those that better cover minority regions, and optimizes the model with LoRA and Group Relative Policy Optimization, without additional image data, external foundation models, or external reward models. This enables stable, prompt-adaptive minority sampling and translates low-density generative coverage into improved zero-shot diffusion classification. To sum up, we show that diffusion classifier perception is biased toward majority regions, demonstrate that this bias can be alleviated through minority preference optimization, and evaluate MiPO on five standard datasets.

</details>


### [160] [City-Level 3D Surface Reconstruction with Viewpoint Orientation Partitioning and Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03771)
*Liang Han,Wenyuan Zhang,Junsheng Zhou,Yu-Shen Liu,Zhizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视角方向划分的场景分区方法（VOP-GS），用于高效、准确地重建大规模场景表面。该方法将相似视角的视图分组进行联合深度估计，提升了表面重建精度，并支持多GPU并行计算。同时提出缺失区域检测与修复策略，进一步改善几何质量。在多个大规模数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的大规模场景重建方法虽在新视角合成上表现出色，但在复杂几何、长时间优化和有限内存条件下，难以生成高质量的表面。

Method: 1) 基于视角方向的场景划分方法，将相似朝向的视图分组进行联合深度估计；2) 多GPU并行计算以实现负载均衡；3) 缺失区域检测与修复策略，处理点云稀疏或纹理不足导致的缺失问题。

Result: 在GauU-Scene、MatrixCity和UrbanScene3D数据集上，该方法在大规模场景表面重建任务中优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 基于视角方向的场景划分策略能够有效提升大规模场景表面重建的质量与效率，解决了复杂几何、内存限制和优化时间长等挑战。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D surface reconstruction is a longstanding challenge in computer vision. Although recent large-scale reconstruction methods based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieve impressive novel-view synthesis, producing high-quality surfaces over large scenes remains difficult, due to complex geometry, long optimization, and limited memory. In this paper, we propose a novel yet simple partitioning method to efficiently and faithfully reconstruct large-scale scene surfaces. Our key insight lies in a scene partitioning method based on viewpoint orientation. This partitioning approach ensures that views with similar orientations are jointly involved for more accurate depth estimations, leading to precise surface reconstructions and balanced computation on multiple GPUs in parallel. In addition, we propose a strategy to detect and repair missing regions in the initial point cloud caused by sparse viewpoints or insufficient textures, thereby further improving the geometric quality. Extensive experiments on the GauU-Scene, MatrixCity, and UrbanScene3D datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in surface reconstruction for large-scale scenes. Project page: https://hanl2010.github.io/VOP-GS.

</details>


### [161] [Rethinking Depth Pruning for Vision Transformers: A Heterogeneity-Aware Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03784)
*Zhenfeng Su,Kang Zhao,Han Bao,Tao Yuan,Zhongzhe Hu,Xianzhi Yu,Wenxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HetDPT，一种感知层间异质性的ViT深度剪枝方法，解决了现有深度剪枝因忽视层间差异而导致精度恢复困难的问题。实验表明，HetDPT在DeiT-B上实现1.58倍加速且保持精度，与宽度剪枝结合的HetDPT+将极端剪枝加速比从4.24倍提升至5.19倍，刷新了SOTA记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT剪枝研究多集中于宽度剪枝，而深度剪枝尽管能带来更高加速比，却因忽视不同层之间的异质性导致精度恢复困难，限制了联合剪枝方法的加速效果。

Method: 提出HetDPT，一种感知层间异质性的深度剪枝方法，通过考虑各层差异来避免维度不匹配问题，从而实现有效的深度剪枝。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-100、COCO和ADE20K上验证：DeiT-B加速1.58倍且保持精度，DeiT-S加速1.39倍且几乎无精度损失；与宽度剪枝结合的HetDPT+在Isomorphic-Pruning-2.6G配置下将加速比从4.24倍提升至5.19倍，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: HetDPT通过感知层间异质性有效解决了深度剪枝的精度恢复难题，与宽度剪枝联合使用能在极端剪枝场景下取得更优的加速-精度权衡，刷新了ViT剪枝的SOTA记录。

Abstract: While prior studies have successfully compressed vision Transformers (ViTs) through various pruning techniques, most have concentrated on width pruning to achieve significant reductions in model size. Depth pruning, which removes entire layers from a ViT, is notoriously difficult for accuracy recovery despite its potential to deliver higher speedups, limiting the acceleration achieved by existing joint width-and-depth pruning methods. In this work, we reveal that the failure of existing depth pruning methods lies in their neglect of heterogeneity between different layers, and we introduce HetDPT, a heterogeneity-aware depth pruning method that avoids dimension mismatch. Comprehensive experiments on ImageNet-1K, CIFAR-100, COCO, and ADE20K validate our method: HetDPT achieves a 1.58$\times$ speedup for DeiT-B while maintaining accuracy and a 1.39$\times$ speedup for DeiT-S with nearly no accuracy degradation. Furthermore, when combined with width pruning, HetDPT+ sets a new state-of-the-art record in extreme ViT pruning, enhancing the acceleration ratio from 4.24$\times$ to 5.19$\times$ for the Isomorphic-Pruning-2.6G configuration while maintaining near-lossless accuracy; our code is available at https://github.com/Efficient-AI-for-All/HetDPT.

</details>


### [162] [G$^2$TAM: Geometry Grounded Track Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03789)
*Chenming Zhu,Peizhou Cao,Jingli Lin,Wenbo Hu,Yunlong Ran,Jiangmiao Pang,Tai Wang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: G²TAM是一个利用3D重建模型提供的空间一致性，通过跨模态空间编码器将视觉和文本提示整合到共享几何空间，实现端到端空间重建和实例一致掩码预测的统一框架，在跨视角一致性、可提示实例空间跟踪、视频对象分割和空间重建方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分割模型依赖显式对象外观记忆库进行实例跟踪，但在大视角变化和长期遮挡下表现脆弱。为克服这一局限，本文利用现代前馈3D重建模型提供的空间一致性，提出几何引导的跟踪方法。

Method: 提出G²TAM统一框架，核心是跨模态空间编码器，将视觉和文本提示整合到共享几何空间，利用空间对齐的几何表示作为隐式记忆，实现端到端空间重建和实例一致掩码预测。同时构建了InsTrack大规模数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: 在跨视角一致性、可提示实例空间跟踪、视频对象分割和空间重建等任务上均取得了优异表现。

Conclusion: G²TAM为交互式、几何引导的空间推理建立了基础框架，通过几何空间记忆替代传统外观记忆，有效提升了跨视角和长时间遮挡下的实例跟踪鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human spatial understanding arises from jointly perceiving geometry and semantics, enabling consistent object identification and localization across viewpoints and time. Current video segmentation models depend on explicit object appearance memory banks for instance tracking, yet they remain vulnerable to large viewpoint changes and long-term occlusions. Leveraging the spatial consistency afforded by modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction models, we propose the Geometry Grounded Tracking Anything Model (G$^2$TAM), a unified framework for promptable instance tracking in 3D using only unordered RGB images or videos. G$^2$TAM employs spatially aligned geometric representations as implicit memory, ensuring stable instance identity and localization across frames and views. At its core is a cross-modal spatial encoder that integrates visual and textual prompts into a shared geometric space, enabling end-to-end spatial reconstruction and instance-consistent mask prediction. To support training and evaluation, we construct InsTrack, a large-scale dataset with a dedicated validation split for benchmarking. Extensive experiments show that G$^2$TAM delivers strong cross-view consistency, promptable instance spatial tracking, video object segmentation and spatial reconstruction, establishing a foundation for interactive, geometry-grounded spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [163] [InfraNet: Quality-Aware RGB Guidance for Efficient Infrared Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03795)
*Zichao Feng,Haodong Zhu,Jingying Yang,Sheng Xu,Yangyang Ren,Yuguang Yang,Xuhui Liu,Juan Zhang,Tian Wang,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfraNet 是一个以红外为中心、质量感知的多模态目标检测框架，通过非对称架构和 QualGate 模块实现 RGB 质量可控的跨模态训练，支持 RGB-IR 或纯红外部署，在四个基准数据集上展现了高精度和高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法在训练和推理中对 RGB 和红外模态同等对待，当 RGB 模态不可靠或不可用时，系统鲁棒性严重下降。本文旨在解决这一长期挑战。

Method: 提出 InfraNet 框架，采用非对称架构：主红外通路提取多尺度红外特征用于预测，辅助 RGB 通路提供质量可控的监督信号。核心模块 QualGate 学习面向任务的控制信号，抑制不可靠的 RGB 指导并补偿红外特征。基于此设计了 InfraNet-IR（纯红外）和 InfraNet-RGB-IR（RGB-IR）两种变体。

Result: 在 LLVIP、FLIR-Aligned、M^3FD 和 DroneVehicle 四个基准数据集上，在低光照和恶劣天气条件下取得了强或具有竞争力的准确性。纯红外推理保持高效率和计算经济性。

Conclusion: InfraNet 在保持纯红外推理高效性的同时实现了高精度，为多模态目标检测在不利视觉条件下的鲁棒部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Robust object detection under adverse visual conditions remains a long-standing challenge for multi-modal perception systems. Existing fusion-based methods typically require both RGB and infrared (IR) inputs, and treat them equally during both training and inference, which compromises their robustness when the RGB modality becomes unreliable or unavailable. In this case, we propose \textbf{InfraNet}, an IR-centric quality-aware framework that regulates RGB guidance during training and supports flexible RGB--IR or IR-only deployment. InfraNet employs an asymmetric architecture where the primary IR pathway extracts multi-scale infrared features for predictions, while the auxiliary RGB pathway provides reliability-controlled supervisory signals. The core of InfraNet is \textbf{QualGate}, a quality-aware fusion module that learns a task-oriented control signal to suppress unreliable RGB guidance and compensate IR features during cross-modal training. Built upon InfraNet, we design two architectural variants: a lightweight IR-only architecture InfraNet-IR and an RGB--IR architecture InfraNet-RGB-IR. Our method is evaluated through extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets (LLVIP, FLIR-Aligned, M$^3$FD, and DroneVehicle), showing strong or competitive accuracy in challenging low-light and adverse weather conditions. Notably, InfraNet maintains high efficiency in IR-only inference, making it both accurate and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [164] [Probabilistic Robustness in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03797)
*Yi Zhang,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用概率鲁棒性（PR）替代对抗鲁棒性（AR）作为医学图像分类模型可信度的更实用度量，并在MedMNIST v2数据集上系统评估了常见深度学习模型在自然损坏条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从最坏情况角度关注对抗鲁棒性（AR），但这在真实医疗应用中缺乏代表性；因此需要一种更贴近临床实际的模型可信度评估方法。

Method: 构建了面向医学图像分类的自然损坏条件集合，并在MedMNIST v2数据集上对常用深度学习模型进行系统评估，采用概率鲁棒性（PR）作为度量标准。

Result: 提供了基于统计视角的深度学习模型可信度评估结果，展示了模型在自然损坏条件下的概率鲁棒性表现。

Conclusion: 概率鲁棒性（PR）是比对抗鲁棒性（AR）更符合医疗实际场景的可信度度量，有助于推动深度学习模型在医学影像中的可信部署。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) has shown strong performance in medical image classification, but its trustworthy deployment remains challenging in safety-critical clinical settings, where prediction errors under perturbations may lead to severe consequences. Existing studies mainly focus on adversarial robustness (AR) from a worst-case perspective; however, such settings may be less representative of real medical applications. In this work, we investigate probabilistic robustness (PR) as a more practical measure of model trustworthiness. To this end, we construct a set of natural corruption settings for medical image classification and systematically evaluate commonly used DL models on MedMNIST v2 dataset. Our study provides a statistically grounded perspective on assessing the trustworthiness of DL models, thereby supporting their more trustworthy deployment in medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [165] [CineMobile: On-Device Image-to-Video Diffusion for Cinematic Camera Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03803)
*Xuyao Huang,Zelai Deng,Xu Wang,Xizhong Xiao,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineMobile 提出了一种面向移动设备的高效图像转视频生成方法，通过蒸馏剪枝、4步生成器优化和混合后训练量化，将DiT模型压缩至1GB以下，在保持视觉质量的同时实现40倍加速，480p视频在H200上每步仅0.6秒，在移动平台上20秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上图像转视频的需求日益增长（如子弹时间、慢动作等电影级效果），但Diffusion Transformers参数量大、多步迭代去噪导致计算开销巨大，难以在移动端高效运行。

Method: 采用三步优化策略：(1) 蒸馏引导的剪枝，得到紧凑高效模型；(2) 结合扩散蒸馏和强化学习，将压缩模型优化为4步生成器；(3) 混合后训练量化，将模型压缩至1GB以下。

Result: 相比Wan 2.1教师模型，CineMobile实现40倍加速，保持可比视觉质量。生成49帧480p视频，每步去噪延迟在H200 GPU上为0.6秒，在MediaTek Dimensity 8400平台上为20秒，峰值内存1.8GB。

Conclusion: CineMobile证明了通过系统级优化可以在移动平台上实现实用的图像转视频生成，为移动端电影级视频创作铺平了道路。

Abstract: The growing demand for image-to-video creation on mobile devices has increasingly focused on cinematic motion effects like bullet time, dolly zoom, slow motion, etc. While Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) exhibit strong performance in video generation, their large parameter sizes and multi-step iterative denoising processes lead to substantial computational overhead, making efficient generation on mobile devices challenging. We propose CineMobile to bridge the gap.
