With all five pipeline agents sharing a single frozen backbone, MetaSkill-Evolve outperforms no-skill, static-skill, and single-level evolution baselines on three agentic benchmarks (OfficeQA, SealQA, ALFWorld), improving held-out test accuracy over the raw backbone by +23.54, +16.09, and +1.92 points respectively.

</details>


### [414] [Evaluating and Understanding Model Editing for Medical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05310)
*Guli Zhu,Chenwei Wu,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: M3Bench是一个为医学视觉语言模型编辑设计的临床基准，包含16,276个问题，覆盖多种解剖结构、模态和专科，评估模型编辑在图像/文本变化、模态转换、知识组合和时间演进下的可靠性、精确性和泛化能力。实验发现梯度编辑方法迁移强但破坏局部性，内存方法保持局部性但缺乏组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态模型编辑基准聚焦于通用任务，无法反映真实临床领域的需求和变异性。为了推动医学VLM的安全部署后编辑，需要一个临床导向的、系统性的评估基准。

Method: 构建M3Bench基准，包含16,276个问题，涵盖多样化解剖结构、模态和专业领域，支持单次和顺序编辑。评估4种代表性编辑方法在6个医学和通用VLM上的表现，并从潜空间几何角度分析编辑失败的原因。

Result: 没有方法在所有评估标准上表现优异。梯度编辑方法迁移能力强但存在严重的局部性破坏问题；内存方法保持局部性但缺乏组合泛化能力，且对骨干模型的超参数高度敏感。

Conclusion: M3Bench为多模态模型编辑提供了严格的临床压力测试，为更安全的部署后适应提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Model editing promises a fast, targeted way to correct post-deployment mistakes in medical vision-language models (VLMs) without costly retraining. However, existing multimodal model editing benchmarks focus on general-purpose tasks and do not reflect realistic clinical domain requirements and variability. To address this, we introduce M3Bench, a clinically grounded benchmark for multimodal model editing that evaluates whether an edit remains reliable, precise, and generalizable under the challenges of image and text variation, modality and protocol shifts, clinical knowledge composition, and temporal progression. M3Bench contains 16,276 questions spanning diverse anatomy, modalities, and specialties, and supports both single and sequential edits.
  By evaluating 4 representative editors across 6 medical and general VLMs, we find that no method excels across all criteria. Gradient-based editors achieve strong transfer but suffer from catastrophic locality violations, whereas memory-based methods preserve locality but lack compositional generality and exhibit high backbone-dependent hyperparameter sensitivity. We further attribute these failures to the latent space geometry of VLMs and how different editing methods shift its landscape. Overall, M3Bench establishes a rigorous clinical stress test for multimodal model editing and offers actionable guidance for safer post-deployment adaptation. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/BioMed-AI-Lab-U-Michgan/M3Bench .

</details>


### [415] [OptiAgent: End-to-End Optimization Modeling via Multi-Agent Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05346)
*Adriana Laurindo Monteiro,Nayse Fagundes,Gabriel Mattos Langeloh,Gustavo de Oliveira Kanno,Priscila Louise Aguirre,Thiago Costa Rizuti da Rocha,Victor Leme Beltran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OptiAgent是一个多智能体框架，能够将自然语言描述的运筹学问题自动转化为求解器可用的数学公式和可执行代码，采用多循环验证架构和四种专用反馈机制，在LP、MILP和非线性规划任务的4个基准测试中取得3项SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学问题从自然语言描述到数学建模和代码实现的过程高度依赖专家知识，自动化程度低，现有方法在准确性、可审计性和对不同问题类型的泛化能力上存在不足。

Method: 采用多智能体架构，优先进行数学建模步骤，由专用智能体提取决策变量和约束等结构，实现迭代自纠正；引入多循环验证架构，包含四种专用反馈机制，分别针对误解、结构缺陷、数学不一致性、验证失败和代码错误等不同故障模式。

Result: 在LP、MILP和非线性规划任务的4个基准测试中，OptiAgent在3个上取得当前最优（SOTA）性能，在剩余1个数据集上也保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: OptiAgent通过模块化设计不仅提升了求解准确性，还因每个智能体暴露其推理和反馈过程而增强了透明度和可审计性，为运筹学问题的自动化求解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We propose OptiAgent, a multi-agent framework that, given a natural language description of an Operations Research problem, is able to output a solver-ready mathematical formulation as well as executable code. Our architecture prioritizes the mathematical modeling step, where dedicated agents extract structures, such as decision variables and constraints, enabling iterative self-correction. We introduce a novel multi-loop validation architecture with four specialized feedback mechanisms, each targeting a distinct failure mode such as misinterpretation, structural defects, mathematical inconsistencies, validation failures, and code errors. Alongside accuracy, our modular design improves the process of solving optimization problems by improving transparency, as each agent exposes its reasoning and feedback, making the full modeling process auditable. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on 3 out of 4 benchmarks across LP, MILP, and Nonlinear Programming tasks, while remaining highly competitive on the remaining dataset.

</details>


### [416] [Graph Sparse Sampling: Breaking the Curse of the Horizon in Continuous MDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05359)
*Idan Lev-Yehudi,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图稀疏采样（GSS）算法，通过共享采样未来路径而非为每个候选动作单独采样，消除了树形搜索的分支结构，在连续域规划中实现了多项式级别的计算复杂度，并提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续域中的在线规划计算量极大，树形搜索方法（如MCTS）的分支结构导致采样预算随搜索深度呈指数级增长，难以应对连续状态/动作空间中的无限分支层次。

Method: 提出图稀疏采样（GSS）算法，采用无分支的图结构共享采样未来路径，利用启发式方法聚焦计算，并通过平滑备份技术适配全秩或低秩生成模拟器，支持离散或采样连续动作空间。

Result: 在理论层面证明了GSS在适当条件下规划复杂度对搜索深度呈多项式依赖；在连续控制仿真中，GSS在长时域任务上大幅优于树形规划器，或达到近乎最优的性能。

Conclusion: 无分支图规划可作为在线控制中树形搜索的互补设计范式，有效规避指数级复杂度瓶颈。

Abstract: Planning under uncertainty in continuous domains is essential for autonomous systems, yet computationally demanding. Tree-based search methods such as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) remain popular, but their branching structure can require sampling budgets that grow exponentially with lookahead depth in the worst case. From a tree perspective, continuous state or action spaces become especially challenging, since the planner must decide where to search in an infinite branching hierarchy. We propose Graph Sparse Sampling (GSS), an online planning algorithm that shares sampled futures across many candidate decisions, rather than sampling separate successors for each candidate action. This branch-free graph exposes large GPU-friendly batches, while using heuristics to focus computation. We prove finite-sample performance guarantees for GSS covering full-rank or low-rank generative simulators via smoothed backups, and discrete or sampled continuous action spaces. Under suitable overlap, regularity, and action-coverage conditions, these bounds have polynomial dependence on the planning horizon, formalizing when shared futures can avoid the exponential horizon dependence of tree-shaped sparse sampling. We demonstrate continuous-control simulations where GSS substantially outperforms tree-based planners on long horizons or achieves near-optimal performance, supporting no-branching graph planning as a complementary design principle for online control.

</details>


### [417] [SovereignPA-Bench: Evaluating User-Owned Personal Agents under Evolving Intent, Platform Mediation, and Consent Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05363)
*Dylan Zongmin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SovereignPA-Bench基准，用于评估用户自主个人代理在维护用户主权（隐私、同意、证据、负担、抗操纵）方面的能力，实验表明完整主权脚手架优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人代理基准多关注工具使用、网页导航等任务完成能力，未评估代理是否维护用户主权（如隐私、同意、证据、抗操纵等），缺乏对用户自主任代理的全面评估框架。

Method: 构建SovereignPA-Bench基准，包含120个主权压力场景，区分代理可见的ObservableState与评估者仅见的HiddenLabels，报告任务成功、对齐、隐私、同意、证据、操纵、负担、可审计性等多维指标，评估4个模型系列和8种策略基线，产生3840条冻结提示轨迹并进行盲审。

Result: 完整主权脚手架在主权得分上优于直接、仅记忆、仅同意、仅证据、ReAct/工具使用、安全提示和裁判守卫基线，同时降低隐私泄露、同意违规、过度让步和操纵捕获。人类审计显示隐私和同意的高一致性，操纵判断一致性较低。

Conclusion: 个人代理评估需超越任务完成导向，转向代表性、同意感知、证据基础的行动评估。

Abstract: Personal agents are becoming persistent user-owned intermediaries: they remember preferences, filter platform-mediated information, use tools, and negotiate with services. Existing benchmarks evaluate tool use, web navigation, desktop control, personalization, recommendation, and evolving context, but rarely ask whether an agent preserves user sovereignty: advancing the user's current interests while respecting privacy, consent, evidence, user burden, and resistance to manipulative incentives. We introduce SovereignPA-Bench, an executable benchmark for evaluating user-owned personal agents under evolving intent, platform mediation, privacy boundaries, consent constraints, evidence requirements, and burden tradeoffs. The benchmark separates agent-visible ObservableState from evaluator-only HiddenLabels, reports component metrics for task success, alignment, privacy, consent, evidence, manipulation, burden, and auditability, and preserves paired scenario ordering for model and policy comparisons. We evaluate 120 sovereignty stress scenarios across 4 model families and 8 policy baselines, yielding 3,840 frozen-prompt trajectories with raw prompts, outputs, provider-form responses, parsed actions, recomputable metrics, hard-set analyses, qualitative cases, and a blinded 3-annotator audit over 240 items. Full-sovereign scaffolding improves sovereignty score over direct, memory-only, consent-only, evidence-only, ReAct/tool-use, safety-prompt, and judge-guard baselines while reducing privacy leakage, consent violation, over-concession, and manipulation capture. Human audit shows high agreement on privacy and consent and lower agreement on manipulation, identifying the subjective frontier of platform-persuasion judgments. These results show that personal-agent evaluation must move beyond task completion toward representative, consent-aware, evidence-grounded action.

</details>


### [418] [LLM-as-a-Verifier: A General-Purpose Verification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05391)
*Jacky Kwok,Shulu Li,Pranav Atreya,Yuejiang Liu,Yixing Jiang,Chelsea Finn,Marco Pavone,Ion Stoica,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-a-Verifier，一种无需额外训练的通用验证框架，通过连续概率评分替代离散评分，实现验证在评分粒度、重复评估和标准分解三个维度的扩展，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能，并可应用于任务进度估计和强化学习反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM验证方法通常使用离散评分（如LM judges），难以提供细粒度、可校准的反馈。作者希望将验证本身作为新的可扩展维度，从而在不额外训练的情况下提升LLM在智能体任务中的表现。

Method: LLM-as-a-Verifier通过计算评分token logits分布的期望生成连续分数，替代传统的离散评分。该方法支持三个维度的扩展：(1)评分粒度——细化评分等级以更好区分正负样本；(2)重复评估——多次评估取平均以降低方差；(3)标准分解——将复杂标准拆解为多个子标准分别评估以降低复杂度。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%)、SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%)、RoboRewardBench (87.4%)和MedAgentBench (73.3%)上达到SOTA。同时，该方法可应用于RL算法（SAC和GRPO），提升样本效率。

Conclusion: 验证可以作为LLM扩展的新维度，连续概率评分框架能够有效提升验证准确率，并具有广泛的应用潜力，包括任务进度监控和强化学习信号提供。

Abstract: Scaling pre-training, post-training, and test-time compute have become the central paradigms for improving the capabilities of LLMs. In this work, we identify verification, the ability to determine the correctness of a solution, as a new scaling axis. To unlock this and demonstrate its effectiveness, we introduce LLM-as-a-Verifier, a general-purpose verification framework that provides fine-grained feedback for agentic tasks without requiring additional training. Unlike standard LM judges that prompt LLMs to produce discrete scores for candidate solutions, LLM-as-a-Verifier computes the expectation over the distribution of scoring token logits to generate continuous scores. This probabilistic formulation enables verification to scale along multiple dimensions: (1) score granularity, (2) repeated evaluation, and (3) criteria decomposition. In particular, we show that scaling the scoring granularity leads to better separation between positive and negative solutions, resulting in more calibrated comparisons. Moreover, scaling repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition consistently lead to additional gains in verification accuracy through variance and complexity reduction. We further introduce a cost-efficient ranking algorithm for selecting the best solution among candidates using the verifier's continuous scores. LLM-as-a-Verifier achieves state-of-the-art performance on Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%), SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%), RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and MedAgentBench (73.3%). Beyond verification, the fine-grained signals from LLM-as-a-Verifier can also serve as a proxy for estimating task progress. We build an extension for Claude Code, enabling developers to monitor and improve their own agentic systems. Finally, we show that LLM-as-a-Verifier can provide dense feedback for RL, improving the sample efficiency of SAC and GRPO on robotics and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [419] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话和操作耦合会话分流到不同路径，在升级路径中使用冲突感知通信和写入触发重审机制，在关键的数据库写入前集中推理和安全检查，避免了在所有服务会话上统一施加额外控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理正从对话界面转向实际的操作执行（如退款、取消、改单等），这产生了服务控制问题：既要保持常规服务快速低摩擦，又要防止涉及客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入交互时的操作错误。

Method: 提出难度路由服务控制架构：1）轻量级路由器将常规会话保留在低成本基线路径上，将操作耦合会话路由到升级工作流；2）升级路径采用冲突感知通信和写入触发重审机制，在关键后端写入前集中推理和安全检查；3）避免在所有服务会话上统一施加额外控制。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升了可靠性。路由证据表明更强的控制被定向到冲突请求而非常规请求。对话和工具使用分析显示改进并非来自无差别扩展交互或更宽工具链，而是通过新增的轮次和工具调用支持证据收集、写入分离和写入前重审。

Conclusion: 该架构在零售和航空两个领域的任务上都验证了服务控制逻辑的有效性，表明难度路由的服务控制能够在不增加常规会话负担的前提下，有效处理操作耦合的复杂服务请求。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [420] [CGGS: Consistency-Augmented Geometric Gaussian Splatting for Ego-centric 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03819)
*Zhenyu Sun,Xiaohan Zhang,Qi Liu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: CGGS是一个文本到3D的框架，通过自中心生成器、布局装饰器和几何精炼器三个模块，解决自中心3D场景生成中的视角重叠有限、几何失真和语义对齐问题，在生成连贯准确的文本驱动3D场景方面优于以往方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自中心3D场景生成面临视角重叠有限和个体视角主导场景理解的挑战，导致难以生成视角一致、语义对齐的视觉内容以及构建准确的几何结构。

Method: 提出CGGS框架，包含三个模块：1) 自中心生成器：微调多视图潜在扩散模型，使用一致性增强损失生成与文本描述对齐的高保真2D内容；2) 布局装饰器：利用光流和点跟踪对应关系估计深度，从自中心2D先验生成稠密点云作为粗布局；3) 几何精炼器：通过基于熵的互信息深度损失(MID)结合分层优化方案，增强3D高斯重建的视觉质量和几何结构。

Result: 综合实验表明，CGGS在生成连贯准确的文本驱动3D场景方面优于以往方法。

Conclusion: CGGS通过一致性增强的多视图扩散、光流点跟踪深度估计和互信息深度损失分层优化，有效提升了自中心3D场景的几何准确性和视角一致性。

Abstract: Challenges remain in ego-centric 3D scene generation due to limited view overlap and the dominant influence of individual perspectives on scene interpretation. These factors hinder the creation of viewpoint-consistent and semantically aligned visual content, as well as the construction of accurate geometric structures. In this paper, we propose CGGS, a text-to-3D framework aiming to enhance 3D-content-awareness and address geometric distortions in ego-centric scene generation. Firstly, the Ego-centric Generator is proposed by fine-tuning a Multi-View Latent Diffusion Model with consistency-augmented loss to generate consistent, high-fidelity 2D content aligned with textual descriptions. Then, Layout Decorator leverages optical flow and point-track correspondence to estimate depth, therefore producing dense point clouds as coarse layouts from the ego-centric 2D priors. Building on this initialization, Geometric Refiner is proposed to enhance 3D Gaussian reconstruction via an entropy-based Mutual Information Depth Loss (MID) combined with a hierarchical optimization scheme for improving visual quality and geometric structure. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that \textcolor{softred}{CGGS} outperforms previous methods in generating coherent and accurate text-driven 3D scenes. Project page: https://cggs-26.github.io/cggs26/.

</details>


### [421] [High-Performance Real-Time Implicit Strand-Based Hair Rendering via Software Rasterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04230)
*Lukas Lipp,Adrian Jarabo,Michael Wimmer,Lukas Bode*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的延迟软件光栅化管线，用于基于发丝的实时头发渲染，通过软件光栅化、延迟着色、发丝过滤与重建以及LOD方案，在提升性能与兼容性的同时保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于发丝的方法通常局限于高端硬件且仅用于少数主角角色，而基于头发网格的实现虽高效但存在显著性能瓶颈。本文旨在解决这些限制，提升性能与硬件兼容性。

Method: 采用软件光栅化方法，结合延迟着色与发丝过滤/重建步骤，实现高效远场发丝渲染（甚至每像素单样本）；引入基于视距与屏幕空间覆盖的LOD方案，自适应调整头发表示与着色复杂度。

Result: 在单样本每像素条件下实现了高效的远场发丝渲染，显著降低了计算开销，同时保持了视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 首次在效率、灵活性、可扩展性和广泛硬件兼容性方面实现了上述组合，为实时发丝渲染提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work we propose an efficient deferred software rasterization pipeline for real-time rendering of strand-based hair using hair meshes. Hair plays a crucial role in creating expressive 3D characters, yet strand-based approaches are often restricted to high-end hardware and typically applied to only a small number of hero characters. Hair meshes have proven to be an efficient representation capable of handling a wide variety of groom styles, but existing mesh shader-based implementations still suffer from significant bottlenecks. In this work, we address these limitations with a software rasterization approach that improves performance and compatibility. Our method enables efficient far-field strand-based hair rendering-even at a single sample per pixel-by combining deferred shading with a strand filtering and reconstruction step, while requiring only minimal hardware support. To further enhance scalability, we introduce a level-of-detail (LOD) scheme that adapts hair representation and shading complexity based on viewing distance and screen-space coverage, reducing computational cost further while preserving visual fidelity. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach to achieve this combination of efficiency, flexibility, scalability, and broad hardware compatibility.

</details>


### [422] [How to Build Digital Humans? From Priors to Photorealistic Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04341)
*Wojciech Zielonka,Tobias Kirschstein,Timo Bolkart,Simon Giebenhain,Vanessa Sklyarova,Xiang Deng,Donglai Xiang,Shunsuke Saito,Yebin Liu,Matthias Niessner,Justus Thies*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文综述了可控3D人体化身创建技术，重点介绍人体外观和运动先验学习以及个性化化身生成阶段，对全身、头部及分层表示方法进行了分类总结，并讨论了开放挑战和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D人体化身在虚拟现实、数字娱乐等领域应用广泛，但现有系统复杂度高、方法分散，缺乏系统的综述来帮助研究者理解当前技术全貌、分类体系及未来方向。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对现有3D化身系统按三个阶段（先验学习、个性化创建、动画驱动）进行归纳，重点聚焦前两个阶段；构建了基于身体区域和所用先验的多轴分类法，分别评述全身、头部及分层表示（手部、头发、服装等组件分解）方法。

Result: 给出了当前3D化身表示的完整定义，建立了涵盖全身、头部及分层组件的分类体系，归纳了共同的基础原理，并为新研究者提供了关键参考文献索引。

Conclusion: 当前可控3D人体化身创建已形成较完整的技术框架，但面部细节、服装物理、实时交互等领域仍存在开放挑战，未来需在多模态先验融合与高效个性化方面进一步突破。

Abstract: This state-of-the-art report provides an overview of controllable 3D human avatar creation. We describe current 3D avatar systems, which typically consist of three stages: (i) learning priors of human appearance and motion, (ii) creating a personalized avatar, and (iii) animating the avatar. To limit the scope, we focus on the prior learning and avatar creation stages. We define current avatar representations and introduce a taxonomy that categorizes existing work along multiple axes, including body regions and employed priors. We review methods for full-body and head avatars, as well as layered representations that decompose the body into components such as hands, hair, and garments. Finally, we outline common underlying principles, reference key literature for newcomers, and discuss open challenges and future research directions.

</details>


### [423] [SceneFrom3D: Geometry-Conditioned Outdoor 3D Scene Generation via View Scheduling with Object-Level Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04540)
*Geonung Kim,Jeongeun Park,Nuri Ryu,Di Liu,Sunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: SceneFrom3D提出了一种面向室外场景的几何条件3D场景生成框架，通过自动视图调度（有向生成图）和物体级条件控制（身份图像+几何 adherence 参数），解决了传统三阶段方法中视图调度瓶颈问题，实现了高质量、可控的室外3D场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何条件3D场景生成方法采用"定义视图调度→合成多视图→重建3D"的三阶段设计，但视图调度在室外大规模、非结构化、无界几何场景中成为主要瓶颈，难以获得兼顾充分覆盖与稳定生成的视图。

Method: SceneFrom3D构建有向生成图，节点为锚定视图，边为插值轨迹，自动确定视图合成、视图对插值及生成顺序；同时通过物体级身份图像引导外观、几何 adherence 参数控制区域几何约束，增强可控性。

Result: 实验表明SceneFrom3D在几何条件室外3D场景生成任务上达到最先进水平，生成高质量场景，并实现可控的物体外观与几何 adherence。

Conclusion: SceneFrom3D通过自动视图调度与物体级条件控制，有效解决了室外场景的视图调度瓶颈，实现了高质量、高可控的几何条件3D场景生成。

Abstract: Geometry-conditioned 3D scene generation enables the creation of 3D environments from user-provided geometry, offering direct control over scene structure and object layout. To generate such 3D scenes, current methods commonly adopt a three-stage design that first defines a view schedule, then synthesizes multi-view observations along the scheduled views, and finally reconstructs a 3D representation from the generated images. However, defining the view schedule becomes a major bottleneck for outdoor scenes, where large, unstructured, and unbounded geometry makes it difficult to obtain views that provide sufficient coverage while supporting stable generation. To address this bottleneck, we present SceneFrom3D, a framework that automatically schedules views from outdoor input geometries. SceneFrom3D constructs a directed generation graph whose nodes represent anchor views and whose edges represent interpolation trajectories, defining which views to synthesize, which view pairs to interpolate, and in which order generation should proceed. Beyond automatic view scheduling, SceneFrom3D further improves controllability through object-level conditioning, assigning each object an identity image for appearance guidance and a geometry-adherence parameter for region-wise control over the input geometry. Experiments demonstrate that SceneFrom3D achieves state-of-the-art geometry-conditioned outdoor 3D scene generation, producing high-quality scenes with controllable object appearance and geometry adherence.

</details>


### [424] [StructuredEdit: Constraint-Aware Graphic Design Editing via Differentiable Parameter Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04612)
*Veeramanohar Avudaiappan,Ritwik Murali*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出StructuredEdit，通过可微参数传播（DPP）将设计编辑重构为参数操纵而非像素生成，在结构化设计编辑中达到89%的约束满足率，相比GPT-4V的52%显著提升，并分别降低33%编辑时间和44%修正迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型基于像素操作进行设计编辑，约束满足率仅52%，难以满足专业设计工作流的可靠性要求。

Method: 提出StructuredEdit管线，核心贡献为可微参数传播（DPP）训练方法，通过轻量级可微光栅化器反向传播像素级约束违反信号，将硬设计约束嵌入视觉语言模型微调；并构建混合候选-过滤流水线生成12.5万验证编辑三元组。

Result: 约束满足率89%（vs GPT-4V 52%），匹配元素IoU 0.82，100种最常用字体的Top-1准确率76%；用户研究（N=35）中编辑时间降低33%，修正迭代减少44%。

Conclusion: 将设计编辑从像素生成范式转向参数操纵范式，结合可微约束传播，显著提升了结构化设计编辑的可靠性和效率，为专业设计工作流提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Graphic design editing requires precise manipulation of typography, layout, and visual hierarchy under strict design constraints. Following the introduction of large language models, organizations have increasingly promoted vision-language models to enhance productivity. However, current models operate on pixels and achieve only 52% constraint satisfaction on structured design edits, thereby limiting their reliability for professional workflows. We present StructuredEdit, a pipeline that reframes design editing as parameter manipulation rather than pixel generation. Our core technical contribution is Differentiable Parameter Propagation (DPP), a training method that embeds hard design constraints into vision-language model fine-tuning by backpropagating pixel-level constraint violations through a lightweight differentiable rasterizer. A hybrid candidate-and-filter pipeline produces 125k validated edit triplets. The resulting system reaches 89% constraint satisfaction versus 52% for GPT-4V, 0.82 matched-element Intersection over Union, and 76% top-1 font accuracy over the 100 most-frequent design typefaces. In a user study (N=35), editing time drops 33% and correction iterations drop 44% relative to a GPT-4V baseline.

</details>


### [425] [Physics-Based Simulation of Contact-Induced Facial Wrinkling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04768)
*Juan Sebastian Montes Maestre,Ladislav Kavan,Edmond Boyer,Ryan Goldade,Stelian Coros,Bernhard Thomaszewski*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个有限元框架，用于模拟面部皮肤在接触过程中由机械不稳定性诱导产生的随时间变化的褶皱模式。该框架将皮肤建模为粘弹性材料，采用高阶棱柱实体壳单元解析厚度方向应力，并引入基于连续介质的皮肤韧带模型来模拟异质性附着，从而实现对褶皱幅度、波长和空间分布的控制。实验表明，该方法能生成时间连贯且视觉真实的瞬态接触褶皱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部皮肤的动态模拟因几何、材料和解剖结构的复杂性而极具挑战性。现有方法难以同时处理皮肤的非线性分层特性、空间异质性附着以及接触事件中因压缩和剪切导致的机械不稳定性，从而无法生成精细且真实的褶皱模式。

Method: 1) 将皮肤建模为具有时间依赖松弛特性的粘弹性材料；2) 使用高阶棱柱实体壳单元解析厚度方向应力和高频变形模态；3) 提出基于连续介质的皮肤韧带模型，模拟皮肤与下层组织之间的异质性附着和移动约束，控制褶皱的幅度、波长和空间分布。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实视频片段上的对比实验表明，所提出的皮肤模型能够在瞬态接触过程中生成时间连贯且视觉真实的褶皱模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限元框架能有效模拟面部皮肤接触诱导的褶皱动力学，通过粘弹性本构和皮肤韧带约束实现了对褶皱时空特征的精确控制，为计算机图形学和生物力学模拟提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Facial skin dynamics are inherently challenging to simulate due to a combination of geometric, material, and anatomical complexities. Human skin is a nonlinear layered material with spatially heterogeneous attachments to the underlying tissues. During contact events, localized compression and shear induce mechanical instabilities, leading to fine-scale wrinkling patterns governed by a delicate interplay of geometry, boundary conditions, and through-the-thickness stresses. We present a finite element framework to simulate contact-induced wrinkling of facial skin. We model skin as a viscoelastic material with time-dependent relaxation that governs the rate, persistence, and damping of wrinkle formation. We employ high-order prismatic solid-shell elements to resolve through-thickness stresses and high-frequency deformation modes. Central to our approach, we introduce a continuum-based formulation of skin ligaments to model heterogeneous skin attachments and provide anatomically inspired mobility constraints. These skin ligaments control the formation and appearance of facial wrinkles by modulating their amplitude, wavelength, and spatial distribution. We evaluate our method on a set of synthetic examples and compare simulations with real-world footage. These results demonstrate that our skin model produces temporally coherent and visually realistic wrinkle patterns during transient contact.

</details>


### [426] [Glare Mitigation using a Differentiable Unified Glare Rating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04796)
*Linas Beresna,Eugene Fiume*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续、完全可微分的统一眩光评级（UGR）代理方法，通过可微分光学散射通道模拟人眼点扩散函数（PSF）修复蒙特卡洛方差导致的优化不稳定性，并用可调Sigmoid边界替代离散的UGR阶跃函数，实现了从心理物理测量到场景参数的平滑梯度流动，从而在复杂全局光照环境中优化视觉舒适度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CIE统一眩光评级（UGR）依赖于严格的二元阈值，与基于梯度的平滑反向渲染方法本质上不兼容，导致无法将眩光评估直接嵌入可微逆渲染框架中进行物理逆向设计优化。

Method: 1) 构建连续、可微分的UGR代理函数，用可调Sigmoid边界替代离散阶跃函数；2) 引入可微分光学散射通道，模拟人眼点扩散函数（PSF）来修复低采样密度下蒙特卡洛方差导致的评估掩膜断裂；3) 将该可微框架应用于三个辐射度量域：表面微几何粗糙化、边界折射率优化、光源侧发射器gobo掩膜。

Result: 通过将被动感知评估转化为主动损失景观，该框架在三个辐射度量域上系统地降低了眩光，证明了其在复杂全局光照环境中优化视觉舒适度的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种鲁棒的、基于物理的管道，将心理物理眩光评估转化为可微损失函数，使得梯度可以从感知度量反向传播至物理场景参数，为实现基于物理的视觉舒适度优化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Recent research in differentiable light transport extends the utility of computer graphics algorithms beyond traditional image generation, offering powerful tools for physical inverse design. In architectural and automotive applications, visual discomfort from glare is a critical design rating, traditionally quantified by the discrete CIE Unified Glare Rating (UGR). The standard UGR formulation relies on strict binary thresholds, making it fundamentally incompatible with smooth gradient-based inverse rendering. In this paper, we introduce a continuous, fully differentiable proxy for UGR. To resolve the severe optimisation instabilities caused by Monte Carlo variance at low sample densities, we introduce a differentiable optical scattering pass that simulates the Point Spread Function (PSF) of the human eye to heal fractured evaluation masks. We replace the discrete UGR step function with a tunable sigmoid boundary, enabling gradients to flow smoothly from the psychophysical measure back to the physical scene parameters. We deploy this differentiable framework to systematically reduce glare across three radiometric domains: surface-side microgeometry roughening, boundary-side index of refraction (IOR) optimisation, and source-side emitter gobo masking. By transforming a passive perceptual evaluation into an active loss landscape, our framework provides a robust, physics-based pipeline for optimizing visual comfort in complex global illumination environments.

</details>
